  H5: ##### 1.	Saint without Polyeleos on Sunday (under )
  H5: ##### 2.	Saint without Polyeleos on weekdays, except Saturday (under )
  H5: ##### 3.	Saint without Polyeleos on Saturday (under )
  H5: ##### 4.	Saint with Polyeleos on Sunday (under )
  H5: ##### 5.	Saint with Polyeleos on weekdays (under )
  H5: ##### 6.	Saint with All-Night Vigil on Sunday (under )
  H5: ##### 7.	Saint with All-Night Vigil on weekdays (under )
  H5: ##### 1.	Forefeast on Sunday with a Saint without Polyeleos (under )
  H5: ##### 2.	Forefeast on a weekday with a Saint without Polyeleos (under )
  H5: ##### 3.	Feast of the Lord on Sunday and weekdays (under )
  H5: ##### 4.	Feast of the Theotokos on Sunday (under )
  H5: ##### 5.	Feast of the Theotokos on weekdays (under )
  H5: ##### 6.	Afterfeast on Sunday with a Saint without Polyeleos (under )
  H5: ##### 7.	Afterfeast on a weekday with a Saint without Polyeleos (under )
  H5: ##### 8.	Afterfeast on Sunday with a Saint with Polyeleos (under )
  H5: ##### 9.	Afterfeast on a weekday with a Saint with Polyeleos (under )
  H5: ##### 10.	Afterfeast on Sunday with a Saint with All-Night Vigil[^44] (under )
  H5: ##### 11.	Afterfeast on a weekday with a Saint with All-Night Vigil (under )
  H5: ##### 12.	Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord and the Theotokos on Sunday (under )
  H5: ##### 13.	Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord and the Theotokos on a weekday (under )
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma 1 *“Blessed is the man”* - whole, according to the Typikon. By our custom today, only certain selected verses of it are sung, which are contained in the church hymnal. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, that is, of the *Octoechos* of the resurrection of the current tone - 7 and of the *Menaion* (or *Anthologion*) to the saint, which is in the order of the *Menology* - 3. Also Glory, Both now: theotokion 1st of the current tone (Dogmatikon) from the *Octoechos*, or, as usual, from the *Heirmologion*, according to the musical notations. If there be a Doxastikon to the saint, then Glory: Doxastikon to the saint, also Both now: 1st theotokion of the current tone[^46]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Great Sunday Prokimenon *“The Lord is King”*[^48]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha - stichera of the resurrection of the current tone. If there be a Doxastikon to the saint, then Glory: to the saint, Both now: theotokion of the Sunday Aposticha of the *Octoechos* in the tone of the Doxastikon[^49]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparia: first of the resurrection of the current tone, also Glory: of the saint, Both now: theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion (New Greek Constantinopolitan Typikon, p. 77). (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Theotokos of the *Octoechos* of the current tone, obligatorily as in the *Octoechos*, that is, every ode with the Heirmos on 5, without Katavasias and without Litanies, only after the 6th Ode - "Lord, have mercy" (3), also *“Glory, Both now”* and the Sessional Hymn (sometimes the Kontakion)[^52]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Troparia after *“It is truly meet”* will not be said, unless one Hypakoe of the current tone from the *Horologion* (among the Sunday troparia)[^53]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Dismissal: from *“Glory to Thee, O Christ God”* with the response *“Glory, Both now”*, "Lord, have mercy" (3), "Lord, bless"[^54]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: troparion of the resurrection of the current tone twice, Glory: of the saint, Both now: theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata: two appointed, after each of which - the Small Litany with the Sunday Sessional Hymns according to the *Octoechos*[^56]. Also Kathisma 17 ("The Blameless"), or 19 (Polyeleos): 17th - on Sundays between the Apodosis of the Holy Cross (September 21) and the Forefeast of the Nativity of Christ (December 20); 19th - on Sundays between the Apodosis of Theophany (January 14) and the 1st Sunday of Great Lent[^57]. After Kathisma 17 or 19 - immediately "The Angelic Host", Small Litany and Hypakoe of the current tone. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual (Gradual) of the current tone from the *Octoechos*, or from the *Heirmologion*. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Prokimenon of the resurrection of the current tone from the *Octoechos*, or from the *Heirmologion*. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Gospel - one of the eleven Sunday Gospels. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 6.	Stichera of the resurrection "Jesus is risen". (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 7.	Canons 4 on 14: three of the *Octoechos* of the current tone on 10 and one of the *Menaion* to the saint on 4. Of the three canons of the *Octoechos*: the first, of the resurrection, is taken with the Heirmos on 4, the second, of the Cross-Resurrection - without the Heirmos on 3 and the third, of the Theotokos - also without the Heirmos on 3. At the end - the fourth from the *Menaion* of the saint, without the Heirmos on 4. For the Heirmoi of only the first canon are taken, and the others are not taken. They are given only to serve as a model for the singing of the troparia that follow them, which were once all sung according to their pattern. After every Ode - the designated Katavasia (Heirmos of the first canon of the feast[^58], for usually there are two canons), the scheme of which is given at the end of this Typikon. After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion and Ikos of the 6th Ode. Kontakion and Ikos of the saint, if there be (for the *Menaion* does not always give a Kontakion-Ikos), and Sessional Hymn of the saint. After the 6th Ode - Kontakion and Ikos of the resurrection, at the 8th Ode, before its Katavasia - the refrain "We praise", we sing to the melody of the refrain of the Idiomela hymns, in the tone of the Katavasia. At the 9th Ode we sing "My soul magnifies" with *“More honorable”*. After the 9th Ode - "Holy is the Lord our God" (3), in the tone of the current Prokimenon and the Exaposteilarion (Hymn of Light), one of the eleven, corresponding to the number of the Sunday Gospel, also Glory: Exaposteilarion of the saint, if there be, Both now: theotokion of the Sunday Exaposteilarion[^59]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 8.	Praises (Lauds) stichera of the Sunday *Octoechos* of the current tone - 8, also Glory: Gospel sticheron, one of the eleven, corresponding to the Sunday Gospel of Matins, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou" from the *Horologion*, according to the rite of Matins. Refrains for the aforementioned 8 stichera are not all selected from the Psalms of Praise, but only 6, because for the 7th and 8th separate refrains are sung: for the 7th - "Arise, O Lord", and for the 8th - "I will confess". (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 9.	After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion *“Today salvation has come to the world”*, when the tone is odd, that is 1st, 3rd, 6th, 9th, or *“Having risen from the tomb”*, when the tone is even, that is 2nd, 4th, 6th, 8th. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparia: Sunday - at all hours, also "Glory" at the 3rd and 9th - also to the saint, at the 6th - to the temple[^64] of the church. Kontakia: at the 1st and 9th - Sunday, at the 3rd - to the saint, at the 6th - to the temple. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Dismissal: from *“Glory to Thee, O Christ God”*, with the commemoration of the Sunday and the saint, the first, and the second, if there be. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Antiphons: the first two - Sunday, from the very day of the Resurrection, 3rd - the usual "Come", with the Sunday refrain (in monasteries - the Beatitudes)[^66]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Sunday Troparion, Glory: Sunday Kontakion, Both now: Theotokion Kontakion, one of the feasts of the Theotokos, in the tone of the Doxastikon of the Sunday Kontakion, or the Kontakion of the church, if it is of the Theotokos, if - not, then the theotokion from the Sunday ones, in the tone of the Sunday Kontakion[^67], or the general one - "O Protection of Christians"[^68]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon, Alleluia - Sunday, of the current tone. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Apostle, Gospel - sequential Sunday. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Communion Hymn - Sunday "Praise the Lord"[^69]. (under ## 2.1 Saint without Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma current[^70]. On Sunday evening, in view of the All-Night Vigil which sometimes occurs, the Kathisma is not taken; on the eves of feasts for which the Typikon prescribes an All-Night Vigil, the current Kathisma is not taken. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 6 stichera[^130]: of the *Octoechos* of the current tone - 3[^71] and from the *Menaion* of the saint - 3. Glory, Both now: theotokion (or Stavrotheotokion) which follows the stichera of the saint. If in the *Menaion* there is a Doxastikon to the saint, then we sing Glory: Doxastikon, Both now: theotokion of the *Octoechos* in the tone of the Doxastikon and of the day of the week, from *“Lord, I have cried”*. Such a Doxastikon, according to the rubrics of the Greek Typikon, is sung by both choirs together ενοὐμένων των δυο χορών. (Καιφ. ε.)[^72]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day of the week, which is in the order of Vespers in the *Horologion*. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha: all stichera from the *Octoechos*, with the two refrains given in the *Horologion* in the order of Vespers; only Glory: to the saint, if there be in the *Menaion*, then Both now: theotokion of the Aposticha of the *Octoechos*, in the tone of the Doxastikon and the day of the week. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Dismissal Theotokion from the *Horologion*, in the tone of the saint's troparion and of the day of the week[^112]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Theotokos of the *Octoechos*, of the current tone, exactly as in the *Octoechos*, that is, every ode with the Heirmos on 5, without Katavasias and without Litanies, only after the 6th Ode - "Lord, have mercy" (3), also Glory, Both now: Sessional Hymn. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Troparia after *“It is truly meet”*: of the temple, if it is of the Lord or of the Theotokos, and also of the day of the week, and the other four that are in the *Horologion* in the order of Small Compline. If the temple is of a saint, then first - of the day[^196], and then - of the temple. On Wednesday and Friday, if the temple is of the Lord or of the Theotokos, there is no need to say that of the temple first, because the Cross Troparion is of the Lord[^74]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: troparion of the saint twice, Glory, Both now: Dismissal Theotokion in the tone of the saint's troparion[^121][^119] and of the day of the week[^75]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata current, usually two, after each of which there is a small litany and a Sessional Hymn from the *Octoechos*[^77]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 on 14: first of the *Octoechos* with the Heirmos on 6, second of the *Octoechos* without the Heirmos, on 4, and of the saint of the *Menaion* without the Heirmos, on 4. For the Heirmoi are taken only of the first canon. Katavasia only after the 3rd, 6th, 8th and 9th Ode - Heirmos of the last canon, that is, of the saint[^78]. After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn of the saint, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the saint, if he has one, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion from the *Octoechos*, also Glory: Exaposteilarion of the saint, if he has one, Both now: Theotokion of the Exaposteilarion of the saint. If the saint does not have an Exaposteilarion, then Glory, Both now: Theotokion of the Exaposteilarion of the *Octoechos*. Only on Wednesday and Friday, because the Exaposteilarion of the saint does not have a Stavrotheotokion, but only a Theotokion, the Stavrotheotokion of the Exaposteilarion of the *Octoechos* is taken. Also on Thursday, for then the Theotokion of the *Octoechos* is special, relating to St. Nicholas, whose service is leading on this day, the Theotokion of the *Octoechos* is taken, and not of the *Menaion*[^79]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha - all *Octoechos*, only if there is a Doxastikon in the *Menaion* to the saint, then at "Both now" the Theotokion is taken from the Matins Aposticha in the tone of the Doxastikon and of the day of the week. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Dismissal Theotokion in the tone of the saint's troparion and of the day of the week, different from the one that was at Vespers and at *“God is the Lord”*. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Sometimes a saint on 6 has nothing more than a saint on 4, except for six troparia in the odes of the Canon of Matins and six stichera on *“Lord, I have cried”*, if it does not coincide with the service of some feast; for then there will be on *“Lord, I have cried”* 3 stichera to the feast and 3 to the saint (September 9). (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Sometimes he has also on the Aposticha of Matins, after the stichera of the *Octoechos*, one sticheron with its own refrain, and in addition to that a Doxastikon (November 17). (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Sometimes he has Praises stichera, numbering 4, and afterwards - the Small Doxology and the Aposticha of the *Octoechos*, in its own place (September 6 and October 7). However, in this case, the decision remains with the Rector: to take the Great Doxology without the Aposticha, or the Small with the Aposticha[^86]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Sometimes he has not only Praises stichera and the Great Doxology, but also at *“Lord, I have cried”* the Dogmatikon in the tone of the Doxastikon and a whole proper Aposticha, and for the troparia - the Sunday Theotokion at Vespers and at Matins. At Matins - proper Sessional Hymns and at the Canon - the designated Katavasia after every Ode (October 23 and July 25). If there be a feast of the Lord or of the Theotokos on 6, then the Canon is only of this service, without the *Octoechos* (July 2 and August 16 and 31). (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion of the day of the week, also Glory: its Kontakion, Both now: Kontakion of the temple, if of the Lord or of the Theotokos. If the temple is of a saint - then the general Theotokion "O Protection" ("O Undisputed Intercessor")[^89], or one of the feasts of the Theotokos, of the tone[^90]. On Wednesday and Friday we do not select Theotokia, but replace the Troparion of the day with the Kontakion of the day with the refrain *“Glory, Both now”*, because this Kontakion is of the Lord. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia, Communion Hymn - of the day (at the end of the Apostle or our *Sluzhebnik* (lit. "Service Book")). (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Apostle and Gospel - of the day of the week, sequential after the Descent of the Holy Spirit[^91]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	If the service is taken only to the saint, then the Theotokion or Kontakion of the temple is taken one of the aforementioned. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	If the service is only for some specific need, then the Theotokion will be for the need, and where there is none, then the Troparion of the service for need is replaced by its Kontakion (with the refrain *“Glory, Both now”*); it does not have to be of the Lord. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	If the service is taken both of the day and to the saint[^115], then the service of the day precedes the saint and there will be the troparion of the day, then - of the saint and Kontakion of the day, also Glory: Kontakion of the saint, Both now: Theotokion or Kontakion of the temple, one of the aforementioned. On Wednesday and Friday: the Kontakion of the saint is replaced by the Kontakion of the day. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	If the service of the day or of the saint and for a need is taken, then the service of the day or of the saint precedes the service for need and there will be the troparion of the day or of the saint and for need, also Kontakion of the day or of the saint, Glory: Kontakion for need, Both now: Theotokion for need, if there be one. On Wednesday and Friday: the Kontakion for need is replaced by the Kontakion of the day; but when the need does not have a Theotokion, then there will be Glory: Kontakion of the day, Both now: Kontakion for need. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	The service for need, according to the decree of the Lviv Synod[^92], must always be taken alone, except for the Apostle and Gospel of the day, which may be combined with the service for the dead and precede its Apostle and Gospel. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 6.	In cathedral churches, where the Divine Service is celebrated properly and daily, one should not leave out the Apostle and Gospel of the day and here it is better to read the Apostle-Gospel of the day, rather than of the saint, for the Apostle-Gospels of the saints are repeated more often during the year, but of the day - not[^93]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 7.	If there be several Prokimena, Alleluias, Apostle-Gospels and Communion Hymns, then one must know how to take them. Know first, that in church typikons more than two are not given, with the exception of Apostle-Gospels, of which only in Slavonic typikons three are given, for the Slavs are richer in supplications than the Greeks. For where three readings coincide, the Greek typikons do not give the sequential one, but the Slavonic ones take it under the refrain of the first reading, as if one with it[^94]. When this is impossible, on the occasion of some great feast, then they order to take it on the day before under the refrain with the sequential one[^95]. However, this rule has been preserved among us perhaps only in cathedral churches, for in others usually only one Apostle-Gospel is taken, as also one service either of the day, or of the saint, or one for need. If, therefore, there be two Prokimena and two Alleluias, then both are taken under one heading, that is both Prokimena - before the refrain of the first reading, both Alleluias - after the completion of the second reading. However, the first Prokimenon is taken twice, namely: once - before and once - after the verse, the second - only once and without a verse, for in this case the Typikon does not give a verse. Similarly also "Alleluia": both of its verses are taken only of the first service, and of the second - only one first verse[^96]. The Heading, that is from whom: from Paul, from Matthew or from another, is taken only of the first reading, and the others usually are not taken, although it would be better to announce the headings of other readings as well, for it is not indifferent to the listeners to know the reading of which holy author is being proclaimed; and for this reason only would the rubric make certain sense, which orders to take the sequential reading under the refrain, for taking one reading under the refrain of another means merging it with the previous one because its heading is not announced. If the heading of the following third reading, which is not taken under the refrain, is not named, then it should have been said that this third reading is also taken under the refrain. "Let us be attentive" is announced only at the beginning of the first reading. If there be two Communion Hymns, then "Alleluia" is repeated only upon the completion of the second[^97]. (under ## 2.2 Saint without Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma current (18th), which is not taken only when it precedes an All-Night Vigil. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 6 stichera: to the saint of the *Menaion* - 3 and of the *Octoechos* - 3, given in the first place[^98], Glory, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone, which is being taken leave of (Dogmatikon of the past Sunday)[^99]. After the Doxastikon, if there be one in the *Menaion* to the saint, the same 1st Theotokion of the tone is taken with the refrain "Both now". (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion*. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha - 3 Martyria stichera of the *Octoechos* with two usual refrains of the *Horologion*. However, because between the stichera given on the Aposticha the 2nd and 3rd (in Tone 4 and 6 - only the 3rd) are for the dead, which today we limit to Souls Saturdays, here the stichera for the dead are not taken, but instead of them, the Martyria stichera are taken from the last ones, which are on *“Lord, I have cried”*[^100]. If there be a Doxastikon in the *Menaion* to the saint, then the Theotokion is taken from the Vespers Aposticha of the *Octoechos*, in its tone and of the day of the week. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones, in the tone of the saint's troparion[^101]. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 6.	Dismissal, by our custom, great, in view of the Apodosis of the tone, with the commemoration of the service of the day and the saint[^102]. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Theotokos from the *Octoechos* of the current tone[^134]. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	After *“It is truly meet”*: Troparion of the day, that is "Apostles and Prophets", "Martyrs", Glory: Kontakion "With the saints give rest", Both now: Kontakion "To Thee, O Lord, the Planter of creation"[^103]. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”* - Troparion of the saint twice, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current, after which - Small Litany and sessional hymns, of which two are Martyria and two for the dead, which, according to some editions do not have refrains, according to some they do, that is 1st Martyron - "Wondrous is God", 2nd - "Many tribulations", 1st for the dead - "Blessed are they", 2nd - "Their souls". (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 on 14: of the temple, if of the Lord or of the Theotokos, with the Heirmos on 6, Heirmoi twice, Troparia on 4[^105], also to the saint, in the order of the *Menaion*, on 4, and the first of the *Octoechos*, that is the Martyria[^106] on 4. (For the second canon of the *Octoechos* is not taken, but is taken only on Souls Saturdays)[^107]. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Small Doxology, because a saint on 6 never has the Great. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the Martyria - 3, given on the "Praises"[^110] with two usual refrains of the *Horologion*[^111]. Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Praises (for the one given on the Aposticha is for the dead). If there be a Doxastikon to the saint, then the Theotokion is selected to it from the Praises of the *Octoechos*, in the tone of the Doxastikon. The Aposticha stichera, since they are for the dead, are taken only on Souls Saturdays, for then the Praises stichera are also taken, and the stichera on the Aposticha. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Dismissal Theotokion in the tone of the saint's troparion and of the day of the week[^112]. (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.3 Saint without Polyeleos on a Saturday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma *“Blessed is the man”* (according to the previous typikon - whole). (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, that is: of the *Octoechos* of the Sunday of the current tone - 4, also to the saint - 6, Glory: to the saint, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone (Dogmatikon). (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Great Prokimenon of the day *“The Lord is King”* and 3 readings to the saint. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the Sunday *Octoechos*, Glory: to the saint, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday Aposticha in the tone of the Doxastikon[^118]. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: to the saint, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the saint's troparion[^119]. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion of the tone twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the saint's troparion[^121]. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata - two first current with their usual Sunday Sessional Hymns of the *Octoechos*, also Polyeleos with Magnifications of the saint and "The Angelic Host", Small Litany with the exclamation "For blessed is" and Hypakoe of the tone, also 1st and 2nd Sessional Hymn of the saint once without Theotokia, Glory: 3rd Sessional Hymn of the saint, Both now: his Theotokion[^122]. (Mark in chapter 65 prescribes 1st Theotokion of the tone). (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual of the current tone and everything else up to the canon - of the Sunday. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons 3 on 14. If a saint on 8 is celebrated, then there will be the Sunday Canon of the *Octoechos* with the Heirmos on 4 and of the Theotokos on 2[^123], and of the saint on 8[^124]; if he is celebrated on 6, then there will be the Sunday Canon with the Heirmos on 4 and of the Theotokos on 4, and to the saint on 6[^125]. The Cross-Resurrection canon in both cases is not taken[^126]. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Praises stichera - 8: of the Sunday *Octoechos* of the current tone - 4 and of the saint with "Glory" - 4, with two of his refrains[^128], given on the Vespers Aposticha. Glory: Gospel sticheron sequential, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou". (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of the two usual ones, that is in an odd tone - *“Today salvation has come to the world”*; in an even tone - *“Having risen from the tomb”*. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Sunday Troparion and of the saint; also Sunday Kontakion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: Theotokion or one of the feasts of the Theotokos in the tone of the Doxastikon, or another, as we gave in the first case on page 53. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia, Gospel and Communion Hymn - first of the Sunday, and after - to the saint. (under ## 2.4 Saint with Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma *“Blessed is the man”* (according to the Typikon - only the 1st antiphon). (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 6 stichera[^130] or 8[^131], all to the saint. Glory: to the saint, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone, in view of its Apodosis. Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion* and three readings to the saint. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha whole to the saint, with his refrains. To the Doxastikon of the saint the Theotokion is selected from the Sunday Aposticha in the tone of the Doxastikon[^132]. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion[^133]. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Troparion to the saint twice, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones, in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata - two current with Sessional Hymns of the saint, also Kathisma 19, that is Polyeleos with Magnifications and the Post-Polyeleos Sessional Hymn of the saint. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual of Tone 4, first antiphon. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Prokimenon, Gospel and Sticheron to the saint. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Canons two: 1st - of the Theotokos, one of the two usual ones ("Having traversed the moisture", Tone 2 of Sunday Matins, or "Having crossed the water", Tone 8 of Compline on Tuesday) with the Heirmos on 6, then to the saint one or two canons on 8[^136]. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 6.	Praises stichera - 4 to the saint, Glory: his Doxastikon, Both now: Theotokion, which is after the Doxastikon of the saint. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 7.	After the Great Doxology with the Trisagion - Troparion to the saint, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.5 Saint with Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“Lord, I have cried”*: stichera - 4, all Sunday of the current tone of the *Octoechos*; also Glory: sticheron to the saint, Both now: Theotokion of the tone of Small Vespers[^138] that is 1st Theotokion of the tone[^139], which is called Dogmatikon[^140]. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On the Aposticha stichera - 4, that is: one Sunday, which is in the first place in the *Octoechos* of the current tone and 3 to the saint with two of his refrains, which are on the Aposticha of Great Vespers[^141], also Glory: to the saint from his Small Vespers, Both now: Theotokion from the Aposticha of Small Vespers[^142]. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: to the saint, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Dismissal with the commemoration of the Sunday and of the saint. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma 1 *“Blessed is the man”* (according to the Typikon - whole, according to our custom today - only certain selected verses). (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* stichera - 10, that is 4 from the *Octoechos* and 6 to the saint, Glory: to the saint, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion* and 3 readings to the saint. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Lytia: according to the Slavonic Typikon, first sticheron - of the temple (which today we usually do not take), also stichera to the saint. Glory: to the saint, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday Aposticha of Great Vespers, in the tone of the Doxastikon[^153][^144][^143]. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the Sunday tone, Glory: to the saint, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday Aposticha of Great Vespers, in the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion "O Virgin Theotokos" twice and to the saint once[^227][^145]. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two current kathismata with their Sunday Sessional Hymns of the *Octoechos*; also Kathisma 19 (Polyeleos) with Magnifications and "The Angelic Host", and Hypakoe of the tone, and immediately all three Sessional Hymns of the saint, that is 1st and 2nd without Theotokion, then Glory: 2nd Sessional Hymn, which is called the Polyeleos one, Both now: its Theotokion[^147]. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual, Prokimenon, Gospel and Sticheron, all only Resurrection Sunday. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons three or four on 14, namely: Sunday with Heirmos on 4 and of the Theotokos on 2, and to the saint on 8, there will be two or only one canon; for usually the saint has two canons[^148]. (under ## 2.6 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* and on the Aposticha all stichera - to the saint, on the Aposticha there will be separate refrains to the saint. Only regarding the Theotokia on *“Lord, I have cried”* and on the Aposticha let it be known, that they sometimes will be special[^150], sometimes only general, designated only by the first words; sometimes even without indication of the first words. And in this last case, we think that they should be selected from the *Octoechos* on Daily Vespers in the tone of the Doxastikon and of the day of the week, which are given at the end of every volume of the *Menaion* and of the Lviv *Anthologion*; if - not, then in the tone of one of the three Doxastika, given at the end of the Pochaiv *Anthologion*[^151]. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the saint's troparion. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”*: stichera of the saint on 8, Glory: His Doxastikon, Both now: 1st Theotokion (Dogmatikon), in the tone of the Doxastikon; on Friday for Saturday - Dogmatikon of the current tone[^152], in view of the Apodosis of the tone. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion* and 3 readings to the saint. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Lytia: first sticheron - of the temple, which today we usually do not take, all the rest - to the saint and Glory: to the saint, Both now: Theotokion or special, if there be, or general from the Aposticha of Sunday Great Vespers, in the tone of the Doxastikon[^153]. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	On the Aposticha all stichera - to the saint, with his two refrains; Theotokion to the Doxastikon, if there be no special one, is taken from the Sunday Aposticha in the tone of the Doxastikon[^154]. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion of the saint twice and "O Virgin Theotokos" once. (under ## 2.7 Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, that is: of the *Octoechos* of the current tone - 4, of the Forefeast - 3 and to the saint - 3. Glory: of the Forefeast, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. If there be a Doxastikon to the saint, then having discarded the Doxastikon of the Forefeast, Glory: Doxastikon to the saint is taken, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone[^158]. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Aposticha: stichera Sunday, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. If there be a Doxastikon to the saint, then after it at "Both now" is taken of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 4 on 14, that is: first - Sunday of the *Octoechos* with Heirmos on 4 and second - of the *Octoechos* of the Theotokos on 2, also of the Forefeast on 4 and of the saint on 4[^162]. (If the saint - on 6, then here is taken only on 4). Katavasia after every Ode - current. After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefeast and of the saint, Glory: Sessional Hymn of the saint, Both now: of the Forefeast. After the 6th Ode - Kontakion-Ikos Sunday. After the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" thrice and Sunday Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the Forefeast[^163]. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Praises stichera - 8, that is: of the *Octoechos* - 4 and to the saint - 4, if he has, with two separate refrains of his; if the saint does not have, then of the feast on 4 (from the Aposticha with their refrains); also Glory: sticheron of the feast, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou"[^164]. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of two usual ones. (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.8 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Beginning from the very Forefeast until the Apodosis of the feast, the service of the *Octoechos*, with the exception of Sunday, is not taken and, instead of it, the service of the feast is taken in the first place, likewise also on Saturday; it is combined together with the service of the current saint, except for the Apodosis, for then the service of the saint is transferred to another day. Only at Compline is the Canon of the Theotokos of the *Octoechos* taken. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	The Divine Service is celebrated in a lesser form, that is Vespers and Matins - daily. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	The sequence of stichera and troparia does not end with usual *Octoechos* Theotokia, but festal ones; with the exception of Friday evening for Saturday, in view of the Apodosis of the tone, usually the 1st Theotokion of the tone is taken. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma current. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 6 stichera, that is: of the Forefeast - 3 and to the saint - 3, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast, or Glory: to the saint, if he has, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the Forefeast; if Glory be to the saint, then Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Troparion to the saint, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Dismissal medium with the commemoration of the service of the day and of the saint[^169]. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Troparion of the Forefeast twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with Sessional Hymns of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 on 12, that is: of the Forefeast with Heirmos on 8 and of the saint on 4[^172]. Katavasia after the 3rd, 6th, 8th and 9th Ode - Heirmos of the last canon, that is of the saint[^173]. After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional Hymn of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefeast, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the saint, if the saint has one, if - not, then only of the Forefeast (December 29, January 2), Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast[^174]. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the Forefeast, Glory: to the saint, if he has, Both now: of the Forefeast, if - not, then Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.9 Forefeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathismata usually none; only on Saturday for Sunday - Kathisma *“Blessed is the man”*, according to the Typikon - all three antiphons; on Sunday for Monday - only the first antiphon. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”*: stichera of the feast on 8. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day; only, if the feast falls on a Saturday, then, instead of the daily Prokimenon, the Great Prokimenon of the feast is taken, given on Vespers of the next day. In this case, Vespers of the next day will be on Saturday for Sunday, which has the Great Prokimenon *“The Lord is King”*, which does not yield to the Prokimenon of the feast. This Vespers, therefore, is already not of the feast itself, but of the Afterfeast and therefore it cannot exclude the Sunday service, which occupies the first place. In order not to discard the Prokimenon of the feast, it is taken before that on Great Vespers of the feast itself, namely on Friday[^181]. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Three readings of the feast. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	On the Lytia and on the Aposticha - stichera of the feast. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the Blessing of Loaves - Troparion of the feast thrice. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Troparion of the feast twice, Glory, Both now: once more. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with Sessional Hymns of the feast, also Kathisma 19 (Polyeleos) and Magnifications, and the Post-Polyeleos Sessional Hymn of the feast. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual of Tone 4, 1st antiphon. Prokimenon, Gospel and Sticheron of the feast. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons one or two. Troparia on 12, Heirmoi - twice. Katavasia current after every Ode. After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the feast. At the 9th Ode *“More honorable”* is not sung, but festal refrains to the Heirmoi and Troparia of the canon. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	Praises stichera of the feast - 4, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the feast. (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.10 Feast of the Lord on a Sunday and Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“Lord, I have cried”*: 4 Sunday stichera of the current tone, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On the Aposticha: one Sunday sticheron of the current tone, which is in the first place, also 3 stichera of the feast from the Aposticha of Great Vespers with their refrains[^184]; Glory, Both now: of the feast, from Small Vespers[^185]. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the end: Sunday Troparion; Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Dismissal small with the commemoration Sunday and festal. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma 1 (*“Blessed is the man”*), according to the Typikon - all three antiphons, by our custom today - only selected verses. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, namely: of the Sunday *Octoechos* - 4, which is in the first place, also of the feast - 6, Glory, Both now: of the feast[^186]. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion*. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Three readings of the feast. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	On the Lytia all stichera - of the feast. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 6.	On the Aposticha: stichera Sunday, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 7.	At the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion of the feast thrice. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with Sunday Sessional Hymns and with their Theotokia, also Polyeleos with Magnifications, "The Angelic Host" and Hypakoe of the tone. All three Sessional Hymns of the feast, that is: 1st Sessional Hymn once, 2nd Sessional Hymn once, then Glory, Both now: 3rd Sessional Hymn, if it is double, then there will be Glory: first, Both now: second[^187]. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual of the current tone, afterwards - "Resurrection of Christ"; everything else up to the canon - to the feast[^188]. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons 3 on 14, that is: Sunday from the *Octoechos*, with Heirmos on 4, Theotokion from the *Octoechos*, without Heirmos, on 2 and both canons of the feast, without Heirmos, on 8; if to the saint there be only one, then one is taken on 8[^189]. Katavasia current after every Ode. After the 3rd Ode - Sunday Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional Hymn of the feast (on the Dormition of the Most Holy Theotokos, instead of the Sessional Hymn, - Hypakoe of the feast); after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the feast, after the 9th - Sunday Exaposteilarion sequential, one of 11, then Glory, Both now: of the feast. At the 9th Ode - *“More honorable”*, festal refrains, according to the Typikon, are not taken; however, we think, that it would be better to coordinate the festal refrains with the troparion of the canon of the feast, than with the general refrain "Most Holy Theotokos, save us"[^190]. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Praises stichera - 8, that is: 4 Sunday and 4 of the feast with two festal refrains; Glory: of the feast, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou". (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Great Doxology with the Trisagion - Sunday Troparion, one of two usual ones. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 8.	Glory, Both now: Gospel Sticheron. (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.11 Feast of the Theotokos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion and Kontakion of the feast itself. (under ## 2.12 Feast of the Theotokos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Dismissal is commemorated also the service of the feast. (under ## 2.12 Feast of the Theotokos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Liturgy, in the Afterfeast of the Lord, first two antiphons - of the feast[^194], third - usual with the refrain of the feast, also and the usual Entrance "Come, let us worship" with the same refrain of the feast[^195]. (under ## 2.12 Feast of the Theotokos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn on Sunday - of the day (that is of the Sunday) and of the feast, on weekdays - only of the feast; Apostle and Gospel - of the day[^196]. When on Sunday the service of a saint is also taken, then the Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the feast are discarded, and only of the Sunday and of the saint are taken. On weekdays, if the service of a saint is combined with the festal service, then all this is taken in the first place - to the feast, afterwards - to the saint. But because among us to a saint without Polyeleos at the Liturgy usually nothing is taken, all this is taken only to the feast. (under ## 2.12 Feast of the Theotokos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	Instead of *“It is truly meet”* - Heirmos of the 9th Ode of the canon of the feast. However, we will present separate and full models. (under ## 2.12 Feast of the Theotokos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma usual *“Blessed is the man”* (according to the Typikon - all three antiphons). (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, that is: 4 from the *Octoechos* of the current tone, 3 of the Afterfeast and 3 to the saint, Glory: of the Afterfeast, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone. If there be a Doxastikon to the saint, then, having discarded the Doxastikon of the feast, the Doxastikon to Him is taken, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone. (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Aposticha: Sunday stichera of the *Octoechos*, Glory, Both now: of the Afterfeast. (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the Afterfeast. (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: of the feast; everything else up to the Sunday Canon - as usual. (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 4 on 14: Sunday *Octoechos* with Heirmos on 4, of the Theotokos *Octoechos* on 2, of the feast on 4 and of the saint on 4. (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Praises stichera - 8, that is: 4 Sunday from the *Octoechos* and 4 of the feast with two of its refrains. Glory: Gospel Sticheron, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou". (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of two usual ones. (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.13 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma current. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 6 stichera, that is: 3 of the feast and 3 to the saint, Glory, Both now: to the feast, or Glory: to the saint, if he has, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the feast, Glory: to the saint, if there be, Both now: of the feast, if - not, then Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Troparion to the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Dismissal medium with the commemoration of the feast and of the saint, without commemoration of the service of the weekday, because it is not taken. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Troparion of the feast twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with Sessional Hymns of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 on 12, that is: of the feast with Heirmos on 8 and of the saint on 4. Katavasia after the 3rd, 6th, 8th and 9th Ode - Heirmos of the last canon, that is of the saint. After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional Hymn of the saint; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the feast; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the saint twice, Glory, Both now: of the feast[^205]. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha: stichera of the feast, Glory: to the saint, if there be, Both now: of the feast, if - not, then Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Antiphons in the Afterfeast of the Lord we take: two first - from the feast itself, third - general with the refrain of the feast, also and "Come, let us worship"; in the Afterfeast of the Theotokos antiphons so, as also on all days. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Entrance - Troparion, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the feast, Trisagion. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn - of the feast, Apostle and Gospel - of the day. Instead of *“It is truly meet”* - Heirmos of the 9th Ode of the Canon of the feast with its refrain. Dismissal with the commemoration of the feast and of the saint[^209]. (under ## 2.14 Afterfeast with a Saint without a Polyeleos on Weekdays and Saturday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma 1-a *“Blessed is the man”* (according to the Typikon - whole). (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, that is: 3 Sunday from the *Octoechos*, 3 of the feast and 4 to the saint, Glory: to the saint, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone (Dogmatikon)[^210]. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day *“The Lord is King”* and 3 readings to the saint. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Aposticha: Sunday stichera of the *Octoechos*, Glory: to the saint, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Theotokos from the *Octoechos* of the current tone. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	After *“It is truly meet”* - Kontakion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the feast[^212]. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with their Sunday Sessional Hymns, also Polyeleos with Magnifications and "The Angelic Host". After the Small Litany - Hypakoe of the tone and all three Sessional Hymns of the saint without their Theotokia, that is 1st and 2nd, also Glory: 3rd, Both now: of the feast. Everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 on 14, that is Sunday with Heirmos on 4, of the feast on 4 and to the saint on 6; Katavasia designated after every Ode, after the 3rd - Kontakion-Ikos of the feast and of the saint, also Sessional Hymn of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast, after the 6th - Sunday Kontakion-Ikos; after the 9th - Sunday Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the feast. On the 9th - *“More honorable”* (with the exception of Sundays in the middle of the Feast of the Resurrection, for then we sing the Heirmos of the Festal Canon)[^213]. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Praises stichera - 8, that is: 4 Sunday and 4 to the saint, with two refrains, given on Vespers, Glory: Gospel Sticheron, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou". (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of two usual ones. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Antiphons in the Afterfeast of the Lord will be: two first - of the feast, third - general with the refrain of the feast, also and "Come, let us worship". (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Entrance - Sunday Troparion, of the feast and of the saint; also Sunday Kontakion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the feast. Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - of Sunday and to the saint[^216]. (under ## 2.15 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma 1-a (*“Blessed is the man”*, according to the Typikon - 1st antiphon). (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 8 stichera[^218], that is: of the feast - 3 and to the saint - 5, Glory: to the saint, Both now: to the feast. On Friday evening - Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone (Dogmatikon). (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion*. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Three readings to the saint. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	Aposticha whole - to the saint with his refrains, only Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the end: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 7.	Dismissal with the commemoration of the feast and of the saint. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Theotokos from the *Octoechos* of the current tone. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	After *“It is truly meet”* - Kontakion of the feast one[^219]. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Troparion of the feast twice, Glory: to the saint, Both now: once more of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with Sessional Hymns of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast. Sometimes these two Sessional Hymns will be only of the feast (January 11 and May 9)[^220]; third Kathisma, Polyeleos with Magnifications, also Sessional Hymn of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Gradual of the 4th tone, 1st antiphon. Everything else up to the canon - to the saint. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons 2 or 3 on 14, that is: of the feast with Heirmos on 6, also two or one canon to the saint on 8[^221]. Katavasia current after every Ode. After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the feast and Sessional Hymn of the saint twice, Glory, Both now: Sessional Hymn of the feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the saint, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the saint twice, Glory, Both now: Exaposteilarion of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	Praises stichera sometimes - 4, all to the saint[^222], sometimes - 6, that is: 3 of the feast and 3 to the saint, Glory: to the saint, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Antiphons in the Afterfeast of the Lord will be: two first - of the feast, third - general with the refrain of the feast, also and "Come". In the Afterfeast of the Theotokos - antiphons daily "It is good". (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Entrance - Troparion of the feast and of the saint, Glory: Kontakion of the saint, Both now: Kontakion of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn - of the feast and to the saint; Apostle and Gospel - of the day and to the saint[^225]. Instead of *“It is truly meet”* - Heirmos of the feast. (under ## 2.16 Afterfeast with a Saint with a Polyeleos on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.17 Afterfeast with a Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma, stichera on *“Lord, I have cried”* and on the Aposticha so, as also with a Polyeleos saint in the Afterfeast on a Sunday, as here above. (under ## 2.17 Afterfeast with a Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On the Lytia: stichera to the saint, Glory, Both now: to the feast. (under ## 2.17 Afterfeast with a Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion "O Virgin Theotokos" twice and to the saint once[^227]. (under ## 2.17 Afterfeast with a Saint with an All-Night Vigil on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.18 Afterfeast with a Saint with an All-Night Vigil on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	The service of the current saint, which falls on the Apodosis of the feast, is transferred to the immediately preceding[^228] or following day[^229], sometimes also to another day, if it will not be occupied by two saints or one with a Polyeleos; sometimes it is limited to the canon at Compline after the Canon of the Theotokos. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	The service of the feast on the Apodosis, from Vespers to the Canon of Matins has only the daily form, from the Canon to the end of Matins - the great form. If the Apodosis falls on a Sunday, then Vespers and Matins are great, in view of the Sunday service. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma *“Blessed is the man”*. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 10 stichera, that is: 4 Sunday from the *Octoechos* and 6 of the feast, Glory: of the feast, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone (Dogmatikon). (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day *“The Lord is King”*. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Sunday, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Sunday Troparion of the tone, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 or 4 on 14, that is: first - from the *Octoechos* Sunday with Heirmos on 4, second - from the *Octoechos* of the Theotokos on 2 and of the feast - one or two on 8. Katavasia current after every Ode. After the 3rd - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional Hymn of the feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos Sunday[^232]; at the 9th - *“More honorable”* with refrains of the feast to the troparion of the canon of the feast; at the 9th - Sunday Exaposteilarion current; Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Praises stichera - 8, that is: 4 Sunday of the *Octoechos* and 4 of the feast with "Glory"[^233] and with two refrains of the feast; Glory: Gospel Sticheron, Both now: "Most Blessed Art Thou". (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of two usual ones. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	First two Antiphons, if the feast is of the Lord - of the feast, third - usual with the refrain of the feast; also and "Come, let us worship". If the feast is of the Theotokos, antiphons - Sunday, as here usual, on all Sundays; sometimes "It is good" with the Sunday refrain. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Entrance - Sunday Troparion and of the feast, Glory: Sunday Kontakion, Both now: of the feast[^235]. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the Sunday and of the feast. Apostle and Gospel on the Apodosis of the Lord - only Sunday[^236], on the Apodosis of the Theotokos - first of the Sunday, and then - of the feast[^237]. (under ## 2.19 Apodosis of a Feast on a Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma current. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	On *“Lord, I have cried”* - 6 stichera: all - to the feast; Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	No Entrance, nor readings; Prokimenon of the day from the *Horologion*. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha whole - of the feast, from Great Vespers. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the feast one. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 6.	Dismissal medium with the commemoration of the feast and of the saint of the day[^240]. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Theotokos - current of the *Octoechos*. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	After *“It is truly meet”* - Kontakion of the feast one. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	On *“God is the Lord”*: Troparion of the feast twice, Glory, Both now: again once. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata two current with Sessional Hymns of the feast and immediately Psalm 50 and Canon. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canon and everything else until the end of Matins - exactly so, as on the feast itself. Therefore at the 9th Ode there will be no *“More honorable”*, but refrains of the feast. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 1.	Two first Antiphons on the Apodosis of the Lord - of the feast, third - usual with the refrain of the feast, also and "Come, let us worship" (on the Apodosis of the Theotokos antiphons - daily) (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Entrance - Troparion, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the feast and Trisagion. Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn - of the feast. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)
  H5: ##### 3.	Apostle and Gospel on the Apodosis of a feast of the Lord - only of the day, on the Apodosis of a feast of the Theotokos - first of the day, and then - of the feast. (under ## 2.20 Apodosis of a Feast on Weekdays)

  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.1.4 14 September: Universal Exaltation of the Precious Cross)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.1.5 Saturday after the Exaltation)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.1.6 Sunday after the Exaltation)
  H5: ##### 1.	Prokimenon and Alleluia of the Feast. (under ### 3.1.6 Sunday after the Exaltation)
  H5: ##### 2.	Apostle-Gospel of the Sunday after the Exaltation. (under ### 3.1.6 Sunday after the Exaltation)
  H5: ##### 3.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Feast[^379][^359][^338]. (under ### 3.1.6 Sunday after the Exaltation)
  H5: ##### 1.	Service of this day - Saint without Polyeleos on 6. However, at Matins, from the canon to the end, - of Great [Doxology] type as to a Saint with Polyeleos, although the canons of the Octoechos do not yield, but precede the canon of the Saint. (under ### 3.1.8 23 September: Conception of St. John the Baptist)
  H5: ##### 2.	At Vespers, after the stichera at "Lord, I have cried", has 1st Theotokion (Dogmatikon) according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (On Friday evening - of the tone that is being given up); at the Aposticha: separate stichera, after the Troparion at Vespers and at Matins - Theotokion from the Resurrectional ones. At Matins, before the canon, - separate sessional hymns. At Compline, after "It is truly meet", - Kontakion to the Saint. (under ### 3.1.8 23 September: Conception of St. John the Baptist)
  H5: ##### 1.	If this feast falls on a weekday, then at the decision of the Lviv Synod, it must be transferred to Sunday; but it should be transferred to the following one, and not to the previous one, so as not to coincide with the Service of the Theologian (26 September), which is also transferred to Sunday. (under ### 3.2.1 1 October: Protection of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	This service is celebrated according to the general rule of great feasts of the Theotokos with All-Night Vigil, however, without Afterfeast and without Apodosis. It is celebrated only for one day, except in the arch-cathedral church of St. George in Lviv, and also where there is a church of the Protection: there they celebrate it with Afterfeast until the eighth day, on which they also give it up, in view of the plenary indulgence granted by the Holy See, which can be obtained on any of the eight days of the feast. (under ### 3.2.1 1 October: Protection of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	The Service of the Saint that falls on the Sunday of the feast is transferred to another day, at the decision of the Superior; for thus the rule of the Menaion leaves to the decision to combine on 1 October the service of Sts. Ananias and Roman with the service of the Protection, however, with us the service of these saints, in this case, is transferred to another day. (under ### 3.2.1 1 October: Protection of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	If 11 October falls on a Sunday, then the Service of the Holy Fathers is sung on the same Sunday; if - on another day, then it is sung on the nearest Sunday, whether previous or following. On the previous, if 11 October falls on Wednesday, Tuesday or Monday; on the following, if it falls on Thursday, Friday or Saturday. The Service of the Saint that falls on the same Sunday must be transferred to another day, at the decision of the Ecclesiarch, as the Menaion gives. (under ### 3.2.2 11 October: Sunday of the Holy Fathers)
  H5: ##### 2.	The Rule of the Service of the Holy Fathers is the same as for a Saint with Polyeleos. However, at Matins, from the beginning to the canon, everything only Resurrectional is taken, as usual; to the Fathers - only the Troparion at "God is the Lord", after the Resurrectional one. After the Exaposteilarion of the Fathers the Theotokion of the Resurrectional Exaposteilarion is not taken, for the Theotokion of the Fathers is separate. At Glory of the Praises there will be not the Gospel Stichera, but of the Holy Fathers; and the Gospel one is taken at the dismissal of Matins. (under ### 3.2.2 11 October: Sunday of the Holy Fathers)
  H5: ##### 3.	According to this rule the Service of the Holy Fathers is taken also on 16 July. (under ### 3.2.2 11 October: Sunday of the Holy Fathers)
