  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.2.3 26 October: Holy Great-Martyr Demetrius the Myrrh-streamer)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.2.3 26 October: Holy Great-Martyr Demetrius the Myrrh-streamer)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.2.3 26 October: Holy Great-Martyr Demetrius the Myrrh-streamer)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.2.3 26 October: Holy Great-Martyr Demetrius the Myrrh-streamer)
  H5: ##### 1.	The fast of this Forty Days the Lviv Synod limits only to three days of each week, that is Monday, Wednesday and Friday, permitting even on those days dairy, and on other days - to eat meat, but on the condition of the 50th Psalm for the clergy, and 5 "Our Father" and 5 "Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos"  for the faithful, before dinner and before supper. Our custom [is] - to adhere to the fast on the Eve of the Nativity of Christ and Theophany, even if it falls on one of the meat days; we eat nothing with dairy, or very little, until evening. Simple people abstain also from dairy and fast until evening. (under ### 3.3.2 15 November: Beginning of the Forty Days before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	Regarding church services: in the first 35 days of this Forty Days the church rule prescribes a Lenten type of service with prostrations, as in Great Lent, however, only on Mondays, Wednesdays and Fridays. On other days - of the usual type. In view of the Forefeast of the Nativity of Christ, which begins on 20 December, for the last five days of the Forty Days this rule does not give a Lenten service. But today with us and with the Greeks the Lenten service is limited only to Great Lent. (under ### 3.3.2 15 November: Beginning of the Forty Days before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 1. ON WEEKDAYS (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma "Blessed is the man", according to the rule - 1st antiphon. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: to the Saint [female] - 3 and to the Saint [male] - 3, Glory: to the Saint [female], Both now: to the Saint [female]. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Prokimenon - 3 readings to the Saint [female]. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Feast [of the Entrance] with the Doxastikon of the Saint [female]. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparion of the Saint [female], Glory: of the Saint [male], Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast once and of the Saint [female] once, Glory: of the Saint [male], Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 7.	Sessional hymns: all three - of the Saint [female] with Glory, Both now: of the Feast. Polyeleos and everything else up to the canon - only of the Saint [female]. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 3 making 14: of the Feast with irmos on 6, of the Saint [female] on 4 and of the Saint [male] on 4; sequential Katavasia. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast and Kontakion of the Saint [male], also Sessional hymn of the Saint [female], Glory: of the Saint [male], Both now: of the Feast, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Saint [female], after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Saint [female], Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Saint [female]; Glory: of the Saint [female], Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Saint [female], Glory: of the Saint [male], Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 2. ON SUNDAY (under ### 3.3.3 24 November: Holy Great-Martyr Catherine and Holy Great-Martyr Mercurius)
  H5: ##### 1. APODOSIS OF THE ENTRY ON WEEKDAYS (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At the 9th ode we do not sing "More honorable", but the refrains of the feast, however, only to the troparia of the canon of the feast; and to the troparia of the canons of the saints we sing the usual refrains[^283]. (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: of the feast - 3 and to St. Peter - 3, Glory: to Peter, Both now: of the feast. (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of Clement, Glory: of Peter, Both now: of the feast[^284]. (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. APODOSIS OF THE ENTRY ON SUNDAY (under ### 3.3.4 25 November: Apodosis of the Entry of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.1 9 December: Immaculate Conception of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.1 9 December: Immaculate Conception of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 1. ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 to the Conception and 4 of the Forefathers, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Prokimenon there are no readings. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Aposticha: Resurrectional stichera, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 7.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual, only after the Hypakoe of the tone there will be the Sessional hymn of the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, Conception on 4 and Forefathers on 6. Katavasia "Christ is born". After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos to the Conception and Hypakoe to the Forefathers, Glory, Both now: Sessional hymn to the Conception; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefathers; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Forefathers, Both now: to the Conception. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Forefathers, Glory: to the Forefathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 11.	After the Dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 12.	Troparia: Resurrectional, Conception and Forefathers, Glory: Kontakion to the Forefathers, Both now: to the Conception. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 13.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel during the Afterfeast - only to the Forefathers, and on the Apodosis of the Feast - both to the Feast, and to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H5: ##### 2. ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers (under ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 to the Forefathers and 3 to the Martyrs, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the Prokimenon - three readings to the Martyrs. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the concluding Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Martyrs, Both now: to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 5.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Martyrs, Both now: to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Polyeleos with "Great Doxology" ("Velychanniamy") and " angelic host" - Hypakoe of the tone and all three Sessional hymns to the Martyrs. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 7.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of the Martyrs on 4 and of the Forefathers on 6. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn to the Martyrs, Glory, Both now: Hypakoe to the Forefathers; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefathers; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Martyrs, Both now: Theotokion of the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 8.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 3 Resurrectional, 2 of the Forefathers and 3 to the Martyrs, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 9.	Everything else, as usual on Sunday. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 11.	Resurrectional Troparion, to the Forefathers and to the Martyrs; Glory: Kontakion to the Martyrs, Both now: to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 12.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - first to the Forefathers, also to the Martyrs. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 13.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and to the Martyrs. (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H5: ##### 3. ### 3.4.2 Sunday of the Holy Forefathers (under ### 3.4.3 13 December: Memory of the Holy Martyrs Eustratius and others)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 to the Forefathers, 2 to Daniel and 2 to the Youths; Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 3.	There are no readings. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: to the Prophet. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to Daniel with the Youths, Both now: to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Youths, Both now: to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 7.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual, only after the Resurrectional Hypakoe - Sessional hymn of the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of Daniel on 3, of the Youths on 3 and of the Forefathers on 4. Sequential Katavasia. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Prophet, Glory: Sessional hymn of the Youths, Both now: Hypakoe to the Forefathers; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefathers; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Prophet, Both now: to the Forefathers "Great Sun". (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Forefathers with their two refrains, Glory: of the Forefathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 11.	After the Dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 12.	Resurrectional Troparion and to the Youths, Glory: to Daniel, Both now: to the Forefathers. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 13.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - only to the Forefathers (according to the ancient rule also to the saints, but now it is proper to take only for Saints with Polyeleos). (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H4: #### RULE AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the Entrance outside the Forefeast - Troparion of the day "Apostles, Prophets"; Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the day "To Thee, O Lord, Planter of creation" ("Yako nachatky"). In the middle of the Forefeast - Troparion of the Forefeast, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Forefeast or Kontakion and Troparion of the Eve, if it falls on the Eve. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the day (Saturday); Apostle-Gospel of the Saturday before the Nativity of Christ (to the Galatians, zaspiv 205, from Luke 72), also on the Eve of the Nativity of Christ, if it falls on a Saturday[^297]. (under ### 3.4.4 17 December: Memory of the Prophet Daniel and the Youths)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1. SUNDAY OF THE HOLY FATHERS (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 Resurrectional of the Octoechos and 4 to the Fathers; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	Three readings to the Fathers. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: Theotokion from the Resurrectional Aposticha according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Troparion of the Fathers. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: Theotokion according to the tone of the Troparion of the Fathers. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual, only after the Polyeleos with "Angelic host", after the Resurrectional Hypakoe, there will be the Sessional hymn to the Fathers with their Theotokion[^298]. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 4: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, Cross-Resurrectional on 2, Theotokos on 2 and Fathers on 6. Sequential Katavasia "Christ is born". After the 3rd ode - Hypakoe to the Fathers; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos to the Fathers; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: their Theotokion. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Fathers with their two refrains; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	Everything else, as usual on Sunday. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 11.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2. SUNDAY OF THE HOLY FATHERS (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 Resurrectional, 3 of the Forefeast and 3 of the Fathers; Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	Three readings to the Fathers. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional as usual, with "The Blameless" ("Neporochnymy"). (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, Forefeast on 4 and Fathers on 6. (After the irmos of each ode of the Canon of the Fathers the first two troparia - Resurrectional, which are not taken here, but are taken only when the Sunday of the Holy Fathers falls on the very Eve, 24 December, for then the Resurrectional Canon of the Octoechos is not taken, but instead of it, these two Resurrectional troparia of the Canon of the Fathers). Sequential Katavasia "Christ is born". After the 3rd ode - Hypakoe to the Fathers; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos to the Fathers; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Fathers with their two refrains; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	Everything else, as usual on Sunday, only after the Dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 11.	Resurrectional Troparion, to the Fathers and of the Forefeast; Glory: Kontakion to the Fathers, Both now: Kontakion of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 12.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - only to the Fathers. Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and to the Fathers[^301]. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3. SUNDAY OF THE HOLY FATHERS (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 to the Fathers and 4 - of the Forefeast; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	Three readings to the Fathers. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Forefeast, only Glory: to the Fathers. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast (Eve). (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord" - Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	After all sequential kathismata and after "The Blameless" with "Angelic host" Sessional hymns of the Fathers are taken; everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 2 making 14: to the Fathers on 8 and of the Forefeast on 6. Sequential Katavasia "Christ is born". After the 3rd ode - Hypakoe to the Fathers; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos to the Fathers; after the 9th (after "Holy is the Lord our God") - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 to the Fathers and 3 of the Forefeast; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	Everything else - Resurrectional, as usual. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 11.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.5 Sunday of the Holy Fathers before the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.4.6 20-24 December: Forefeast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.6 20-24 December: Forefeast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.6 20-24 December: Forefeast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Lviv Synod orders pastors to celebrate these Hours together with the Typika in church, in violet vestments, in view of the fast[^308], in bright vestments it is celebrated before Theophany only when the Hours from Saturday or Sunday are transferred back, to Friday, for then there is no fast, but it will be a general dispensation. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	Now we sing all the Hours adding "The Typika" (Typika); and therefore we begin the Hours 3rd, 6th and 9th from "O come, let us worship", and we make the dismissal only at the end of "The Typika". (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	These Hours are celebrated in the morning, earlier or later, at the decision of the Superior; according to the custom of the arch-cathedral church - at 9 o'clock before noon[^309], so that there would be a certain distance between the Hours and the Liturgy, which according to the custom of the same church begins at 11 o'clock. If the Hours begin earlier, then the Liturgy can also begin earlier, but since it is celebrated with Vespers, it should begin around 10 o'clock, as the Lviv Synod decreed about Vespers with the Presanctified[^310]. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	The composition and form of the Great Hours is the same as of the usual Hours, with the exception of three troparia and three readings, given after the Theotokion "What shall we call Thee" at the 1st Hour and after the corresponding ones at the other Hours. Regarding the essence, the Royal Hours differ from the usual ones also by the tripalm, which at the Royal Hours is special, appropriate to the given feast[^311]. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	In the temple, before the Holy Doors, opposite the people, two analogia are placed, on the first of which the Gospel is placed; the second, slightly lower, - opposite the Holy Doors, from which the Paremia and Apostle are to be read. Where there is no analogion for the Gospel, it lies on the Holy Table and is brought out by the Priest through the Holy Doors only for reading, and is supported by servers[^312]. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	The Royal Hours are celebrated with the Holy Doors closed, which are opened only for the reading of the Gospel. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	Candles on the altar are lit only for the reading of the Gospel. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the concluding prayer of the 9th Hour the Priest, having bowed before the Holy Doors, enters the sanctuary and sits in his place. (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	The choirs read: Psalm 102 "Bless the Lord, O my soul" and Psalm 145 "Praise the Lord, O my soul", "Glory, Both now", "Only-begotten Son". (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	The Beatitudes: "In Thy kingdom... great in heaven", "Glory, Both now". (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	The Priest goes out before the Holy Doors in an epitrachelion, reads the prayer "O All-Holy Trinity" (if there be Liturgy, then the Choir immediately: "It is truly meet" and the rest; if not, then before "It is truly meet" the Choir sings: "Blessed be the name of the Lord" (3) and Psalm "I will bless the Lord"[^418], and the Priest then waits before the Holy Doors until the end). (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	Choir: "It is truly meet" with "More honorable than the Cherubim". (under ### 3.4.7 24 December: Eve of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	Its Vespers on the Eve is combined with the Service of Basil, if the Eve falls on one of the first five weekdays, starting from Monday. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	On this feast two Vespers are not celebrated: one Small at its proper time, and the second Great at the All-Night Vigil with Matins, as on other feasts, but one, Great, either with the Service of Basil, if the Eve falls on one of the five fast days of the week, or separately at its proper time, if it falls on Saturday or Sunday. All-Night Vigil is served with Great Compline. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	Vespers of this feast will be either with the Service of Basil, or separately, as on Saturday or on Sunday. It is never served completely, but, if it be with the Service, only until the end of the readings, after which the Small Litany is exclaimed and the exclamation of the Trisagion; and from here, according to the order, the Service of Basil. If it be separately, then only until the end of the litany "Let us complete", that is until the end of its second exclamation ("For Thine is the kingdom"), because the Aposticha and everything else is taken at the All-Night Vigil after the Litya. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	Vespers has not only 3 readings, as on other feasts, but 8 (on Theophany - 11); therefore, after the first three and after the second three readings, it has a separate troparion with its verses. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	Compline is served only until the end of "Glory to God in the highest" and immediately the Litya begins with the blessing of loaves and everything else, as at the All-Night Vigil with Vespers. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	Theophany has the Great Blessing of Water directly after Vespers; also, according to our custom, more solemnly - on the very day of the feast, after the Prayer behind the Ambo. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	On the Eve of the Nativity of Christ and Theophany, when there is to be the Service of Basil with Vespers, the Lviv Synod forbids read services. If several priests wish to serve, it orders them to sing only one service in the cathedral (Title IV, chap. I, part 5). (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	In the arch-cathedral church Vespers with the Service of Basil begins at 10.30. If the Eve falls on Saturday or on Sunday, then the Service of Chrysostom without Vespers will be at 08.30, and Vespers separately begins at 11.00, in order to hasten the Holy Supper, which according to the typikon should begin later, that is at 3 o'clock in the afternoon (current 1 o'clock after noon) on the Eve of the Nativity, and on the ### 3.5.3 Eve of Theophany - in the evening hours, that is at the usual time. All-Night Vigil with Compline - at the usual time[^318]. Now we will give three detailed rules: one - for the case of Vespers with the Service of Basil, one - for the case of Vespers which is celebrated separately, without the Service, and one - for the All-Night Vigil with Great Compline. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 1. VESPERS WITH THE SERVICE OF BASIL (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to "Blessed is the Kingdom" everything is exactly as usual at the Service without Vespers[^319]. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "Blessed is the Kingdom", immediately after "Amen" - "O Heavenly King" and the rest with the Trisagion, as usual, up to "O come, let us worship" inclusive. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the introductory Psalm the Priest, standing near the Holy Table, reads with uncovered head the usual evening prayers and afterwards from there exclaims the litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord". If there be also a Deacon, then he goes out at this litany before the Holy Doors. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	The Kathisma "Blessed is the man" will not be (for it is taken only on Saturday evening for Sunday or on Sunday evening for Monday), nor will there be a Small Litany, but immediately - "Lord, I have cried" with censing, as usual. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 8 stichera, Glory, Both now: Stichera from the Irmologion, if there be none, then according to the tone of the idiomesa. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	Entrance with the Gospel, with the usual prayer of the Entrance. If there be deacons, then the first of them, who is to the right of the Priest, carries the Gospel, and the second goes before him with the censer; and they do in this everything as at the Entrance with the Gospel at the Liturgy. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	Prokimenon of the day from the Horologion, only on Friday evening for Saturday there will be the Great Prokimenon of the Feast, given for the very day of the Feast at Vespers on 26 December. After the singing before the steps of the altar "Let us attend! Peace be unto all!", "Wisdom, let us attend!" the Priest withdraws behind the Holy Table and sits there during the readings in his place. The deacons, if there be [any], remain at the Holy Doors and from here exclaim according to the order "Wisdom" and "Let us attend" with the lifting of the orarion towards the people, as the order of All-Night Vigil prescribes. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	There will be 8 readings, and on Theophany - 11. After the first three and the second three readings of both feasts there will be a separate troparion with its verses; there will be three of them[^321], besides "Glory, Both now", for the whole troparion is sung only twice, namely: once before the first verse and once before the last verse, that is before "Both now", and after the other verses - only up to the half[^322]. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	At the end of the readings - Small Litany (which the Deacon, if there be one, sings before the Holy Doors) with the exclamation of the Trisagion and immediately the Trisagion; and further according to the order of the Liturgy of Basil. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	Apostle on the Nativity of Christ: to the Hebrews, zaspiv 303 "God, who at sundry times", Gospel according to Luke, zaspiv 100 "And it came to pass in those days". On Theophany: to the Corinthians, zaspiv 343 "Am I not an apostle? am I not free?", Gospel according to Luke, zaspiv 9 "Now in the fifteenth year". (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 11.	Instead of "It is truly meet" we sing "In Thee rejoiceth" or, according to the later rule of our service books, the irmos of the 9th ode of the canon given for Compline of the Eve. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 12.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord". (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 13.	After the Prayer behind the Ambo - "Blessed be the name of the Lord" with Psalm "I will bless the Lord". (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 14.	After the Dismissal both choirs gather in the middle of the church and sing together the Troparion, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 2. VESPERS WITHOUT THE SERVICE (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Priest, having put on an epitrachelion and having gone out before the Holy Doors, bows low and begins the usual: "Blessed is our God". Choirs: "Amen", also "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee", "O Heavenly King" and the rest with the Trisagion and "Our Father" up to "O come, let us worship" inclusive. Deacons, if there be [any], go out with the Priest before the Holy Doors and the right one of them exclaims: "Bless, Master", and the Priest: "Blessed is our God"[^323]. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	During the introductory Psalm the Priest, standing before the Holy Doors, says the evening prayers, as usual, and the deacons stand beside him and, at the end of the Psalm, one of them (the right) sings the Great Litany, and the Priest - its exclamation, and then, all bow low and withdraw to the sanctuary. If there be no deacons, then the Priest himself sings the litany. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	At "Lord, I have cried" - usual censing, which the Priest or deacons, if there be [any], perform; stichera on 8, "Glory, Both now" from the Irmologion or according to the tone of the idiomesa. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	At "Glory, Both now" - Entrance with the Gospel, which the first deacon carries, if there be one, and the second precedes him with the censer; the Priest says the usual Prayer of the Entrance, as at Vespers, and blesses the Entrance, as at the Liturgy. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the end of the stichera the Deacon exclaims, if there be one, if - not, then the Priest himself: "Wisdom, attend", lifting up the Gospel, and while "O Gladsome Light" is sung, the Priest or deacon carrying the Gospel goes out, and behind them the Priest, through the middle doors to the sanctuary and, having placed the Gospel on the Holy Table, censes everything as usual and stands before the steps of the Holy Table. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	After the singing of "O Gladsome Light" the Priest exclaims with the deacons, if there be [any]: "Let us attend! Peace be unto all!", "Wisdom", "Let us attend" and, while the Prokimenon of the day is sung, the Priest withdraws behind the Holy Table, and the deacons remain near the Holy Doors, exclaiming "Wisdom" and "Let us attend" to all the readings, of which on the Nativity of Christ there will be 8, and on Theophany - 11, with a separate troparion and its verses after the first three and after the second three readings. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	At the end of the readings the Priest returns before the steps of the Holy Table or the first deacon, if there be one, enters before the Holy Doors, where he sings the Small Litany; the Priest - the exclamation of the Trisagion, but the Trisagion is not sung, but immediately after the exclamation there will be "Let us attend! Peace be unto all!", "Wisdom", "Let us attend" and the Prokimenon of the Feast is sung; after "Wisdom" and "Let us attend" - Apostle to the Galatians, zaspiv 201 "I speak after the manner of men"; Gospel according to Matthew, zaspiv 83 "The kingdom of heaven is like to a grain of mustard seed"[^324]  (on Theophany: to the Corinthians, zaspiv 143 from the half "Brethren, I would not have you ignorant"; Gospel according to Luke, zaspiv 9 "Now in the fifteenth year"). The Gospel is read by the Priest himself[^325]  from the Holy Doors, and the first of the deacons, if there be [any], exclaims: "Wisdom, attend! Let us hear the Holy Gospel"; priest: "The reading from the Holy Gospel according to (Name)"; and the second: "Let us attend". (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	After the singing of the Gospel the Priest, having kissed the beginning of the Gospel reading and having given [it] to the first deacon to kiss, also to the second, approaches the Holy Table, on which he places the Holy Gospel, which remains there. The deacons, having bowed low before the steps of the Holy Table, go out, each through HIS doors, before the Holy Doors and, having bowed low, first the second Deacon exclaims "Let us all say", and also the first after "Vouchsafe" - the litany "Let us complete". If there be no deacons, then the Priest himself sings the litanies from the Holy Table and immediately after the second exclamation of the litany "Let us complete" gives the Great Dismissal of the Feast itself. (On Theophany there is no dismissal here, but it will be after the Blessing of Water). (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Dismissal both choirs gather together in the middle of the church and sing the Troparion, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Feast, and at the end the sacred ministers close the Holy Doors and, having bowed low, withdraw to the sacristy. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	At the usual time of the All-Night Vigil the Priest puts on an epitrachelion, and the deacons, if there be [any], - dalmatics with an orarion and go before the steps of the Holy Table and, having bowed low to the Holy Mysteries, open the Holy Doors and the deacons cense everything exactly as at the All-Night Vigil with Vespers[^326]. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the censing the Priest (after the Deacon, if there be one, exclaims: "Bless, Master") begins: "Blessed is our God"; the choirs sing "Amen", and the sacred ministers, having closed the Holy Doors, stand before the steps of the Holy Table. The choirs continue: "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee", "O Heavenly King" and the rest with the Trisagion and "Our Father" up to "O come, let us worship". Then the sacred ministers, having bowed low, withdraw behind the Holy Table and sit there in their places. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the end of the second tripalm the choirs sing alternatingly "God is with us", that is selected verses of the eighth ode of the prophecies of Isaiah. They sing thus: 1st choir: "God is with us, understand ye nations,... for God is with us", afterwards the second choir sings the same. Also other verses the choirs sing alternatingly, ending each verse with the words "For God is with us". After the end of all verses they sing "God is with us" three times[^327]. The custom of singing the whole first verse after each of the following ones as is usually done here, we do not find in any typikon[^328]. The Lviv Synod also adds that the Priest should not go out before the Holy Doors and sing these verses alternatingly with the choirs, but only there/, where there will be only one choir, for then the Priest replaces one of the choirs. Similarly he should not go out for the moleben verses "O Most Holy Lady", which the choirs say twice, that is once one, and once the other choir, but only, if there be one choir[^329]. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	Instead of the troparia of the day, given after the moleben verses "O Most Holy Lady", the Troparion of the Feast is sung; and instead of the troparia "Have mercy on us, O Lord", given after the Trisagion, which is after the Prayer of Manasses, the Kontakion of the Feast is sung. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	The Priest goes out before the Holy Doors for three prayers: of Basil, of Manasses and the second of Basil. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	After "Glory to God in the highest" the Priest, having put on a phelonion, opens the Holy Doors and there will be Litya and everything else until the end of Matins exactly as at the All-Night Vigil with Vespers. (under ### 3.4.8 25 December: Nativity of our Lord Jesus Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Service of the Hieromartyr is sung at Compline on another day. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	St. Joseph has nothing in the divine service, except one troparion and kontakion, and this only in our horologia and service books. And the Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos also has almost nothing. Vespers of this feast has an entrance not for the sake of the Synaxis, but for the sake of the Great Prokimenon of the Feast. Matins from the beginning to the canon - of small type, from the canon to the end - of great type. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Although the divine service of this day, as also of the following, does not have All-Night Vigil, as also Polyeleos, however, our custom, approved by the Synod of Zamość (Titolo de Jeiuniis et Festis) /On fast and feasts/ and Lviv (Title IV, chap. 6) orders during the first three days of this feast to abstain from heavy work and to participate in the divine service; to these days belong: 25, 26 and 27 December. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	The Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos has three cases; it can fall: 1) on a weekday, except Monday; 2) on Monday; 3) on Sunday. The reason that the Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos has a separate rule for Monday is that in this case the service of the Synaxis is combined with the service of the Sunday after the Nativity, namely of the Holy Kinsmen of God, King David, Joseph the Betrothed and James, the Brother of the Lord. If, therefore, the Synaxis falls on Monday, then the Sunday after the Nativity would fall on 1 January, that is already after the Apodosis of the Feast, which will be 31 December; therefore, according to the typikon, it is transferred back, to 26 December. We will give the rule for all three above-mentioned cases. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	There will be no Kathisma. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 7 stichera of the Feast, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance - Great Prokimenon of the Feast "Who is so great a God as our God". (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion to St. Joseph, Glory, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great Dismissal of the Feast with commemorations of the Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos, of the Betrothed and of the Holy Hieromartyr[^331]. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Midnight Office (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord"[^399]: Troparion of the Nativity twice, Glory: of the Betrothed, Both now: of the Nativity. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two sequential Kathismata with separate Sessional hymns and immediately the 50th Psalm. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Both canons of yesterday and everything else in the canon, as yesterday, only after the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Hypakoe of the Feast; and after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	Yesterday's 4 stichera of the Praises of the Feast, also Glory: of the Synaxis, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	Great Doxology, and after the Trisagion - Troparion of the Betrothed, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great Dismissal with commemoration, as at Vespers. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	The first two Antiphons - of the Feast, the third - usual with the refrain of the Feast; also "O come, let us worship" with the refrain of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the Entrance: Troparion of the Feast and of the Betrothed, Glory: Kontakion of the Betrothed, Both now: Kontakion of the Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos. Trisagion. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Everything else - to the Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos, only instead of "It is truly meet", Heirmos of the Feast; Communion Hymn of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. SYNAXIS OF THE MOST HOLY THEOTOKOS ON MONDAY (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	There will be no Kathisma. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera, namely: to the Feast - 3 and to the Kinsmen of God - 3, Glory: to the Kinsmen of God, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance - Prokimenon of the Feast and "Let us all say". (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Feast, only Doxastikon - to the Kinsmen of God. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion to the Kinsmen of God, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great Dismissal of the Feast with commemoration of the Synaxis and of the Saints. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Midnight Office (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: to the Kinsmen of God, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two sequential Kathismata with Sessional hymns of the Feast, also Psalm 50 and immediately the canon. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: first to the Feast with irmos on 6, irmos - twice, also second - of the Feast on 4 and to the Kinsmen of God on 4. Katavasia of the Feast after each ode. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Synaxis, also Sessional hymn of the Kinsmen of God, Glory, Both now: to the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Kinsmen of God; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", - Heirmos of the canon of the Feast with the refrain of the Feast; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Kinsmen of God, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	Four stichera of the Praises of the Feast, Glory: to the Kinsmen of God, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion to the Kinsmen of God, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great Dismissal of the Feast with commemoration, as at Vespers. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the Entrance - Troparion of the Feast and of the Kinsmen of God, Glory: Kontakion of the Kinsmen of God, Both now: of the Synaxis. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon and Apostle first - to the Synaxis, afterwards - to the Kinsmen of God; Alleluia and Gospel - to the Synaxis and to the Kinsmen of God common (according to Matthew, zaspiv 4). (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Communion Hymn of the Feast and to the Kinsmen of God. (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. SYNAXIS OF THE MOST HOLY THEOTOKOS ON SUNDAY (under ### 3.4.9 26 December: Synaxis of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.10 Saturday after the Nativity)
  H5: ##### 1. SATURDAY AFTER THE NATIVITY 26 DECEMBER (under ### 3.4.10 Saturday after the Nativity)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.10 Saturday after the Nativity)
  H5: ##### 2. SATURDAY AFTER THE NATIVITY 27, 28, 29 OR 30 DECEMBER AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.10 Saturday after the Nativity)
  H5: ##### 3. SATURDAY AFTER THE NATIVITY 30 DECEMBER (under ### 3.4.10 Saturday after the Nativity)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.10 Saturday after the Nativity)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1. SUNDAY AFTER THE NATIVITY 26 DECEMBER (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2. SUNDAY AFTER THE NATIVITY 27, 28, 29 OR 30 DECEMBER (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 of the Feast and 3 of the Saints; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon of the day "The Lord is King". There are no readings. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha Resurrectional; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	Great Dismissal with commemoration of the Resurrectional service, of the Feast and of the Saints. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, Theotokos on 2, of the Feast on 4 and of the Saints on 4. (26 December - 3 canons; Resurrectional on 4, both canons of the Feast on 6 and of the Saints on 4). Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast and Sessional hymn of the Saints; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Saints; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast with its two refrains; Glory: to the Saints, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 11.	Great Dismissal with commemorations, as at Vespers, and immediately Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3. SUNDAY AFTER THE NATIVITY 31 DECEMBER (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	At Matins: Resurrectional Canon on 4, of the Saints on 4 and of the Feast both canons on 6. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Liturgy: Prokimenon of the Feast and to the Saints (Resurrectional is not taken). Alleluia - only of the Feast, Apostle-Gospel - only to the Saints. Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.11 Sunday after the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 1.	One Saturday and one Sunday. (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two Saturdays and one Sunday. (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 3.	One Saturday and two Sundays. (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 4.	Two Saturdays and two Sundays. (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1. Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Fr	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Fr (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2. Mo	Tu	We	Th	Mo	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Mo	Sa (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 3. Tu	We	Th	Mo	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Mo	Sa	Su (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 4. We	Th	Mo	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Mo	Sa	Su	Mo (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 5. Th	Mo	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Mo	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 6. Fr	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Fr	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 7. Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th	Fr	Sa	Su	Mo	Tu	We	Th (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	Which of the two Saturdays or Sundays to take? after the Nativity or before Theophany? (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	What to do with the second Saturday or Sunday, which then has no place? (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H4: #### RULE FOR SATURDAYS AND SUNDAYS (under ### 3.4.12 Rule Concerning Saturdays and Sundays between the Nativity and Theophany)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 of the Feast and 3 of the Saints; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Feast "To Augustus". (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 3.	There are no readings. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: to the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 7.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 8.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of the Saints on 4 and of the Feast both canons on 6. Katavasia of the Feast, irmos of the 1st canon. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Heirmos and Sessional hymn of the Saints, Glory, Both now: Sessional hymn of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th - "More honorable", after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Resurrectional Exaposteilarion; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 9.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 10.	After the Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 11.	After the Dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)

  H5: ##### 13.	Prokimenon of the Feast and of the Saints; Alleluia - only of the Feast; Apostle-Gospel - only to the Saints. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H5: ##### 14.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Feast. (under ### 3.4.14 31 December: Apodosis of the Feast of the Nativity of Christ)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 8 stichera: 4 of the Feast and 4 of Basil, Glory: of Basil, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day and three readings, that is 2 to the Feast and 1 to the Saint. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya and at the Aposticha stichera of Basil, only Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion to Basil twice and of the Feast once[^341]. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two sequential kathismata with Sessional hymns of Basil, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 3.	Polyeleos with "Great Doxology" ("Velychannya"), if you have it printed in the Psalter for 1 January, and Sessional hymn to the Saint, only Both now: of the Feast[^426]. Gradual of Tone 4, 1st antiphon; everything else up to the canon - to Basil. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons 2 making 12: of the Feast with irmos on 4, irmos - twice, and of the Saint on 8. Sequential Katavasia, each Choir - its own irmos. After the 3rd ode - Sessional hymn of Basil, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of Basil; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", - refrains of the Feast and of the Saint; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Saint, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 5.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 to the Feast and 3 to the Saint, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of Basil, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 7.	Great Dismissal of the Feast with commemoration of Basil. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 1.	Daily Antiphons "It is a good thing". (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	Troparion to the Circumcision and to the Saint, Glory: Kontakion to the Saint, Both now: to the Circumcision. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon, Apostle-Gospel and Communion Hymn - to the Circumcision and to the Saint; Alleluia to the Circumcision and to the Saint - common[^342]. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 4.	Instead of "It is truly meet" we sing "In Thee rejoiceth", or according to the new rule of our service book - irmos of the 9th ode of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 1.	If the Nativity of Christ, and consequently 1 January, falls on Monday or Tuesday, then the Sunday before Theophany will not take place, as the table on p. 184 shows, and then later Slavic typikons[^343]  give the Prokimenon, Apostle-Gospel and Communion Hymn of that Sunday on 1 January with the Circumcision, thus, according to such a rule, which should have been given on 1 January on a Sunday, the Sunday before Theophany takes the first place, after it - the Circumcision; the Apostle-Gospel of Basil falls out. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	If 1 January falls on a Saturday, then after the Apostle-Gospel of the Circumcision the Apostle-Gospel of the Saturday before Theophany is read, without Basil[^344]. Everything else was mentioned above. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried": Resurrectional stichera, as usual. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Aposticha: first stichera - Resurrectional, also stichera of Basil of Great Vespers. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: to the Circumcision. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 of the Feast and 4 of the Saint; Glory: of the Saint, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon "The Lord is King" and 3 readings (2 to the Circumcision and 1 to Basil). (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya: stichera of the Saint, only Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 5.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Saint twice and "Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos" once. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two sequential kathismata; everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual; however, after the Polyeleos (if you have it) with "Angelic host" there will be all three sessional hymns of the Saint, only Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of the Feast on 4 and of the Saint on 6; sequential Katavasia with two irmoi, each Choir - its own irmos. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast and Sessional hymn of the Saint, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Saint; at the 9th - "More honorable"; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Resurrectional Exaposteilarion; Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Saint with His two refrains; Glory: Gospel Stichera, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the Entrance: Resurrectional Troparion, of the Feast and of the Saint; Glory: Kontakion to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.1 1 January: Circumcision of the Lord and memory of St. Basil the Great)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon - of the Sunday before Theophany and of the Saint; Alleluia - of the Sunday before Theophany and of the Feast; Apostle-Gospel - of the Sunday before Theophany, also of the Feast, without Basil.   H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.2 2, 3, 4 and 5 January: Forefeast of Theophany)

  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the day. Apostle and Gospel before Theophany. (under ### 3.5.2 2, 3, 4 and 5 January: Forefeast of Theophany)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.2 2, 3, 4 and 5 January: Forefeast of Theophany)
