  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the day. Apostle and Gospel of the Sunday before Theophany. Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord". (under ### 3.5.2 2, 3, 4 and 5 January: Forefeast of Theophany)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.5.4 6 January: Holy Theophany)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Kathisma is not taken, in view of the All-Night Vigil that has taken place. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Forerunner, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Entrance[^381] and Great Prokimenon of the Feast; and "Let us all say". (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Feast, only Doxastikon - to the Forerunner. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Canon to the Theotokos is not taken. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion to the Forerunner, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Feast[^356]. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT Midnight Office (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two sequential kathismata with Sessional hymns of the Feast and immediately Psalm 50 and the canon. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: first of the Feast with irmos on 6, also second of the Feast with irmos on 4, irmoi of both canons - twice, and of the Forerunner on 4. Katavasia after each ode - irmoi of both canons of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast and Sessional hymn of the Feast, and at the same time also of the Forerunner; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forerunner; after the 9th - instead of "More honorable", refrains of the Feast and of the Forerunner; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Forerunner, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises on 4, all - of the Feast, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great Dismissal of the Feast with commemoration of the Synaxis. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion of the Feast and of the Forerunner, Glory: Kontakion to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel of the Forerunner. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 of the Feast and 3 of the Forerunner, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: Dogmatikon of the sequential tone. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, both canons of the Feast on 6 and of the Forerunner on 4. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast and Sessional hymn of the Forerunner, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forerunner; at the 9th - "More honorable", after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 9: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast with their refrains and Doxastikon of the Forerunner with its separate refrain, also Glory: Gospel Stichera, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Trisagion - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion, of the Feast and of the Forerunner; Glory: Kontakion to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany, and then - of the Forerunner, only Communion Hymn - of the Feast and of the Forerunner[^357]. (under ### 3.5.5 7 January: Synaxis of Saint John the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Apostle-Gospel of the ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany. (under ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the Feast and of the Forerunner. (under ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Apostle-Gospel of the ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany and of the Forerunner. (under ### 3.5.6 Saturday after Theophany)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - only of the ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany[^358]. (under ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion and of the Feast; Glory: Kontakion of the Publican, Both now: Kontakion of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon and Alleluia of the ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany, Apostle-Gospel first of the ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany, and afterwards - of the Sunday of the Publican. (under ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany)
  H5: ##### 3.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.7 Sunday after Theophany)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 of the Triodion with Doxastikon and 4 of the Feast; Glory: of the Feast, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual, only after the 50th Psalm at "Glory" we sing: "Open to me the doors of repentance"; Both now: "Guide me in the paths of salvation"; at "Have mercy on me, O God": "As I ponder the many". (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of the Triodion on 4 and both canons of the Feast on 6. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Triodion, Glory, and now: of the Feast[^417]; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th - "More honorable"; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast with Doxastikon; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion and of the Feast, Glory: Kontakion of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon and Alleluia of the tone and of the Feast, Apostle-Gospel of the Sunday of the Publican. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H5: ##### 3.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Feast. (under ### 3.5.9 14 January: Apodosis of Theophany)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 27 JANUARY)
  H5: ##### 1.	Sunday of the Publican, of the Prodigal [Son] and Meatfare. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 2.	Meatfare Saturday (of Souls). (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 3.	Cheesefare Monday, Tuesday or Thursday. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 4.	Cheesefare Wednesday or Friday. We give the rule for all these cases. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 of the Triodion and 4 of the Saints (on Meatfare Sunday: 4 of the Triodion and 3 of the Saints); Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "The Lord is King" - 3 readings to the Saints. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Litya: Stichera of the Praises of the Triodion; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Resurrectional; Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion "Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos" twice and of the Saints once. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the canon everything - according to the general rule of a Saint with All-Night Vigil on Sunday, only if there be also the Sunday of the Prodigal [Son] or Meatfare, to the Polyeleos we add the Psalm "By the waters of Babylon" and after the 50th Psalm we sing "Open to me the doors" and the rest with the stichera "As I ponder the many"[^363]. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, Theotokos (according to the order of the Octoechos) on 2, Triodion on 4 and Saints on 4 (if Meatfare Sunday, then Resurrectional on 4, Triodion on 6 and Saints on 4). Sequential Katavasia "The dry land deep". After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Saints, Both now: of the Triodion; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Triodion; at the 9th - "More honorable"; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Saints with Doxastikon, with their two refrains (on Meatfare Sunday, after the stichera of the Saints with their refrains, we use the refrain "Arise, O Lord" and the stichera of the Triodion, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion and of the Saints, also Resurrectional Kontakion, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: Kontakion of the Triodion. (Thus in the rules of Slavic menaia, but in Greek rules and of our liturgikons the Resurrectional Kontakion is not given). (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon and Alleluia Resurrectional of the tone (on Meatfare and Cheesefare Sunday there will be separate and of the Saints). (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 3.	Apostle-Gospel of the Sunday and of the Saints. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 4.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Saints. (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H4: #### RULE AT SMALL VESPERS (under ### 3.5.10 30 January: The Three Hierarchs)
  H5: ##### 1.	On the Sundays of the Publican, of the Prodigal [Son], Meatfare and Cheesefare. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Meatfare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	On Cheesefare Monday, Tuesday and Thursday. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	On Cheesefare Wednesday and Friday. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Cheesefare Saturday[^375]. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 of the Octoechos, 3 of the Triodion and 3 of the Forefeast (on Meatfare Sunday: 3 of the Octoechos, 4 of the Triodion and 3 of the Forefeast); Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Aposticha Resurrectional; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual, only on the Sundays of the Prodigal [Son], Meatfare and Cheesefare we add to the Polyeleos the Psalm "By the waters of Babylon" and before the canon we sing "Open to me the doors". (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, Theotokos on 2, Forefeast on 4 and Triodion on 4. Katavasia of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 5 of the Octoechos and 3 of the Triodion with their refrains (on Meatfare Sunday: 4 of the Octoechos and 4 of the Triodion); Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion and of the Forefeast, Glory: Kontakion of the Triodion, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon and Alleluia of the tone, Apostle-Gospel and Communion Hymn of the day. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	Three great prostrations we make without the Prayer of Ephraim at the end of Vespers and at the end of Matins immediately after the concluding litany, and at Compline - after the Kontakion of the Forefeast, and at each Hour - after "More honorable". (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Compline will be Great, at which we sing the 1st three-ode canon of the Matins canon and after the 1st and 2nd Trisagion - usual troparia, and after "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	At Matins - canons on 14: Forefeast on 6, to the Saint on 4 and Triodion on 4. And where the second three-ode canon (for the first was sung at Compline), there the canon of the Forefeast on 4, three troparia of the Saint on 2 and of the Triodion on 8. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma is sequential. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Forefeast and 3 of the Saints, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone that is being given up. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Prokimenon of the day - one reading of the Triodion and Prokimenon of the Triodion, without the litany "Let us all say"[^371]. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Triodion, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 6.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God". (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Forefeast twice, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two sequential Kathismata with Sessional hymns of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 14: Forefeast on 6 and Fathers on 8. Katavasia after each ode - irmos of the Triodion. After the 3rd - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefeast and Sessional hymn of the Fathers; Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Fathers; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - of the Fathers on 4, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	Great Doxology and Troparion of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.6.1 1 February: Forefeast of the Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	On the Sundays of the Publican, of the Prodigal [Son], Meatfare and Cheesefare. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Meatfare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	On Cheesefare Monday, Tuesday and Thursday. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	On Cheesefare Wednesday and Friday. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Cheesefare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 6.	On Monday of the first week of Lent. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 of the Triodion and 4 of the Feast (on Meatfare Sunday: 4 of the Triodion and 3 of the Feast). (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Litya: 3 stichera of the Feast and 2 (on the Prodigal [Son] - 3) stichera of the Triodion, given at the Praises; also Glory: of the Triodion, given at the Litya, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	On the Sundays of the Prodigal [Son], Meatfare and Cheesefare we add to the Polyeleos the Psalm "By the waters of Babylon" and afterwards - "Open to me the doors". (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, Triodion on 4 and of the Feast on 6 (on Meatfare Sunday: Triodion on 6 and of the Feast on 4). Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Triodion, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast, at the 9th - "More honorable", after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast, with refrains of the Feast. (On Meatfare and Cheesefare Sundays, after the stichera of the Feast, we sing the verse "Arise, O Lord" and the idiomelon of the Triodion). Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	Entrance verse of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)

  H5: ##### 3.	Everything else - first of the Sunday, and then - of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 4 stichera of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the Prokimenon of the day - Paremia and Prokimenon of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)

  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Troparion to the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Feast and 4 to the Fathers, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day and 3 readings of the Feast[^372]. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya: stichera of the Feast[^373]. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	Aposticha of the Feast, only Doxastikon - to the Fathers. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Feast twice and of the Fathers once. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 14: of the Feast on 8 and of the Fathers on 6. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Fathers; Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast, at the 9th - "More honorable"; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Fathers, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	Entrance verse of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 2.	Troparion of the Feast and of the Fathers, Glory: Kontakion to the fathers, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 3.	Everything else - of the Feast, and then - of the fathers. (under ### 3.6.2 2 February: Meeting of the Lord)
  H5: ##### 1.	On the Sundays of the Publican, of the Prodigal [Son] and Meatfare. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Meatfare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 3.	On Cheesefare Monday, Tuesday and Thursday. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 4.	On Cheesefare Wednesday and Friday. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Cheesefare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	At Matins: the last canon will be of the Feast, and the penultimate - of the Triodion. (And in the Forefeast: the last - of the Triodion, and the penultimate - of the Feast). And at the Praises here on Meatfare Sunday there will be not only 8 stichera, but also a 9th, with the refrain "Arise, O Lord". (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Liturgy, instead of "It is truly meet", we sing here the irmos of the Feast; everything else as in the Forefeast here on p. 213. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	At Vespers, at "Lord, I have cried", instead of the Dogmatikon of the tone that is being given up, typikons here give the stichera of the Feast[^376]. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	At Compline the named typikons give here only the Kontakion of the Feast, and for the Forefeast they give first the Kontakion of the Fathers, and then Glory, Both now: of the Feast. We think that here too it should be the same. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 3.	At Matins the named typikons give here the Katavasia of the Feast, and for the Forefeast - the Katavasia of the Triodion. However, if in the Afterfeast of other feasts at Small Matins the Katavasia of the Feast is not taken, but the irmos of the last canon is taken, then here too it should be so. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Liturgy there will be Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn to the Feast and to the Fathers, and in the Forefeast - both of the day, and to the Fathers. Apostle and Gospel both here, and there will be of the day and to the Fathers. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1. Sun. of Prodigal [Son], Mon. and Tue. of Cheesefare	Fri. Meatfare (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2. Meatfare Wed., Thu., Fri., Sat.	Tue. Cheesefare (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 3. Meatfare Sun., Cheesefare Mon.	Thu. Cheesefare (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 4. Cheesefare Tue. and Wed.	Sat. Cheesefare (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 5. Cheesefare Thu., Fri., Sat.	Sun. Cheesefare (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 6. Cheesefare Sun.	none (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 7. Mon. of 1st week of Lent	none (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	Apodosis of the Meeting on the Sundays of the Publican, of the Prodigal [Son] and Cheesefare[^377]. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	Apodosis of the Meeting on Cheesefare Tuesday and Thursday[^378]. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 3.	Apodosis of the Meeting on Cheesefare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Canon on 4, of the Triodion on 4 and of the Feast on 6. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	Stichera of the Praises - 9: 4 Resurrectional, 4 of the Feast, also the idiomelon of the Triodion with the refrain "Arise, O Lord"; and afterwards - Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	The canon, where the three-ode canon comes, will be of the Feast on 6 and of the three-ode canon on 8. After the 3rd ode - Sessional hymn of the Triodion, given after the 2nd Kathisma, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; and at the 9th ode we sing "More honorable". (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Praises after the stichera of the Feast there will be the Small Doxology, and after "Let us complete" - stichera at the Aposticha from the Triodion, as at Vespers and, at the end, - Troparion of the Feast, litany and dismissal (great). (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	Both Sessional hymns of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons of the Feast on 8 and to the Saints on 6. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast. (under ### 3.6.3 3 - 8 February: Afterfeast of the Meeting)
  H5: ##### 1.	Since the last boundary of the Triodion is 14 February, this feast always falls in the middle of the Triodion. If the Triodion is earlier, then this feast enters all the deeper into the Triodion and coincides with many Triodion services. Even more, this feast can coincide with other services, given in the Triodion from Wednesday of Meatfare Week to Tuesday of the 8th week of the Triodion, that is of the 4th of Great Lent, inclusive. The last boundary of the beginning of the Triodion is 14 February, and after subtracting from 14 February 13 days, which precede the beginning of the Triodion, 11 will remain, dividing which by 7, we will get in the quotient 1, with 4 in the remainder, that is one whole week and one not whole - the first four days; and since the first day of the first week is Sunday (for the Triodion begins on Sunday), then the first day of the second week will be Sunday, the second - Monday, the third - Tuesday, the fourth - Wednesday. And since the first Sunday of the Triodion is the Sunday of the Publican, the second - of the Prodigal [Son], the third - Meatfare, then the Wednesday on which the Finding falls will be Meatfare Wednesday (for in the Lenten Triodion the days of the week are named after the following Sunday), as the earliest boundary of this feast in relation to the Triodion. And that the last boundary of this feast in relation to the Triodion is Tuesday of the 4th week of Great Lent is proven thus. The first boundary of the Triodion is 11 January, from which to 24 February will be 45 days, dividing which by 7, we will get 6 whole weeks with a remainder of 3; but, since the first day of this remainder is Sunday, as is the first day of each of the 6 past weeks, it will be the 7th Sunday of the Triodion, and the second day will be Monday, the third - Tuesday of the 8th week of the Triodion, of the 4th of Great Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	The Service of this feast - of the type of a Saint with Polyeleos without All-Night Vigil, and since it coincides with services of the Triodion, it has 12 following cases. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	On Meatfare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	On the Sundays of Meatfare and Cheesefare. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	On Cheesefare Monday, Tuesday and Thursday. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	On Cheesefare Wednesday and Friday. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Cheesefare Saturday. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 6.	On one of the first weekdays of the 1st week of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 7.	On Saturday of the 1st week of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 8.	On the 1st Sunday of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 9.	On one of the first weekdays of the 2nd and 3rd weeks of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 10.	On Saturdays of the 2nd and 3rd weeks of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 11.	On the 2nd Sunday of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 12.	On the 3rd Sunday of Lent. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 of the Triodion and 3 of the Forerunner (on Cheesefare Sunday: 4 Resurrectional, 3 of the Triodion and 3 of the Forerunner); Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "The Lord is King" - 3 readings to the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)

  H5: ##### 4.	Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the canon everything - according to the general rule of a Saint with Polyeleos on Sunday, only to the Polyeleos we add also the Psalm "By the waters of Babylon" and after the 50th Psalm - "Open to me the doors". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, of the Triodion on 6 and of the Forerunner on 4. (On Cheesefare Sunday - 4 canons: Resurrectional on 4, Theotokos on 2, Triodion on 4 and Forerunner on 4). Katavasia of the Triodion. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Forerunner, Glory, and now: of the Triodion; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Triodion; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 9: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Forerunner with Doxastikon, with His two refrains; also the verse "Arise, O Lord" and stichera of the Triodion, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Dismissal - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)

  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the Sunday, and then - of the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma sequential, only on Sunday evening - "Blessed is the man" (according to the rule - 1st antiphon)[^380]. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: all to the Forerunner, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: 1st Theotokion (Dogmatikon) according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Entrance, Prokimenon of the day from the Horologion, 3 readings to the Forerunner (on Wednesday evening: Prokimenon of the day and Paremia of the Triodion, Prokimenon of the Triodion and 3 Paremias to the Forerunner) and immediately "Vouchsafe, O Lord", without "Let us all say". The Priest, at the end of the readings, having closed the Holy Doors, takes off the phelonion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Triodion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: his Theotokion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory, and now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 6.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and Great Dismissal. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the 1st Kathisma there will be the Sessional hymn of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	In the canon, where the three-ode canon comes, we sing the canon of the Forerunner on 6 and of the Triodion on 8. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the stichera, at the Praises, there will be the Small Doxology, and after "Let us complete" - Aposticha of the Triodion. Everything else, according to the rule, - of Daily Matins. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 to the Forerunner and 3 to the Fathers, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone that is being given up. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance: Prokimenon of the day and Paremia of the Triodion, Prokimenon of the Triodion and 3 readings to the Forerunner; and immediately after the readings the Priest having taken off the phelonion and having closed the Holy Doors: "Vouchsafe, O Lord".   H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Triodion and one stichera to the Forerunner with his refrain; Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: their Theotokion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 6.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and three great prostrations. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion to the Forerunner twice, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Fathers. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	After two sequential kathismata - Sessional hymns of the Fathers, and also Polyeleos with "Great Doxology" and everything else up to the canon - only to the Forerunner, that is all three sessional hymns, Gradual, Prokimenon, Gospel and Stichera. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 14: of the Forerunner on 6 and of the Fathers on 8. Katavasia of the Triodion. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Forerunner, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: their Theotokion; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Fathers; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Forerunner, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: their Theotokion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 to the Forerunner and 3 to the Fathers, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: their Theotokion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	Great Doxology, Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory: to the Fathers, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Fathers. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)

  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 of the Triodion, 3 to the Forerunner and 4 to Theodore, Glory: to Theodore, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone that is being given up. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance: Prokimenon of the Triodion, one reading of the Triodion and 2nd Prokimenon of the Triodion, and then "The Light of Christ" with a candlestick and the second reading of the Triodion, and also 3 readings to the Forerunner and "Let my prayer be set forth" and everything else of the Presanctified[^383]. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canons for the deceased of the Octoechos and sequential of the Menaion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	If Small Compline is celebrated, as Greek typikons give, then after "It is truly meet" will be Kontakion to the Forerunner and Theodore. If - Great, as Slavic Triodia have, then after the 1st Trisagion we say the Troparion of both saints, after the 2nd - usual troparia "Have mercy on us, O Lord", and at the last Trisagion - Kontakion to the Forerunner[^385]. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At Matins both first sessional hymns will be Theodore's. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Liturgy: Prokimenon and Alleluia will be only to both saints together[^386]. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 8. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 of the Triodion and 3 to the Forerunner, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon of the day and 3 readings to the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: "To Thine immaculate Image". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: "To Thine immaculate"[^387]. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - according to the general rule of a Saint with Polyeleos on Sunday, only after Ps. 50 - "Open to me the doors". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, Forerunner on 4 and Triodion on 6. Katavasia of the Triodion. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn to the Forerunner, Glory: Both now: of the Triodion; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Triodion; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises on 9: 4 Resurrectional, 3 of the Triodion on 4 with their two refrains, and also idiomelon stichera of the Triodion with the refrain "Arise", Glory: the same, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Dismissal - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion, "To the Image" and to the Forerunner, Glory: Kontakion to the Forerunner, Both now: Kontakion "To the Image". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - of the Sunday, afterwards - to the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 9. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 of the Triodion and 6 to the Forerunner, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: 1st Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	Entrance, Great Prokimenon of the Triodion, 3 readings to the Forerunner and immediately "Vouchsafe, O Lord". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Triodion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday Aposticha according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 5.	After "Now lettest Thou" - Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory, and now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 6.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God", three great prostrations without prayer and Great Dismissal. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2. At "Lord, I have cried" there will be only 6 stichera: 3 of the Triodion and 3 to the Forerunner, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: 1st Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3. After the Entrance with "O Gladsome Light" there will be two Paremias of the Triodion, each with its Prokimenon, and also 3 readings to the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4. After the three prostrations, which are after the litany "Have mercy on us, O God", there will be the conclusion of "The Typika" (Typika), that is "O All-Holy Trinity", "Blessed be the name of the Lord", "Blessed be the Lord", "It is truly meet" and Dismissal. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" there will be 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 to the Forerunner, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: 1st Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Immediately after the readings to the Forerunner - "Let my prayer be set forth" and according to the order Liturgy of the Presanctified. If the Finding falls on Wednesday, then after "Let my prayer be set forth" there will be also Prokimenon, Alleluia and Apostle-Gospel of the Forerunner, Communion Hymn "O taste" ("Vkusite") and of the Forerunner. If the Finding falls on Thursday, then on Wednesday evening there will be no Apostle-Gospel of the Forerunner, but it will be in the morning on Thursday. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	On Sunday - Small Compline, after "It is truly meet" - Kontakion to the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	On other days - Great Compline. After the 1st Trisagion - Troparion to the Forerunner, Glory, and now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion; after the 2nd Trisagion - usual troparia "Have mercy on us, O Lord"; after "It is truly meet", instead of "O Lord of hosts, be with us", - Kontakion to the Forerunner; after "More honorable" - three great prostrations. We do not take the Trisagion. Small Dismissal, as at Small Compline. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	The Canon will be only one, namely of the Forerunner on 8. Katavasia "I shall open". And where there is a three-ode canon, there will be the Canon of the Forerunner on 6 and both canons of the three-ode canon on 8; Katavasia of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the stichera of the Praises there will be the Small Doxology, after which - Aposticha of the Triodion in its place, with the Doxastikon of the Forerunner; after the Troparion of the Forerunner - Glory, and now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion and after the litany "Have mercy on us, O God" - three great prostrations without dismissal. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 10. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 11. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Before the canon we sing "Open to me [the doors]". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 4 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, of the Triodion on 4 and of the Forerunner on 6. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion of the Triodion and Kontakion-Ikos of the Forerunner; after the 6th - Resurrectional Kontakion-Ikos; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Forerunner, Both now: Theotokion of the Resurrectional Exaposteilarion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Praises, after 8 Resurrectional stichera and of the Forerunner, there will be also one of the Triodion with the refrain "Arise, O Lord" and the same with the refrain "Glory", and Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Dismissal - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion and to the Forerunner, Glory: Kontakion to the Forerunner, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the Sunday, and then - of the Forerunner. (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)
  H5: ##### 12. FINDING OF THE PRECIOUS HEAD ON THE THIRD SUNDAY OF LENT (under ### 3.6.4 24 February: First and Second Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner)

  H5: ##### 6.	On the 3rd Sunday of Lent. (under ### 3.7.1 1 March: Holy 40 Martyrs)
  H5: ##### 7.	On the 4th and 5th Sundays of Lent. (under ### 3.7.1 1 March: Holy 40 Martyrs)

  H5: ##### 1.	On one of the fast days of the Forty Days. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Saturday of the 3rd and 4th weeks of Lent. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Sunday of the Veneration of the Cross. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 4.	On Wednesday and Friday of the Veneration of the Cross. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Thursday of the Veneration of the Cross. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 6.	On the 4th and 5th Sundays of Lent. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 7.	On Tuesday of the 5th week of Lent. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 8.	On Thursday of the 5th week of Lent, that is of the Great Canon. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 9.	On the Akathist Saturday, 5th of Lent. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 10.	On Lazarus Saturday. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 1. FOREFEAST OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried", after 6 stichera, which are in the Triodion, there will be 4 - of the Forefeast; Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Aposticha, after the stichera of the Triodion, - Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Forefeast on 6 and of the Triodion on 8. After the 3rd ode - Sessional hymn of the Forefeast, and after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion, Glory, and now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 2. FOREFEAST OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Forefeast twice, Glory, and now: the same. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathismata and Sessional hymns usual of the Octoechos. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canon of the Forefeast on 6[^394]. After the 3rd ode - Katavasia of the Forefeast and Sessional hymn of the Forefeast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Forefeast, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Forefeast. When there is a four-ode canon, that is the 6th, 7th, 8th and 9th odes, then - of the Forefeast on 6 and of the Triodion on 8, Katavasia of the Triodion. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Octoechos, as on all Saturdays, only Glory, and now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparion of the Forefeast one and after the litany - dismissal, as usual. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 3. FOREFEAST OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)

  H5: ##### 2.	Aposticha Resurrectional; Glory: Both now: of the Cross, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)

After the 3rd ode - Kontakion of the Forefeast, and also Sessional hymn of the Cross; Glory, and now: of the Forefeast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Cross; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion; Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Forefeast.   H5: ##### 4.	Everything else to the end - according to the rule of the Sunday of the Veneration of the Cross. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)

  H5: ##### 2.	Likewise also at the Aposticha of Vespers and Matins after the stichera of the Triodion there will be Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canon of the Triodion on 6 and of the Forefeast on 4, and where there is a three-ode canon, there the Canon of the Cross on 4, of the Forefeast on 2 and the three-ode canon on 8. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Praises - 4 stichera of the Cross, which are taken at "Lord, I have cried", which were left, that is 3 last on 4; Glory, and now: again must be taken from "Lord, I have cried"[^397]. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion, Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Forefeast. (under ### 3.7.2 24 March: Forefeast of the Annunciation)

After the 3rd ode - Kontakion of the Forefeast and Sessional hymn of the Triodion; Glory, and now: of the Forefeast; after the 6th - Resurrectional Kontakion-Ikos; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory, and now: of the Forefeast.
