  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 of the Triodion and 7 of the Feast; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance - Prokimenon of the day from the Horologion and 3 readings of the Feast[^400]; other things of Vespers with the blessing of loaves - according to the general rule of feasts of the Theotokos. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	There is no Kathisma. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 8 stichera: 3 of the Triodion and 5 of the Feast; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance - Prokimenon of the day from the Horologion, readings of the day and three of the Feast[^401]  and immediately - "Vouchsafe, O Lord". At the Aposticha: idiomelon twice and martyricon with the usual refrains; Glory, and now: of the Feast. After "Now lettest Thou" - Troparion of the Feast, litany, three great prostrations and Great Dismissal with commemoration of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	There is no Kathisma. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 5 of the Triodion and 5 of the Feast; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance - 2 readings of the Triodion with their Prokimena and 3 of the Feast, also "Let my prayer be set forth" and other things of the Presanctified. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the 1st poetic session there will be the Sessional hymn of the Triodion, without litany. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canon of the Feast, irmoi twice, troparia on 12, and where there is a three-ode canon, there will be 2 canons on 14, that is of the Feast with irmos on 6, of the three-ode canon on 8 and katavasia - irmos of the three-ode canon. At the 9th ode to the irmos and to the troparia of the Canon of the Feast we sing separate refrains, and to the troparia of the three-ode canon - "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"; also after the Katavasia we sing the irmos of the Triodion and of the Feast; the refrain and irmos both choirs sing together. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the stichera of the Praises - Small Doxology; Aposticha of the Triodion; Glory, and now: of the Feast. "It is a good thing" once, and after "Our Father" - Troparion of the Feast and litany "Have mercy on us, O God" with three great prostrations. This Matins ends just as the daily one, that is without dismissal. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Hours are read with kathismata according to the Lenten rule. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	There are no prostrations, with the exception of three great ones before the concluding prayer of each Hour. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	On all there will be the Troparion and Kontakion only of the Feast, without the Lenten ones. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the 6th - Troparion, Paremias of the Triodion, Paremia. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	There is no Kathisma, nor prostrations. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 11 stichera: 5 of the Triodion, 3 of the Feast and 3 of Archangel Gabriel, the last of which has a separate refrain; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Entrance with the Gospel; 2 readings of the Triodion with their Prokimena and 2 of the Feast[^402]; after the Small Litany - exclamation of the Trisagion and further according to the order the Liturgy of Chrysostom, at which - everything to the Feast. Instead of "It is truly meet" - irmos of the 9th ode of the canon. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 of the Triodion, 4 of the Feast and 3 of the Archangel; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Entrance with the Gospel; 2 readings of the Triodion with prokeimena and 2 of the Feast, and also Prokimenon; Apostle and Gospel of the Feast; and immediately "Vouchsafe, O Lord". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha of the Triodion, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	After "Now lettest Thou": Troparion of the Archangel, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God", three great prostrations and dismissal (great). (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. ANNUNCIATION ON THE 3RD AND 4TH SATURDAYS OF GREAT LENT (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	There is no Kathisma. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: idiomelon of the Triodion twice and 8 of the Feast; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Readings - 7: 2 of the Triodion with their Prokimena and 5 of the Feast, and then - "Let my prayer be set forth" and according to the order the Liturgy of the Presanctified. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Great Compline, according to the rule given here on p. 252. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Great Matins exactly according to the general rule of a feast of the Theotokos, of the Triodion - nothing. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - stichera on 10: 3 Resurrectional, 3 of the Cross and 4 of the Feast; Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Five readings of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya - 6 stichera: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Cross from the Praises; Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	Aposticha Resurrectional; Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Feast twice and of the Cross once. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Other things up to the canon - according to the general rule of a feast of the Theotokos. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, of the Cross on 4 and of the Feast on 6. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Cross, Glory, Both now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th - "More honorable"; after the 9th - Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Cross, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 9: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast, and also idiomelon "Arise, O Lord" and idiomelon of the day; Glory: the same, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - everything only to the Cross with prostrations, according to the rule of this Sunday. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Dismissal - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion, of the Cross and of the Feast; Glory: Kontakion of the Cross, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Instead of the Trisagion - "Before Thy Cross", and instead of "In Thee rejoiceth" - Heirmos of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Everything else - first of the Feast, and then - of the Cross. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. ANNUNCIATION ON WEDNESDAY OF THE VENERATION OF THE CROSS (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. ANNUNCIATION ON THE 4TH AND 5TH SUNDAYS OF LENT (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional on 4, of the Triodion on 4 and of the Feast on 6. After the 3rd ode there is no Resurrectional Kontakion-Ikos, but only the Sessional hymn of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the "Praises", after 4 Resurrectional stichera and 4 stichera of the Feast there will be also the 9th idiomelon of the Triodion with its refrain, also Glory: the same, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Dismissal - Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion and of the Feast; Glory, and now: Kontakion of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - of the Sunday, only Communion Hymn of the Sunday and of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6. ANNUNCIATION ON THURSDAY OF THE GREAT CANON (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 7. ANNUNCIATION ON FRIDAY OF THE 5TH WEEK OF LENT (OF THE AKATHIST) (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 8. ANNUNCIATION ON SATURDAY OF THE 5TH WEEK OF LENT (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 2 of the Triodion and 8 of the Feast, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. After the Entrance - 7 readings: 2 of the Triodion with their prokeimena and 5 of the Feast, and also "Let my prayer be set forth" and everything else of the Presanctified[^408]. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory, and now: again the same. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. Kathismata of the day. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. Canons 2 making 14: of the Triodion (that is of the Temple) on 6 and of the Feast on 8. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion of the Temple; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. At the "Praises", stichera on 4: of the Feast 3 and the 4th with Glory, and now. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. At the Aposticha - stichera of the Feast, with their refrains; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 9. ANNUNCIATION ON LAZARUS SATURDAY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 10. ANNUNCIATION ON PALM SUNDAY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 5 of Palm Sunday and 5 of the Feast; Glory: of Palm Sunday, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. Readings: three of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. At the Litya: stichera of Palm Sunday; Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. At the Aposticha: stichera of the Feast; Glory: of Palm Sunday, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Feast twice and of Palm Sunday once. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice; Glory, and now: of Palm Sunday. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. Everything else up to the canon - of Palm Sunday, only after the 50th Psalm instead of the stichera of Palm Sunday the stichera of the Feast is sung. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. Canons 2 making 14: of Palm Sunday on 6 and of the Feast on 8. Katavasia of Palm Sunday and of the Feast, after the 3rd ode - Kontakion of the Feast, after the 6th - Kontakion of Palm Sunday, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of Palm Sunday, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. At the "Praises", 8 stichera: 4 of Palm Sunday and 4 of the Feast with their refrains; Glory: of Palm Sunday, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Feast, Glory, and now: of Palm Sunday. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. Troparion of the Feast and of Palm Sunday; Glory: Kontakion of Palm Sunday, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. Instead of "It is truly meet" - irmos of the 9th ode of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 11. ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Feast and 4 of Palm Sunday, Glory: of Palm Sunday, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. At the Litya - stichera of the Feast from the Praises and 3 stichera of Palm Sunday. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. At the Aposticha: stichera of the Feast, and at the Glory - stichera of Palm Sunday, Glory and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Feast twice. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 5 of the Triodion and 5 of the Feast; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. Entrance with the Gospel; 5 readings: 2 of the Triodion with their Prokimena and 3 of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Alleluia": Troparion of the Feast twice. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. After the first two kathismata - Sessional hymns of the Triodion; after the 3rd - Small Litany and Sessional hymn of the Feast, also Polyeleos with "Magnifications" and Post-Polyeleos Sessional hymn of the Feast[^410]. Other things up to the canon - of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. Canon of the Feast with irmos on 14, irmos - twice; troparia on 12. Katavasia of the Feast with irmos on 6, and if a two- or three-ode canon, then the Canon of the Feast with irmos on 6 and the two- or three-ode canon on 8; katavasia - irmos of the Triodion. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", there will be the refrain of the Feast, and regarding the troparia of the two- or three-ode canon - "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee", katavasia - irmos of the Triodion, and according to the Pochaiv and Russian typikons - refrain and irmos of the Feast. After the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. Stichera of the Praises - 4, all - of the Feast, Glory: of the Feast, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. After the Small Doxology at the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion with the usual refrains of the Horologion; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. On all: Troparion and Kontakion of the Feast. Thus Tserkovne Oko in chapter of Mark 93 and the Handwritten Typikon in chapter of Mark 39, and according to the Pochaiv and Russian typikons, on all - Troparion of the Feast. Kontakia - alternating: on the 1st and 6th - of the day ("Behold the Bridegroom"), on the 3rd and 9th - of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. There are no prostrations, only before the concluding prayer of each hour - 3 great prostrations. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. At "The Typika": Kontakion of the Feast, and according to the Pochaiv and Russian typikons: Kontakion of the day ("Behold the Bridegroom"), Glory, and now: of the Feast; and after "More honorable" - great prostrations. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 5 of the Triodion and 5 of the Feast, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. After the Entrance with the Gospel - 4 readings: 2 of the Triodion with their Prokimena and 2 of the Feast. After the Small Litany - exclamation of the Trisagion and Trisagion; and according to the order the Liturgy of Chrysostom, at which everything - of the Feast, only the Gospel - first of the Feast, and then - of the day. Instead of "It is truly meet" - Heirmos of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 5 first of the Triodion, repeating the first two, and 5 of the Feast; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. After the Entrance with the Gospel - 2 readings of the Triodion, each with its Prokimenon, 2 of the Feast, and then: Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel of the Feast and Gospel of the day, and immediately the Priest, having closed the holy doors and divested: "Vouchsafe, O Lord". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. At the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion, two last from "Lord, I have cried", repeating the first, with two usual refrains of the Horologion. Glory: of the Triodion, the 4th from "Lord, I have cried": "O Lord, Who goest to suffering", Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5. After "Now lettest Thou" - Troparion of the Feast, litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and dismissal (great with commemoration of the Passion and of the Feast). (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 12. ANNUNCIATION ON GREAT THURSDAY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory, and now: of the day "When the glorious" and immediately: (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Polyeleos and everything else up to the canon - to the Feast, according to the general rule of a feast of the Theotokos. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 16: of the Feast with irmos on 8, irmos - twice, and of the Triodion with irmos on 8, irmos - twice. Katavasia of the Triodion. Everything else to the end of Matins - according to the rule of Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday, here on p. 263-264, only at the end: Troparion of the day, Glory, and now: of the Feast and "Have mercy on us, O God", without prostrations[^411]. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 13. ANNUNCIATION ON GREAT FRIDAY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 5 of the Triodion and 5 of the Feast, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. Entrance with the Gospel. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Readings - 6: 3 of the Triodion, the first two - with their Prokimena, and 3 of the Feast, after this - Small Litany and exclamation of the Trisagion, Trisagion and according to the order the Liturgy of Basil, at which everything - only of the day. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory, and now: of the day ("When the glorious"). (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the 7th Gospel the Gradual of Tone 4 and everything else up to the canon - to the Feast, according to the general rule of a feast of the Theotokos, only after the Gospel of the Feast the 8th Passion [Gospel] is also read. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canon of the Feast with irmos on 14; irmos - twice; troparia on 12. Katavasia of the Feast, and where - three-ode canon, there the Canon of the Feast on 6, three-ode canon on 8 and Katavasia of the three-ode canon. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the day; Glory, and now: Sessional hymn of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", there will be the refrain of the Feast; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast, Glory: of the day, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	At "The Praises": stichera of the Triodion, only Both now: of the Feast, and at the Aposticha: stichera of the Feast, only Glory: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	After "Our Father" - Troparion of the Triodion "Thou Hast redeemed us", Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the litany - separate dismissal of Friday with commemoration of the Annunciation. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 of the Feast, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. Entrance with the Gospel. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Readings - 5: 3 of the Triodion, the first two - with their Prokimena, and 2 of the Feast; and immediately Small Litany with exclamation of the Trisagion and according to the order the Liturgy of Chrysostom, at which everything - first of the Feast, and then - of the day. Communion Hymn only of the Feast. Instead of "It is truly meet" - Heirmos of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 14. ANNUNCIATION ON GREAT SATURDAY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 of the Feast; Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the Entrance with the Gospel - 8 readings: 3 of the Triodion, the first two with their Prokimena, and 5 of the Feast, and immediately Prokimenon of the day of the Triodion; also Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - of the day. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion, only Glory: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end, according to the rule of the Triodion: Troparion of the Feast, and then Glory, and now: "The noble Joseph"; but since according to the present custom the Bringing Out of the Shroud takes place and the Troparion "The noble Joseph" we sing after placing it in the prepared tomb, therefore one should sing only the Troparion of the Feast, and after the Bringing Out of the Shroud - Troparion "The noble". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the Dismissal of Great Saturday there will be commemoration of the Annunciation. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion "The noble" once and "When Thou didst descend" once, Glory: "Unto the myrrh-bearing women", Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the Sessional hymn of the Triodion, which is before the canon, we sing the Polyeleos and everything else up to the canon - to the Feast, according to the general rule of a feast of the Theotokos. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 16: of the Feast with irmos on 8, irmoi - twice; troparia on 6 and of the Triodion with irmos on 8, irmoi - twice. Katavasia of the Triodion. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Triodion, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", there will be the refrain of the Feast and after the Katavasia of the Triodion - refrain and irmos of the Feast; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Exaposteilarion of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Triodion, and then stichera of the Feast with its refrain, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 of the Feast, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance with the Gospel there is no Prokimenon, but immediately the Priest: "Wisdom", and the Reader - readings of the day according to their sequence and, after their conclusion - Small Litany, exclamation of the Trisagion and "As many as have been baptized into Christ"; and according to the order the Liturgy of Basil, at which everything - first of the day, and afterwards - of the Feast. Instead of "Cherubic Hymn" - "Let all mortal flesh keep silence", and instead of "In Thee rejoiceth" - Heirmos of the day "Weep not for Me, O Mother". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 15. ANNUNCIATION ON THE SUNDAY OF THE RESURRECTION (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma none. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 of the Feast, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the Entrance with the Gospel there is no Prokimenon, but the Priest: "Wisdom", and the Reader - the first reading of the Triodion, and also 5 readings of the Feast and other readings of the Triodion and, after the Small Litany - exclamation of the Trisagion, "As many as have been baptized into Christ" and according to the order the Liturgy of Basil, at which everything - first of the day, and afterwards - of the Feast. Instead of "Cherubic Hymn" - "Let all mortal flesh keep silence", and instead of "In Thee rejoiceth" - Heirmos of the day "Weep not for Me, O Mother". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the verses "Let God arise" we do not add to the troparion "Christ is risen", "Glory, and now", but after the last verse the Choir sings "Christ is risen" once, the Priest - the same up to the half, and all finish: "And upon those in the tombs", after which we enter the temple, the Priest - the sanctuary, and we sing Glory: Troparion of the Feast, Both now: the same. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Paschal Canon with irmos on 8 and of the Annunciation with irmos on 8. Katavasia of Pascha. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Hypakoe of the Resurrection; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast, also Prokimenon of the Feast "Let everything that hath breath"; Gospel of the Feast; "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" (3) and stichera "Jesus having risen from the grave" (3). At the 9th - refrains of Pascha and of the Feast, Exaposteilarion of Pascha and of the Feast with the refrain "Glory, and now". (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Feast with their refrains, also stichera of the Resurrection "Let God arise", Glory: of the Feast, Both now: of the Resurrection, "It is the Day of Resurrection" and, after "Christ is risen", - litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and "Let us complete"; Paschal Dismissal with commemoration of the Annunciation. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion "Christ is risen" (3), Glory: Troparion of the Feast, Both now: Kontakion of the Resurrection. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the Resurrection, and then - of the Feast, only bells are not rung for the Gospel of the Feast. Instead of "It is truly meet" - "The Angel Cried" with the Heirmos of the Resurrection. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 16. ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 of the Triodion and 6 of the Feast, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. After the Great Prokimenon - 5 readings of the Feast and, if Sunday evening, then after "And that we may be accounted worthy" the usual Paschal Gospel is read. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. At the Litya: stichera of the Feast, and at the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion, Glory: of the Feast, Both now: "It is the Day of Resurrection" and, after "Christ is risen" (3), Troparion of the Feast three times with the blessing of loaves. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. Instead of "Blessed be the name of the Lord" - "Christ is risen" (3) and Psalm "I will bless the Lord"[^415]. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. At the beginning, after the ending of the verses "Let God arise", we do not say Glory, and now: Troparion "Christ is risen", but Glory: Troparion of the Annunciation, Both now: the same. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. After the litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord" - Polyeleos with "Great Doxology", Post-Polyeleos Sessional hymn, Gradual of Tone 4, 1st antiphon and everything else up to the canon - according to the general rule of a feast of the Theotokos on Sunday, only "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" (3) and Ps. 50 are not taken. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 3. Paschal Canon with irmos on 6 and of the Feast with irmos on 8. Katavasia of the Feast. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Resurrection and Sessional hymn of the Feast, and other things, as in the previous case on p. 271. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 4. At "The Praises" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Triodion, also stichera of the Resurrection with Glory: of the Annunciation, Both now: "It is the Day of Resurrection" and, after "Christ is risen" (3), Glory, and now: Troparion of the Annunciation, then two litanies and Paschal Dismissal with commemoration of the Annunciation. (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.3 25 March: Annunciation to the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1. On one of the fast days of Great Lent. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. On Saturday of the 3rd and 4th weeks of Lent. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 3. On the 3rd Sunday of Lent (Veneration of the Cross). (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 4. On Wednesday of the 4th week of Lent. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 5. On the 4th and 5th Sundays of Lent. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 6. On Thursday of the 5th week of Lent (of the Great Canon). (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 7. On Akathist Saturday. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 of the Triodion, 4 of the Feast and 3 of the Archangel, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Everything else - according to the Lenten rule of the Triodion on Sunday evening, only at the Aposticha - Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. Canons 2: of the Feast with irmos on 6 and of the Archangel on 6. Katavasia of the Feast[^417]; if there be a three-ode canon, then the canon of the Feast together and of the Archangel with irmos on 6 and the three-ode canon on 8. Katavasia - irmos of the Triodion. After the 3rd ode - sessional hymn and after the 6th - kontakion of the Archangel. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. At "The Praises": 4 stichera of the Archangel, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: of the Archangel, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. After the kathismata: Sessional hymn of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 3. Canons 2: of the Feast on 8 and of the Archangel on 6. Katavasia of the Feast (the four-ode canon is not sung here, but is sung before that, at Compline)[^418]. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Archangel and his Sessional hymn, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable" - refrain and irmos of the Feast; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast; Glory: of the Archangel, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 4. Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Archangel, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 5. After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Archangel, Glory and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. Troparion of the Feast and of the Archangel, Glory: Kontakion of the Archangel, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn - of the Feast; Apostle-Gospel - of the day and of the Feast; instead of "It is truly meet" - Heirmos of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 3. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. The canon of the feast is sung before the canon of the Cross. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Stichera of the Praises - 9: 3 Resurrectional, 2 of the Cross and 3 of the Feast with their refrains, and also the Idiomelon of the Triodion with its refrain; Glory: the same, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia, Gospel and Communion Hymn - first of the Cross, and afterwards - of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Instead of "It is truly meet" - "In Thee rejoiceth". (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 4. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 of the Feast, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. After the Entrance - 2 readings of the Triodion with their prokeimena and the first 2 of the 5 of the Feast that are given at yesterday's Vespers, and also Small Litany with the exclamation of the Trisagion, and from here according to the order the Service of Chrysostom. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. Canons 2 on 10: of the Feast on 6 and of the Cross on 4, and where there is a three-ode canon, there will be 4 canons on 14, that is of the Feast on 4, of the Cross on 2 and 2 three-ode canons on 8; also katavasia - irmos of the three-ode canon[^419]. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast and Sessional hymn of the Cross, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Cross; at the 9th - "More honorable"; after the 9th - Triadikon Exaposteilarion. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. At "The Praises" and at the Aposticha: stichera of the Triodion, only at "The Praises" - Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 5. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. Before the canon we sing "Open to me the doors". (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Canons 4: Resurrectional on 4, of the Triodion on 4 and of the Feast together with the Archangel on 6. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Archangel, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 3. At "The Praises", after 8 stichera Resurrectional and of the Triodion there will be also the 9th Idiomelon of the Triodion with the refrain "Arise" and Glory: the same, and the Gospel stichera - after the dismissal of Matins. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 6. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 7. APODOSIS OF THE ANNUNCIATION ON SATURDAY OF THE 5TH WEEK OF LENT (AKATHIST SATURDAY) (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. At "Lord, I have cried" - stichera on 10: Idiomelon of the day twice and 8 of the Feast, Glory: of the Akathist, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. After the Entrance - 2 readings of the Triodion with their prokeimena and 2 readings of the Feast, and immediately Small Litany with exclamation of the Trisagion, Trisagion and according to the order the Liturgy of Chrysostom, at which - everything to the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Stichera of the Praises of the Akathist on 4, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 3. After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1. Troparion of the Feast, Glory, and now: Kontakion of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 2. Everything else - to the Mother of God, of Akathist Saturday, only Apostle-Gospel - first of the day, and afterwards - of Akathist Saturday; and instead of "It is truly meet" - Heirmos of the Feast. (under ### 3.7.4 26 March: Apodosis of the Annunciation and Synaxis of the Holy Archangel Gabriel)
  H5: ##### 1.	On Great Friday, Great Saturday and on the very day of the Resurrection. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	On one of the days of Bright Week. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Sunday of the Apostle Thomas. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Sundays of the Myrrh-bearers, of the Paralytic and of the Samaritan Woman. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Mid-Pentecost Wednesday. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 6.	On one of the weekdays from the Sunday of the Apostle Thomas to the Ascension. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1. SAINT GREAT MARTYR GEORGE (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2. SAINT GREAT MARTYR GEORGE (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried": 4 Resurrectional stichera of the Octoechos of the current tone; Glory: to the Saint, Both now: Theotokion of Small Vespers of the Octoechos, according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon Great of the day. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Aposticha: one Resurrectional stichera of the Octoechos, also stichera of the Saint of Great Vespers, only Both now: to the Resurrection "It is the Day of Resurrection", "Christ is risen" (3) and immediately the Troparion of the Saint; Glory, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Saint; "Have mercy on us, O God" and dismissal. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 of the Triodion and 6 of the Saint, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: 1st Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the Great Prokimenon of the Triodion - 3 readings to the Saint, and if on the very day of the Resurrection, then the Priest reads the Gospel of the Resurrection from "And that we may be accounted worthy". (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Litya: stichera of the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Aposticha: one Resurrectional stichera, also stichera of the Resurrection; and after the stichera of the Resurrection there will be the Doxastikon of the Saint, Both now: "It is the Day of Resurrection" and, after "Christ is risen" (3) we sing the Troparion of the Saint twice and "Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos" once. Blessing of loaves, "Christ is risen" (3) and "Blessed be the name of the Lord". (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	At the beginning, after the 4 verses "Let God arise" and others - Glory: Troparion to the Saint, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Troparion of the Saint. Litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord" and immediately Polyeleos; up to the canon - everything to the Saint, only after the 50th Psalm - "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" (3). (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canon of the Resurrection with irmos on 8 and of the Saint on 6. Katavasia of Pascha. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of Pascha, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Saint and "Jesus having risen from the grave"; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Resurrection, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Resurrection. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises: 3 of the Triodion, 3 of the Saint, also stichera of the Resurrection with their refrains. Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Resurrection "It is the Day of Resurrection", and, after "Christ is risen" (3) - Glory: Troparion of the Saint, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Saint, two litanies and dismissal. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion "Christ is risen" (3) and of the Saint, Glory: His [Kontakion], Both now: to the Resurrection. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - of the day and of the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3. SAINT GREAT MARTYR GEORGE (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried": stichera to the Saint, only Doxastikon - to the Saint; at the Aposticha: stichera of the Saint of Great Vespers, only Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Troparion to the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Feast and 4 of the Saint, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	After "The Lord is King" - 3 readings of the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya: stichera of the Feast and of the Saint, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 5.	Aposticha of the Feast; Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Feast twice and of the Saint once. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - only to the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 14: of the Feast on 8 and of the Saint on 6. Katavasia of Pascha. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th "More honorable" we do not sing[^423]; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Exaposteilarion of the Feast; Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 7: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Saint, and also idiomelon of the Saint with His refrain; Glory: of the Feast, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast, and after the litanies - dismissal, Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion of the Feast and to the Saint, Glory: Kontakion to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first to the Feast, and afterwards - to the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4. SAINT GREAT MARTYR GEORGE (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Triodion and 4 of the Saint, Glory: of the Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	After "The Lord is King" - 3 readings of the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya: stichera of the Saint, only Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the Aposticha: one Resurrectional stichera and stichera of Pascha, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Triodion. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion "Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos" twice and of the Saint once. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord", if the Sunday of the Myrrh-bearers, Troparion Resurrectional once, "The noble Joseph" once, Glory: of George, Both now: to the Myrrh-bearing women. In other Sundays: Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion according to the tone of the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon is taken according to the general rule of a Saint with Polyeleos on Sunday, only "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" - three times. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with Theotokion on 6, that is Resurrectional on 4 and Theotokion on 2, of the Saint on 4 and of the Triodion on 4. (Since in the Canon of the Triodion there are many troparia, of which the first are - Resurrectional, and some - festal, that is of the Myrrh-bearers or of Arimathea, the penultimate - Triadikon, and the last - Theotokion, one Resurrectional and one of the Feast is taken, then the Triadikon and Theotokion). Katavasia of Pascha. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th "More honorable" we do not sing, but to the troparia of the Canon of the Resurrection we sing the refrain of the Resurrection as on Pascha; to the troparia of the Saint we sing the usual refrain of the Saint; to the troparia of the Triodion - "Glory to Thee, O Lord, for Thy holy Resurrection"[^424], except "Glory" to the penultimate and "Both now" to the last, as usual; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: to the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Saint with His refrains, Glory: of the Feast, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, the second of the two usual ones, and two litanies, and after the dismissal - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)

  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the canon everything - according to the general rule of a Saint with Polyeleos in the middle of a Feast, only the first two sessional hymns will be of the Feast, and after the Polyeleos all three sessional hymns - of the Saint; after the Gospel - "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" once[^427]. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 3 making 14: first of the Feast on 6, of the Saint on 4 and second of the Feast on 4. Katavasia - irmos of the last canon. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th "More honorable" we do not sing[^428]; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Saint, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)

  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the Feast, and afterwards - of the Saint. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 6. SAINT GREAT MARTYR GEORGE (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" and at the Aposticha: stichera to the Saint, only Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.8.1 23 April: Holy Great-Martyr George)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the end, in the middle of the feast of the Sunday of the Apostle Thomas and of Mid-Pentecost: Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast. In the week of the Myrrh-bearers: Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: "The noble Joseph"; in the weeks of the Paralytic and of the Samaritan Woman (with the exception of Mid-Pentecost): Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: Theotokion from the Resurrectional ones, according to the tone of the Saint.   H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.9.2 7 May: Appearance of the Sign of the Precious Cross in the sky in Jerusalem)

  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Troparion of the Cross, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.2 7 May: Appearance of the Sign of the Precious Cross in the sky in Jerusalem)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory, and now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.9.2 7 May: Appearance of the Sign of the Precious Cross in the sky in Jerusalem)

  H5: ##### 2.	Apostle-Gospel of the day and of the Cross. Everything else - of the Feast and of the Cross[^431]. (under ### 3.9.2 7 May: Appearance of the Sign of the Precious Cross in the sky in Jerusalem)

  H5: ##### 3. JOHN THE THEOLOGIAN (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 4. JOHN THE THEOLOGIAN (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)

  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Feast and 6 of the Apostle, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)

  H5: ##### 2.	After the 1st poetic session: Resurrectional Sessional hymn; after the 2nd - Sessional hymn of the Blind Man; after the Polyeleos with "Great Doxology" - all three sessional hymns of the Apostle; and everything else up to the canon - to the Apostle, only before the 50th Psalm - "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" once.   H5: ##### 4.	At "The Praises" stichera of the Apostle on 4, and also Resurrectional stichera, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Resurrection and "Christ is risen" once. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)

  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the Resurrection and of the Apostle. Apostle-Gospel of the day and of the Apostle. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 5. JOHN THE THEOLOGIAN ON ASCENSION THURSDAY (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)

  H5: ##### 2.	At the end: Troparion of the Apostle, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Feast and 4 of the Apostle, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day, 3 readings of the Feast and 3 - of the Theologian. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the Litya: stichera of the Feast and of the Apostle, Glory: of the Theologian, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 5.	Stichera of the Feast, only Doxastikon of the Apostle. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion of the Feast twice and of the Apostle once. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - only of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: of the Feast first with irmos on 6, of the Apostle on 4 and of the Feast second on 4. Katavasia sequential. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Apostle, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th "More honorable" we do not sing, but we sing the refrain of the Feast; to the troparia of the Apostle "Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian, pray to God for us"; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Apostle, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Apostle, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion of the Feast and of the Apostle, Glory: Kontakion of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the Feast, afterwards - of the Apostle. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 6. JOHN THE THEOLOGIAN IN THE SUNDAY OF THE HOLY FATHERS (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - Resurrectional stichera, as usual, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: Theotokion of the Resurrectional stichera, which is at "Lord, I have cried" of Small Vespers, according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Aposticha: Resurrectional stichera, then stichera of the Apostle of Great Vespers with his refrains, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: Theotokion of the Resurrectional Aposticha of Small Vespers, according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 3.	Resurrectional Troparion and of the Fathers, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Ascension[^438]. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 4 of the Fathers and 3 of the Apostle[^433], Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon of the day, 3 readings of the Fathers and 3 - of the Apostle. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Litya: Doxastikon of the Fathers and stichera of the Apostle, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Ascension. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha Resurrectional, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Ascension. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the blessing of loaves: Troparion "Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos" twice and of the Apostle once. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion once and of the Fathers, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion, according to the tone of the Apostle[^434]. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual, only, instead of "The Blameless", there will be Polyeleos with "Great Doxology" and after the Hypakoe - all three Sessional hymns of the Apostle. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of the Apostle on 4 and of the Fathers on 6. Katavasia sequential. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Apostle and Sessional hymn of the Fathers, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Feast[^435]; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos to the Fathers; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and Resurrectional Exaposteilarion and of the Fathers, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: Theotokion (of the Resurrectional Exaposteilarion)[^436]. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 3 Resurrectional, 2 of the Fathers and 3 of the Apostle with Doxastikon, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Dismissal of Matins - Glory, and now: Gospel Stichera. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion, of the Fathers and of the Apostle; Kontakion of the Fathers, Glory: of the Apostle, Both now: of the Ascension[^438]. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel - first of the Fathers, and then of the Theologian; Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord" and of the Theologian. (under ### 3.9.3 8 May: Holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian)
  H5: ##### 1.	On one of the weekdays of Pentecost (under ### 3.9.5 10 May: Holy Apostle Simon)
