  H4: #### RULE FOR THE 7th and 11th CASES (under ### 25 MAY)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 of the Feast and 4 of the Saint, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 3.	Prokimenon of the day and 3 readings to the Saint. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Feast, only Doxastikon - to the Saint. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 5.	Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Feast twice, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 2.	First two sessional hymns of the Feast, after the Polyeleos with "Great Doxology" - two sessional hymns of the Saint, then Glory: his Post-Polyeleos Sessional hymn, Both now: of the Feast. Everything else up to the canon - to the Saint. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: first of the Feast with irmos on 6, of the Saint on 4 and second of the Feast on 4. Sequential Katavasia. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Feast; at the 9th "More honorable" we do not sing, but we sing the refrain of the Feast; to the troparia of the Saint we sing his usual refrain; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Feast, Glory: to the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Feast and 3 of the Saint, with two of his refrains; Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion of the Feast and of the Saint, Glory: Kontakion of the Saint, Both now: of the Feast. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn of the Feast and of the Saint. Apostle-Gospel of the day and of the Saint. (under ### 25 MAY)
  H5: ##### 1.	On one of the weekdays of Pentecost (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Friday of the Apodosis of the Ascension (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 3.	On Saturday of Pentecost (of Souls) (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 4.	On the Sunday of Pentecost (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 5.	On Monday of Pentecost (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 6.	On Saturday of the Apodosis of Pentecost (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 7.	On the Sunday of All Saints (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 8.	On Thursday of the Eucharist (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 9.	On the Sunday of the Eucharist (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 10.	On the Apodosis of the Feast of the Eucharist (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 11.	On the Co-suffering of the Most Holy Theotokos (under ### 3.10.1 11 June: Holy Apostles Bartholomew and Barnabas)
  H5: ##### 1.	At Matins, before their canon, the Canon to the Theotokos is not taken, but only their two canons on 14, irmos of the first canon - twice, then troparia of both canons on 12, that is of one on 6 and of the other on 6. (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Liturgy the Apostle-Gospel is read only of the Saint, and of the day is read on the previous day just as on Feasts of the Lord and of the Theotokos. (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H5: ##### 3.	Their service is not celebrated in one day, but in two; however, on the second day their service - is of the daily type and combined with the service of the Octoechos and of the sequential saint so, that the Octoechos takes the first place, the Forerunner or the Chiefs [of the Apostles] - the second, according to the rule of a Saint on 4, and the sequential saint - the third. It is also proper to take their Troparion and Kontakion of the day at the Hours and at the Liturgy. (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H4: #### RULE FOR 24 AND 29 JUNE (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H5: ##### 1.	In the middle of the Feast of the Eucharist. (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Sunday, to which the Feast of the Eucharist is transferred. (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the Apodosis of the Feast of the Eucharist. (under ### 3.10.3 29 June: The Holy Pre-eminent Apostles Peter and Paul)
  H5: ##### 1. On weekdays (under ### 3.11.1 2 July: Deposition of the Precious Robe of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 2. On Sunday (under ### 3.11.1 2 July: Deposition of the Precious Robe of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE OF THE DEPOSITION OF THE PRECIOUS ROBE (under ### 3.11.1 2 July: Deposition of the Precious Robe of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.11.4 25 July: Dormition of St. Anne, mother of the Most Holy Theotokos)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Feast of the Procession of the Precious Wood on 1 August has a Forefeast, which is - today, that is 31 July, but it has no Afterfeast. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2.	Although it has a Forefeast, however, of small type, that is without Polyeleos, and the service of the Cross does not exclude the service of the sequential saint, nor of the Octoechos, neither in the Forefeast, nor on the Feast, nor on Sunday, nor on a weekday. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 3.	In the Forefeast and on the Feast the first place is occupied by the service of the Octoechos, the second - of the Cross, the third - of the Saint. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 4.	The service of the Forefeast has the composition of a service of a Saint on 4 and the service of St. Eudocimus also has the appearance of a Saint on 4, since these two services are combined with the service of the Octoechos both on weekdays, and on Sunday, then the order on weekdays will be according to the general rule of two saints, falling with the Octoechos on weekdays, only the stichera at "Lord, I have cried" and at the Aposticha we end with the stichera of the Forefeast, except Friday evening, when the stichera at "Lord, I have cried" we end with the 1st Theotokion of the tone. On Sunday the order will be according to the general rule of a Forefeast with one Saint without Polyeleos with Octoechos on Sunday. Likewise also troparia we do not end with the usual Theotokion, but with the Troparion of the Forefeast, according to the general rule of a Forefeast. Therefore, there will be: (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H4: #### RULE OF THE FOREFEAST (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1. On weekdays (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Cross and 3 of the Saint, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Cross (On Friday evening - Glory: of the Cross, Both now: Dogmatikon of the tone). (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Aposticha: stichera of the Octoechos, Glory, and now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the end: Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord" - Troparion of the Cross, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2.	Both Sessional hymns of the Octoechos. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: one of the Octoechos on 6, of the Cross on 4 and of the Saint on 4. Katavasia after the 3rd, 6th, 8th and 9th odes - irmos of the last canon. After the 3rd ode - Sessional hymn of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Cross[^452]; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Saint, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Octoechos, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 4.	Aposticha of the Octoechos, Glory: of the Saint, Both now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 5.	At the end: Troparion of the Saint, Glory, and now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparia: of the Cross, of the Martyrs and Glory, and now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon of the Cross and of the Martyrs. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 3.	Apostle and Gospel of the Cross and of the Martyrs. (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 4.	Communion Hymn "The light of Thy countenance" ("Znamenovasya") and of the Martyrs "Rejoice in the Lord" ("Raduitesya"). (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2. On Sunday (under ### 3.11.5 31 July: Forefeast of the Procession of the Precious Cross)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1. On weekdays (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the Dismissal of Vespers the Bringing Out of the Precious Cross from the sacristy to the Holy Table takes place, according to the rule of 14 September, here on p. 121-122. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2.	At Matins: Canon of the Octoechos on 4, of the Cross on 6 and of the Saints on 4. Katavasia after each ode - sequential "The Cross having been traced" (14 September). After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos and Sessional hymn of the Saints, only Both now: of the Cross; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Cross; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Octoechos, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 6: 3 of the Cross and 3 of the Saints, Glory: of the Saints, Both now: of the Cross. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 4.	During the singing of the Praises the Priest, having vested in all sacred vestments (of violet color)[^455]  and having exclaimed before the steps of the Holy Table: "Glory to Thee, Who Hast shown us the light", and having censed the precious Cross, carries it out around the Holy Table through the northern doors before the Holy Doors, and after the end of the Great Doxology with the Trisagion, exclaims: "Wisdom, attend", goes before the tetrapod and, having placed the precious Cross on it, when the Choir sings the Troparion "Save, O Lord", censes it from four sides and venerates the precious Cross, everything exactly as on 14 September, here on p. 122-127. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 5.	The Blessing of Water, if it be outside Matins, is celebrated whole just as you have in the Trebnik. If it be at Matins, then after the singing of the Doxology and the Troparion "Save, O Lord", that is immediately before the Veneration, in the rite of the Blessing of Water the beginning and the end is not taken, but immediately after the Troparion "Save, O Lord" begins "That rejoice" ("Yezhe raduisya") and ends with the stichera "O Most Holy Lady, receive", not taking the litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and the rest. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 6.	The Blessing of Herbs, if there be any, usually takes place at the Liturgy after the Prayer behind the Ambo just as on the Dormition of the Most Holy Theotokos. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2. On Sunday (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1.	On Saturday evening, after the Dismissal of Vespers the Bringing Out of the Precious Cross from the sacristy to the Holy Table takes place, and at Matins, during the Great Doxology, from the Holy Table to the tetrapod, according to the rule of 14 September. However, after the exclamation "Wisdom, attend", instead of the Troparion "Save, O Lord", we sing the Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones, at which the Priest should cense the Precious Cross from four sides and immediately after this perform the veneration of the Precious Cross, singing the Troparion "Before Thy Cross we bow down", or before the veneration perform also the rite of the Blessing of Water, according to the rule given here for weekdays, no. 5. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 2.	At the Liturgy: the entire service is taken first of the Sunday, and then - of the Cross. The Service of the Saints, since it is without Polyeleos, is not taken. If someone would prefer to take it too, then Prokimena, Alleluia and Communion Hymns will be only two: to the Cross and to the Saints, and Apostle-Gospels - three: sequential, of the Cross and of the Saints[^456]. (under ### 3.12.1 1 August: Procession of the Wood of the Precious Cross)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Service of the Holy Martyr is sung at Compline, and two services remain, that is of the Dormition and of the Image. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	Although the service of the Image, as of the Lord, is greater than of the Theotokos, however, here the Theotokos one takes the first place. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 3.	The Service of the Image, according to the rule of our and Russian menaia, is on 6 and is celebrated according to the general rule of a Saint without Polyeleos on 6, with Praises and Great Doxology. It has also in the canon after each ode the sequential katavasia and, in Slavic books[^460], a separate Great Dismissal[^461]. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 4.	Although the Service of the Image - is without Polyeleos, nevertheless, at the Liturgy we do not omit it, as other services of Saints without Polyeleos of feasts of the Lord and of the Theotokos. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H4: #### RULE (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 1. On weekdays (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 1.	There is no Kathisma, in view of the All-Night Vigil that has taken place. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Dormition and 3 of the Image, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. On Friday evening - Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha of the Dormition, only Doxastikon - of the Image. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Troparion of the Image, Glory, and now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 5.	Separate Dismissal. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Image twice, Glory, and now: of the Feast. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the first and second kathisma - Sessional hymn of the Image, Glory, and now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 making 12: of the Dormition with irmos on 6 and of the Image on 6. Katavasia after each ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Dormition and Sessional hymn of the Image, Glory, and now: of the Dormition; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Image, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Image, Glory, and now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 4.	At "The Praises" - 4 stichera of the Image, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 5.	Great Doxology and after the Trisagion - Troparion of the Image, Glory, and now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great separate Dismissal. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion of the Image and of the Dormition; Glory: Kontakion of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon, Alleluia and Communion Hymn - only of the Image, Apostle and Gospel - of the Image and of the day. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2. On Sunday (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	At "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Resurrectional, 3 of the Dormition and 4 of the Image; Glory: of the Image, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 3.	At the Aposticha: Resurrectional stichera, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Resurrectional Troparion, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 5.	Separate Dismissal. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 1.	At "God is the Lord": Resurrectional Troparion twice, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Resurrectional, as usual. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 making 14: Resurrectional with irmos on 4, of the Dormition on 4 and of the Image on 6. Sequential Katavasia. After the 3rd ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Dormition and Sessional hymn of the Image, Glory, and now: of the Dormition; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Image; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God", also Resurrectional Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Resurrectional and 4 of the Image, with two of its refrains, Glory: Gospel Stichera, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Resurrectional Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 6.	Dismissal, as at Vespers. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 1.	Resurrectional Troparion, of the Image and of the Dormition; Resurrectional Kontakion, Glory: of the Image, Both now: of the Dormition. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - first of the Sunday, then - of the Image. (under ### 3.12.4 16 August: Image Not-Made-By-Hands)

  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 2.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 7 of the Octoechos and 3 of the Triodion, that is, the two given at "Lord, I have cried" and the one from the Doxastikon of the Litiya; Glory: Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the current tone (Dogmatikon). (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha of the Sunday Octoechos, Glory: Triodion, Both now: Theotokion of the Sunday Aposticha according to the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory, Both now: Sunday Theotokion according to its tone. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 1.	On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: Sunday Theotokion according to its tone. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon is of the Sunday, as usual, only after the 50th Psalm, instead of Glory: "Through the prayers of the holy apostles" and the rest, at all odes of the Sundays of the Triodion we sing: Glory: "Open to me the doors", Both now: "Lead me on the paths of salvation", and after the verse "Have mercy on me, O God", the sticheron: "Thinking upon the multitude". (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 4 [making] 14: Octoechos Sunday with heirmos on 4, Cross-Resurrection on 2, Theotokos on 2, and Triodion on 6. Katavasia: The sequential "I shall open". After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn of the Triodion; after the 6th - Kontakion and Ikos of the Triodion; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" and the Sunday Exaposteilarion, Glory: Triodion, Both now: Its Theotokion. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 of the Octoechos and 4 of the Triodion with two Sunday verses, Glory: Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 5.	Everything else to the end of Matins - as usual. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the dismissal of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Sticheron. (Thus we sing on all Sundays until the Sunday of All Saints, as the Typikon prescribes. However, on the Sunday of All Saints itself and on the Sunday of the Myrrh-bearers, the Typikon places the Gospel Sticheron in its proper place). (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the Entrance: Sunday Troparion, Glory: Kontakion of the Triodion, Both now: Usual Theotokion, as outside the Triodion period[^466]. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 2.	Prokimenon and Alleluia of the Sunday tone. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 3.	Epistle and Gospel of the Sunday. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 4.	Communion Hymn "Praise the Lord". (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 1.	On the other six days of this week, the Triodion has nothing, and it yields place to the Octoechos. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 2.	During this week, the Typikon permits meat even on Wednesday and Friday[^467]. (under ### 4.1.1 Sunday of the Publican and Pharisee)
  H5: ##### 1.	The service of the saint of the day is transferred to the preceding Compline or to another day, and the Saturday service of the Octoechos and Triodion remains. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	At Vespers, instead of the Prokimenon, and at Matins, instead of "God is the Lord", we sing "Alleluia". (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathisma proper. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 Martyria of the Octoechos of the current tone and 3 of the Triodion, Glory: Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone (Dogmatikon). (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Instead of the Prokimenon of the day, we sing Alleluia in Tone 8 with two verses for the dead. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Aposticha of the Octoechos of the current tone, Glory: Triodion, Both now: also Triodion. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 6.	At the end: Troparion for the dead ("By the depth of wisdom"), Glory, Both now: its Theotokion "Thee as a wall and haven". (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	We do not take the Canon of the Theotokos, as was prescribed for the Sunday of the Prodigal Son. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion "With the saints give rest". (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 2.	Instead of "God is the Lord", we sing Alleluia in Tone 8 with three verses for the dead, as found at the end of the Horologion[^471]. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 3.	Troparion for the dead twice, Glory, Both now: its Theotokion. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 4.	Kathisma 16 proper, Small Litany and Sessional Hymn of the Octoechos of the current tone. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Everything else up to the canon, as at the Parastas, that is, Kathisma 17 ("The Blameless") with its verses, divided into two stations, the first of which concludes with the litany for the dead, and the second - with the troparia for the dead "The Choir of the saints", then we sing the litany for the dead, and at the end - the Sessional Hymn for the dead and Psalm 50. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 6.	Canons 2 [making] 14: Temple on 6 and Triodion on 8. Katavasia after each ode - Heirmos of the Triodion canon. After the 3rd Ode - usual litany and Sessional Hymn of the Triodion; after the 6th - usual litany and Kontakion and Ikos of the Triodion; after the 9th - usual litany and Exaposteilarion of the Triodion. The refrain to the troparia of the temple canon will be the usual one, corresponding to the form of the canon, and to the troparion of the Triodion canon - "Give rest, O Lord, to the souls of Thy departed servants". (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 7.	Stichera of the Praises - 4 of the Triodion, Glory, Both now: Triodion. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 8.	Small Doxology. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 9.	Aposticha of the Octoechos, Glory, Both now: Triodion. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 10.	Troparion and Glory, Both now: Theotokion for the dead, as at Vespers. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 11.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and dismissal for the dead and "In blessed falling asleep", as at Vespers. (under ### 4.1.3 Meatfare, or Soul Saturday)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Synod of Lviv permits meat during this week, except on Monday, Wednesday, and Friday, on the condition of [reciting] 5 "Our Father" and 5 "Virgin Theotokos" for the laity and Psalm 50 for the clergy. On the other three days, we use dairy products. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	The divine services of this week approach the Lenten [order], however, gradually, for Monday, Tuesday, and Thursday have only certain parts in the Triodion, while all other parts are filled by the Octoechos and Menaion. Wednesday and Friday have in the Triodion a fuller order of Lenten character with prostrations[^472]. Saturday and Sunday, although they have a full service in the Triodion, nevertheless are not of Lenten form, as are all Saturdays and Sundays of Great Lent. The parts that Monday, Tuesday, and Thursday have in the Triodion are as follows: 1. Aposticha at Vespers and at Matins. 2. 2nd Sessional Hymn at Matins before the Canon. 3. Canon of the Triodion, usually double, that is, with two heirmoi. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	Instead of the 2nd Sessional Hymn of the Octoechos, it will be - of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons: where there is no triodion [three-ode canon], there will be two of the Octoechos and one of the saint, as usual; but where there is a triodion, both canons of the Octoechos are omitted and the canon of the saint is sung alone or two together (if there are two) with heirmos on 6[^475] and both canons of the triodion on 8[^476]. The Katavasia will be after Odes 3, 6, 8, and 9, and also where there is a triodion, always the heirmos of the last canon. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	Aposticha of the Triodion with the usual verses of the Horologion. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the troparia after "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart", everything - according to the general rubric for a saint without Polyeleos on weekdays, however, with prostrations. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" with the Trisagion - 3 troparia, that is, "Virgin Theotokos", Glory: To the Baptist, Both now: "Intercede", each with a prostration at the end, and "Beneath Thy tender mercy", without a prostration[^478]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	From "Lord, have mercy" (40) to the end, everything - as in the Horologion; however, at the last Trisagion and at "Blessed be the name of the Lord", which must be said before the psalm "I will bless the Lord", there will be three prostrations (small); and at the prayer of St. Ephrem - 16 prostrations[^479], that is, 3 great, 12 small, and again one great, as at the daily Midnight Office after "Thou Who at all times"[^480]. Dismissal with the usual commemorations of the service of the day and the saint. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	The prostrations we make at the three first troparia, according to the Typikon - are not great, but small, although due to ignorance of the rubric, or due to excessive piety, great ones are made among us. For here this prostration is called simply metanoia, and with the addition megale [great] it is given only at the prayer of St. Ephrem and never at the end of Vespers, after the litany "Have mercy on us, O God". Likewise at the Great Canon the rubric does not prescribe great prostrations, but simply metanoias. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	Neither Greek nor Slavonic rubrics give the form of the great or small prostration. However, according to custom it will be thus: he who makes a great prostration falls on his knees and touches the ground with his forehead; he who makes a small prostration does not fall on his knees, nor touches the ground with his forehead, but only crossing himself reaches the ground with his right hand, or only to the knees as the Synod of Lviv prescribes in Title V "On the Holy Liturgy", in the prayers before the iconostasis before the Proskomedia[^481]. We reach the ground with the right hand when we bow to the Holy Mysteries; only to the knees when we bow to holy icons, or at various supplications. However, our people in these parts do not have the custom, when bowing, to extend hands to the ground, or to the knees, but only crossing themselves to bow low before the Holy Mysteries, and higher - in other circumstances. We suppose, however, that at small Lenten prostrations we should reach the knees with the hand, for throughout the whole year at the Trisagion and at "O come, let us worship" we make prostrations, not reaching the knees with the hand[^482]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the Creed, the prostrations at the supplicatory verses take place thus: The 1st choir begins: "O All-holy Lady Theotokos, pray to God for us", and the 2nd Choir makes a prostration, then rises and responds, and the 1st Choir makes a prostration. So also at all other supplicatory verses. And when we say "O God, be merciful to me, a sinner" (3), both choirs sing together and make three prostrations (Greek Triodion, Monday of the 1st week of Lent). (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "It is truly meet" we do not sing the Troparion "O Lord of Hosts, be with us" with its verses, but the usual troparia, as at Small Compline, that is: of the weekday, of the Temple, and "O God of our fathers" with the other three[^485]; and on the days of Great Lent we sing "O Lord of Hosts, be with us". (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the end of the litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord", everything - as usual for every day. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	Instead of "God is the Lord" we sing Alleluia in the current tone of the Octoechos and the Trinity Hymns of the tone. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	Two Kathismata with the usual prostrations at "Glory"; after the 1st Kathisma - Sessional Hymn of the Octoechos; after the 2nd - Sessional Hymn of the Triodion, then Psalm 50 and immediately - Canon[^491]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons 3 [making] 14: first of the Octoechos (the second of the Octoechos is not taken) with heirmos on 6, of the Menaion of the saint on 4, and of the Triodion on 4. In those odes where the triodion [three-ode canon] occurs, we sing the canon of the Triodion on 6 and both canons of the triodion together on 8, and one more separate troparion with the refrain "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"[^572][^561], and instead of the Katavasia it will be the heirmos of the second canon of the triodion. In the odes in which there is no triodion, we suppose that the Katavasia will be the heirmos of the last canon, that is, of the Triodion, after each ode, as given in the Triodion on Cheesefare Friday, for these two days have a common rubric, although for Wednesday it is given imprecisely. The Canon of the Octoechos and Menaion in these odes, according to the rubric of the Greek Triodia, is omitted[^492]. The rubrics of the Slavs, for Whom the Greek length is not enough, prescribe transferring the odes of the Menaion canon to the preceding ones, in which the triodion has no place, and they sing on Wednesday the 1st ode of the Octoechos with heirmos on 4, the 1st and 3rd odes of the Menaion (without Theotokia) on 6 and the Triodion on 4. On Friday - the 4th ode of the Octoechos on 4, the 4th and 5th odes of the Menaion on 6 and the Triodion on 4. Similarly, the 6th ode of the Octoechos on 4, the 6th and 8th of the Menaion on 6 and the Triodion on 4. Also the 6th ode of the Octoechos on 4, the 7th and 9th of the Menaion on 6 and the Triodion on 4. The Biblical Odes in the Psalter we do not sing[^493]. To the troparia of the canon we chant the usual refrains (on pp. 29-30 [→REF:p29-30]). After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn to the Saint, after the 6th - Kontakion to the Saint, and if there is none, then we recite the Martyricon of the Octoechos of the current tone, given at the end of the Triodion, after the Sessional Hymns of Matins. In general, in our modern Horologia Kontakia are given to all saints[^494]. After the 9th Ode - Trinity Exaposteilarion of the tone. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 5.	Before the Small Doxology the Priest exclaims: "To Thee belongs glory". (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 6.	Aposticha of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 7.	After "It is a good thing" with the Trisagion - Troparion "Standing in the temple", then "Lord, have mercy" (40) and the rest with 16 prostrations, as in the Horologion at the end of Matins. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	Three small prostrations: at "O come, let us worship", at the Trisagions, at Alleluia after the three psalms, at "How shall we call Thee"; and at the prayer of St. Ephrem - 16 usual ones[^495]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the conclusion of the three psalms with Alleluia, the troparion is sung, called also the Prokimenon, with two verses in the tone of the Prokimenon. "The appointed reader (or priest) sings in the middle of the church the Troparion 'In the morning' (at the 1st Hour, and at the others - the specific one), and we sing loudly the same and bend our knees to the ground. Exactly so at the other two verses and again the same troparion"[^496]. This bending of the knees is done three times: once - after the singing of the troparion before the verse, once - after the singing of the troparion after the 1st verse, and once - after the singing of the troparion after the 2nd verse[^497]. After singing the troparion with its verses, we say Glory, Both now: Theotokion and make three prostrations. (At the 6th Hour, immediately after the Theotokion, there will be the Troparion of the Prophecy and one reading of the Prophecy between two Prokimena in the Triodion). (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	Then we sing "Direct my steps" with 4 verses, that is: "Direct my steps" twice, "Deliver me" twice, "Make Thy face to shine" twice and "Let my mouth be filled" three times. These verses - only at the 1st Hour, and at the others - only one troparion without verses. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Trisagion, instead of the Kontakion, the Lenten Troparia are sung, which are in the Horologion. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 6.	Trisagion with "Our Father", "For Thine is the Kingdom" and "Lord, have mercy" (12). (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 7.	Final prayer: at the 1st Hour - "O Christ, the True Light", and at the others - the other one. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	Without stichera on the Beatitudes and without Epistle and Gospel. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	At each of the three "Remember us, O Lord" - one small prostration[^499]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	After 16 (or 15) prostrations at the prayer of St. Ephrem, we immediately begin Vespers, and the prayer "O All-holy Trinity" and the rest of the Typika is transferred to the end of Vespers. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	Begins from "O come, let us worship". (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	Kathisma proper. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Octoechos and 3 of the Menaion, Glory: of the Saint, if He has one, Both now: Usual Theotokion of the tone and day. (On Friday all 6 stichera - of the Triodion and the 1st Theotokion of the departing tone). (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 4.	Instead of the Prokimenon of the day we sing two Prokimena of the Triodion, and between them - one reading of the Triodion (so also on Friday). (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the 2nd Prokimenon - immediately "Vouchsafe". (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 6.	Aposticha of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 7.	After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Troparion to the Saint, Glory, Both now: Dismissal Theotokion of the tone and of the day of the week[^500]. (On Friday evening - Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the Troparion). (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 8.	Litany "Have mercy on us, O God" and 3 great prostrations, without prayer. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	Instead of the Theotokion Canon we sing the Canon for the Dead of the Octoechos of the current tone from Matins. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Fathers[^503]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	On "God is the Lord": Troparion of the Fathers twice, Glory, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the Troparion of the Fathers. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 2.	Two Kathismata with Sessional Hymns of the Triodion, Psalm 50 immediately Canon. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 2 [making] 14: of the Temple Saint with heirmos on 6 and of the Triodion of the Fathers on 8. Katavasia - Heirmos of the Canon of the Fathers. We read the usual refrains. (To the Canon of the Fathers - "Venerable Fathers, pray to God for us")[^504]. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion to the Fathers, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the [Troparion of the] Fathers. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 6.	Great Dismissal with commemoration of the Fathers. (under ### 4.1.5 Cheesefare Week)
  H5: ##### 1.	Since the five first weekdays of each week of Great Lent, that is Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, and Friday have a service of Lenten form, beginning from Sunday evening, we use sacred vestments of Lenten colors, according to the previous rubric - purple, according to the Synod of Lviv - violet. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 2.	We do not make prostrations at Vespers of these Sundays from the beginning of Vespers, but from "Vouchsafe, O Lord". We make, therefore, three small prostrations after "Vouchsafe, O Lord" and at the Trisagion following "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart"; and 3 great prostrations - at the troparia, at "Virgin Theotokos" and at the prayer of St. Ephrem, without the 12 small ones[^505]. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 3.	No Kathisma. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 4.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 Penitential of the Octoechos of the current tone, 2 given at the Aposticha of Vespers and 2 at the Aposticha of Matins (which are taken from the Octoechos and given at the end of the Triodion), 3 of the Triodion and 3 of the Menaion of the saint; Glory: of the saint, Both now: Usual Theotokion in the tone of the Doxastikon; if none, then Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Menaion. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 5.	Entrance, on account of the Great Prokimenon. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 6.	Instead of the Prokimenon of the day we sing the Great Prokimenon of the Triodion (on this Sunday, on the 2nd and 4th: "Turn not away"; on the 1st, 3rd and 5th: "Thou Hast given an inheritance"). (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 7.	After the Prokimenon there will be no litany[^508]; the Priest takes off the phelonion and closes the holy doors, and we say "Vouchsafe, O Lord" and after this we make 3 small prostrations. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 8.	Aposticha of the Triodion with the usual verses of the Horologion, only, if there be Glory: of the saint, then the Theotokion will be the usual one, in the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 9.	After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" and the Trisagion with three small prostrations we sing 3 troparia with prostrations, that is "Virgin Theotokos" and a prostration, Glory: "O Baptizer of Christ" and a prostration; Both now: "Intercede for us" and a prostration, and then "Beneath Thy tender mercy" without a prostration. (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 4.1.6 Cheesefare Sunday)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the end of the litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord", everything, just as on every day. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Instead of "God is the Lord" we sing Alleluia in the sequential tone of the Octoechos. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	We sing three Trinity Hymns of the current tone, given in the Horologion, three for each tone. They are all sung three times with the refrain "Glory" to the second, and "Both now" - to the third. At each first one we make a commemoration of the weekday service, at the second - a commemoration of all saints, at the third - a commemoration of the Theotokos. However, since the weekday service of the week changes daily, and the troparia are sung the same each day of the week (except Saturdays and Sundays, on which, because they are not Lenten, the Trinity Hymns are not sung), at the end of each first Trinity Hymn a colon is given, which signifies that the troparion must be completed with the commemoration of the weekday service, the form of which is indicated at the end of the 3rd troparion of Tone 1. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4. Three Kathismata with their Sessional Hymns, the Sessional Hymns of which, sung after the 1st Kathisma, are of the Octoechos of the current tone, given also at the end of the Triodion. The Sessional Hymns sung after the 2nd and 3rd Kathismata are of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5. Psalm 50 and the first petition of the Great Litany "Save, O God", and after the exclamation "Through the mercy and compassion" immediately - the Canon. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6. Odes and Canons. The Biblical Odes, given at the end of the Psalter, are intertwined with the odes of the canon, as given: before the ode of the canon the corresponding ode of the Psalter is recited, leaving its last verses for refrains to the troparia of the canon, in such quantity as there will be troparia in the ode of the canon, including the heirmos, except two, for to the penultimate troparion we refrain "Glory", and to the last - "Both now". The 2nd Ode is recited in full before the ode of the canon, and to the troparia of its canon we refrain: "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee". According to our ancient custom, the Odes of the Psalter are recited only in those odes in which the triodion occurs, and in the other odes we refrain the usual refrains to the troparia of the canon. The Greek Typikon prescribes singing the Odes of the Psalter in the tone of the Menaion canon, but now we do not even know what their melody was. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Before the Small Doxology the Priest exclaims: "To Thee belongs glory" and the rest, and at "Glory to God in the highest" we make three prostrations. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Aposticha of the Triodion, Glory: of the saint, if there be, Both now: Usual Theotokion in the tone of "Glory". (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	We make 3 small prostrations: at "O come, let us worship", at each Trisagion, at "Alleluia", which is after the three psalms, and at "Alleluia" after each antiphon; at each of the three singings of the troparion with two verses we bend the knees[^517] and, after the Theotokion, - 3 prostrations, and at the prayer of St. Ephrem - 16 usual ones. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the three psalms with "Alleluia" - Kathisma proper according to the Lenten marker of the Psalter, except the 1st Hour on Monday and the 1st and 9th Hours on Friday, for then there will be no Kathisma. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Troparion ("In the morning" at the 1st Hour, and at the others - the other one specific to the Hour) with two verses and with the bending of knees at each of the three singings of the troparion, then Glory, Both now: Theotokion ("What shall we call Thee" at the 1st, and at the others - the other, specific to the Hour). (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	Selected verses from the Holy Scripture of the Old Testament, that is: at the 1st Hour - "Direct my steps", at the 3rd - "Blessed is the Lord God", at the 6th Hour - "Let Thy compassions come quickly", at the 9th - "Forsake us not". At the 1st Hour "Direct my steps" is divided into 4 verses, of which we sing the first 3 twice, and the 4th - three times. At the 6th Hour between the Theotokion and the Old Testament verses we sing the Troparion of the Prophecy of the Triodion and one prophecy between two Prokimena there. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the Trisagion, instead of the Kontakion, we read three Lenten troparia, according to the order of the Hours in the Horologion, adding to the 2nd "Glory", and to the 3rd - "Both now". (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 7.	Trisagion with 3 prostrations, and after "Our Father" - the exclamation "For Thine is the Kingdom" and "Lord, have mercy" (12). (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 8.	Final prayer of the Hour and "Glory to Thee, O Christ God", dismissal of the weekday with the commemoration of the saint at the 1st Hour, and at the other Hours, since they are combined with the Typika, there will be no dismissal, but it will be at the end of the Typika. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	In the archcathedral temple the Hours 3rd, 6th, 9th, Typika and Vespers are celebrated together, beginning at 11 o'clock in the morning, and in the afternoon, at the usual time of Vespers, - Great Compline[^518]. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	The last prayer of the 9th Hour "O Master Lord Jesus Christ" is transferred to the Typika, to the place of the prayer "O All-holy Trinity", and the prayer "O All-holy Trinity" with the psalm following it is transferred, according to the rubric of our Pochaiv Triodion, to the end of Vespers[^519]. And on Wednesday and Friday, since Vespers will be with the Presanctified, "O All-holy Trinity" cannot be transferred to the end of Vespers, for it ends with the Liturgy and therefore remains in its place at the Typika, exactly so the prayer "O Master Lord Jesus Christ" remains in its place at the 9th Hour. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	Kontakia, which are in the Horologion. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6.	Trisagion with 3 prostrations and the rest with "Our Father" and "Lord, have mercy" (12). (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 7.	Priest - prayer of the 9th Hour "O Master Lord Jesus Christ". (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 8.	Dismissal of the day with the commemoration of the saint. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Priest: "Blessed is our God"; choir: "O come, let us worship" three times with prostrations. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the pre-introductory psalm, at Alleluia, - 3 prostrations. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Triodion and 3 of the Menaion, Glory, Both now: Theotokion of the Menaion. If there is a Doxastikon of the saint, then Theotokion - usual, in the tone of the Doxastikon. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	Prokimenon of the Triodion and one reading of the Triodion, and afterwards also another Prokimenon and second reading of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the 2nd reading immediately - "Vouchsafe" and 3 prostrations. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 7.	Aposticha of the Triodion with the usual verses of the Horologion. If there is a Doxastikon of the saint, then we select the Theotokion in His tone, as on "Lord, I have cried"; if not, then Glory, Both now: Triodion. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 8.	After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart", at the Trisagion, - 3 prostrations; and after "Our Father" - troparia "Virgin Theotokos" and the two others with a prostration at the end of each, then "Beneath Thy tender mercy" without a prostration. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 10.	Trisagion with prostrations and, after "Our Father", - "Lord, have mercy" (12). (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 12.	Glory, Both now: Psalm "I will bless the Lord". (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 14.	Dismissal "O Christ our true God" with the commemoration of the service of the day and the saint. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	At the usual time of Vespers we sing Great Compline with prostrations, as we mentioned for Tuesday and Thursday evening of Cheesefare week, here on page 327. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday of the first week of Great Lent, after "O come, let us worship", we recite Psalm 69 "O God, come to my assistance", given in the order of Great Compline after the 3rd "O come, let us worship", after the prayer "O Master God the Father"; then one fourth part of each ode of the Great Canon (Matins on Thursday before the 5th Sunday of Lent)[^524]. Refrain to the troparia of the canon - "Have mercy on me, O God, have mercy on me"[^525], to the troparion of St. Mary of Egypt - "Venerable Mother Mary, pray to God for us", and to the troparion of St. Andrew - "Venerable Father Andrew, pray to God for us". According to the rubric, 3 small prostrations should be made at each troparion of the canon, however, among us this is not done, since there are no people in church at that time. (under ### 4.1.7 Beginning of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	After "Blessed is the Kingdom" we recite "O Heavenly King", Trisagion (with prostrations) and, after "Our Father", - "Lord, have mercy" (12), Glory, Both now: "O come, let us worship" three times (with prostrations). (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 2.	Of the eight prayers recited at the prefatory psalm, the Priest recites here only the last 5, for the first three he has to read afterwards, one at each of the three antiphons of the Kathisma. (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 4.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: Triodion on 6 and Menaion on 4, Glory, Both now: Theotokion of the Menaion. If there is a Doxastikon of the saint, then we select the Theotokion in His tone. (On Friday evening stichera: Idiomelon of the Triodion twice, Martyria of the Octoechos of the current tone - 4[^528] and of the Saint - 4, Glory: of the saint, if there is one, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the departing tone; if not, then Glory, Both now: the same Theotokion). (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 5.	Entrance with the Censer (on the Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner, on the 40 Martyrs and on Great Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday[^566] entrance - with the Gospel). (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 6.	At "O Gladsome Light" - Prokimenon of the Triodion, one reading of the Triodion and the second Prokimenon of the Triodion[^529]; then the Priest, having taken the triple-candle with the censer and exclaiming "Wisdom, Aright", begins "The Light of Christ", blessing with the triple-candle crosswise to the east, then, blessing to the west, says: "Illumines all"[^530]; all make three prostrations to the ground, and the Reader sings: "The Reading from Proverbs" and, when the Priest or Deacon exclaims: "Let us attend", reads the second reading of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 8.	Immediately after the end of the singing of "Let my prayer be set forth" is exclaimed "Let us all say" and according to the order - Liturgy of the Presanctified. (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 1.	If there is no Liturgy of the Presanctified, then we sing Vespers according to the rubric of Monday, Tuesday and Thursday evening, that is from the 10 stichera on "Lord, I have cried" we transfer the first 3 to the Aposticha, and the other 6 we sing on "Lord, I have cried"[^534]; and there will be on Wednesday stichera of the Triodion - 3 and of the Menaion - 3, and on Friday Martyria of the Octoechos - 3 and of the Menaion - 3. On Friday evening, after "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart", there will be no Lenten troparia with prostrations, but the Troparion of the Saint, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones, in the tone of the Troparion of the Saint[^651] and, after the litany "Have mercy on us, O God", - 3 great prostrations[^535] and the Great Dismissal, as usual. (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 2.	If on this day falls a saint with Polyeleos, namely the Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner and the 40 Martyrs, and also on Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday of Passion Week[^536], then immediately after "Let my prayer be set forth" there will be censing and on the two previously named feasts their Epistle and Gospel are read, the Gospel - beginning from "Wisdom, Aright". On the three days of Passion Week only the Gospel is read, beginning from "And that we may be accounted worthy", since there is no Epistle. (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H5: ##### 3.	By the decision of the Synod of Lviv, the pastor must celebrate the Liturgy of the Presanctified on every Wednesday and every Friday of Great Lent and on Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday of Passion Week. (under ### 4.1.8 Vespers on Wednesday and Friday with Presanctified)
  H4: #### AT COMPLINE (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	If it be Small Compline, then after "It is truly meet" we recite the Kontakion of the Great Martyr; if Great, then after the 1st Trisagion - Troparion of the Great Martyr, Glory, Both now: Theotokion (Sunday, in the tone of the Troparion of the Saint)[^538]; after the 2nd Trisagion - usual troparia "Have mercy on us, O Lord"; after "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Great Martyr, and the Troparion "O Lord of Hosts" we do not recite[^539]. (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	At Compline we sing the Canon for the Dead, which is in the Octoechos for the current tone, and the Canons of the Menaion to the saints of the nearest Saturday and Sunday we sing, if the Ecclesiarch prefers, one - at Compline on Friday with the Canon for the Dead, and the other - on Sunday at Compline[^540]. The Greek Triodion adds: "we do so on all Friday evenings of holy and Great Lent". Before the Canon for the Dead we should sing also the usual Theotokion Canon of the Octoechos, for the rubric does not exclude it[^541]. (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the canon everything - according to the general rubric for a saint without Polyeleos on Saturday, except the 2nd Sessional Hymn, which will not be of the Octoechos, but of the Triodion of St. Theodore. (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons 3 [making] 14: Temple with heirmos on 6 and two of Theodore on 8. Katavasia after each ode - "I shall open". (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises to the Saint on 4, Doxastikon - to him, and Theotokion - in the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	Small Doxology and, after "Let us complete", - Aposticha of the Saint with his two verses and the subsequent order of Matins according to the rubric for a saint without Polyeleos on Saturday. (under ### 4.1.9 First Saturday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	The Triodion service of this Sunday has neither an All-Night Vigil nor a Polyeleos[^543], but is combined with the Sunday service of the Octoechos. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	The service of a saint without Polyeleos we sing at Compline or on another day. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: Sunday of the Octoechos - 6 and of the Triodion - 4, Glory: Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone (Dogmatikon). (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Sunday Aposticha, Glory: Triodion, Both now: Triodion. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: "We venerate Thy most pure image", Tone 2; Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the same Tone 2. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: "We venerate Thy most pure image", Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the same Tone 2. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual, only "Glory" will be "Open to me the doors", Both now: "Lead me on the paths of salvation" and at "Have mercy on me, O God": "Thinking upon the multitude". (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 [making] 14: Octoechos Sunday on 4, Cross-Resurrection on 2, Theotokos on 2 and Triodion on 6[^544]. Katavasia of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	After the Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of the two usual ones. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	After the dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Sticheron. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion Sunday and "We venerate Thy most pure image", Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - of Orthodoxy, only Communion Hymn - "Praise the Lord" and "Rejoice in the Lord". (under ### 4.1.10 First Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: Idiomelon of the Triodion of the day twice, 4 Martyria of the Octoechos and 4 of the Menaion of the saint, Glory: of the saint, if there be, but if not, then Glory: Sticheron for the Dead of the tone, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the departing tone. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Instead of the Prokimenon of the day of the Horologion there will be Prokimenon of the Triodion and reading, second Prokimenon and second reading of the Triodion, then "Let my prayer be set forth" and the rest of the Presanctified. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	After the end of the Presanctified, in other services of this day (except the Panakhida for the dead) vestments of red color should rather be used, than black, for red ones are - predominantly for martyrs. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	If there be no Presanctified, then we sing Vespers according to the rubric given here, on page 347. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Instead of "God is the Lord" we sing Alleluia in Tone 2 with verses. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Troparion of the day "Apostles, prophets" twice; Glory: "Remember, O Lord", Both now: Theotokion "Holy Mother". (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the 1st Kathisma - Small Litany and Sessional Hymn of the Octoechos of the current tone. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	Canons 3 [making] 14: of the Temple of the Lord or Theotokos with heirmos on 6, of the Saint of the Menaion on 4 and of the Octoechos of the current tone the second[^547] on 4. If the Temple is of a Saint, then - Canon of the Saint with heirmos on 6, Temple on 4 and Octoechos on 4. From the 6th Ode, leaving the Canons of the Temple and Octoechos, we sing the Canon of the Saint of the Menaion on 6 and both canons of the tetraodion on 8. Refrain to the troparia of the tetraodion - "Holy Martyrs, pray to God for us". At the end of the eight troparia of each ode of the tetraodion we add two more troparia: one Martyric with the Martyric refrain, and the second - for the dead with the refrain for the dead. From the two Martyric refrains "Wondrous is God" and "To the saints that are in His earth" and from the two refrains for the dead "Their souls" and "Blessed are they Whom Thou Hast chosen", the first we refrain to the Martyric and for the dead of the 6th and 8th Odes, and the second - to the Martyric and for the dead of the 7th and 9th Odes. Katavasia after Odes 3, 6, 7, 8, and 9 - Heirmos of the last canon: after the 3rd - of the Octoechos, after the others - of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6.	Stichera of the Praises, Martyria of the Octoechos - 4, Glory: for the Dead, Both now: Theotokion of the "Glory". (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 7.	After the Small Doxology at the Aposticha - Stichera for the Dead of the Octoechos with their verses for the dead. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 8.	At the end: Troparion to all saints ("Apostles, prophets"), Glory: for the dead "Remember, O Lord", Both now: "Holy Mother". (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparia: "Apostles, prophets" and "Remember, O Lord"; Glory: Kontakion "With the saints give rest", Both now: "Thee as a wall and haven". (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else - of the day and of the dead. (under ### 4.1.12 Saturday of the Second Week of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Before the Canon we sing "Open to me the doors" and the rest, as on the first Sunday of Lent. (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons: Sunday of the current tone with heirmos on 6, Triodion on 4 and Saint of the Menaion on 4. (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Stichera of the Praises - 9: Sunday of the Octoechos on 8, with the usual verses of the Praises psalms, one of the Triodion with the verse "Arise, O Lord my God", then Glory: and again the same sticheron; Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end of Matins - Glory, Both now: Gospel Sticheron. (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Instead of the Sunday Kontakion we sing the Kontakion of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Sunday Prokimenon of the current tone and the rest - according to the general rubric. (under ### 4.1.13 Second Sunday of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	The service of a saint without Polyeleos we sing at Compline. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	The entire divine service, according to the Synod of Lviv, should be celebrated in violet vestments (according to the previous - in purple). (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT Great Vespers (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 6 Sunday of the Octoechos and 4 of the Triodion; Glory: Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Sunday Aposticha; Glory, Both now: Triodion. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the end: Sunday Troparion, Glory: of the Cross, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones, in the tone of the Cross. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	Dismissal: "Christ our true God, Who rose from the dead, through the prayers of His most pure Mother, by the power of the precious and life-giving Cross, which we venerate today, through the intercessions of the holy, glorious and all-laudable apostles, of our venerable and God-bearing fathers and of all the saints, have mercy on us and save us, for He is good and loves mankind". (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT Great Matins (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: to the Cross, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones in the tone of the Cross. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual, only after the 50th Psalm we sing "Open to me the doors" and the rest. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Canons 3 [making] 14: Octoechos Sunday with heirmos on 4, Theotokos on 2 and Triodion on 8. Katavasia of the Triodion. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	Stichera of the Praises - 9: 4 Sunday of the Octoechos and 5 of the Triodion, of which the three last - with the verses of the Cross; Glory: once more the last sticheron of the Triodion, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou". (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	During the Great Doxology the bringing out of the Precious Cross from the altar to the tetrapod takes place, according to the rubric given here on pp. 122-123 [→REF:p122-123]; then veneration, immediately after the singing of the troparion "Save, O Lord", for then the Priest begins to sing the troparion "Before Thy Cross" (3) and there will be veneration in such a way as given here on pp. 124-127 [→REF:p124-127]. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the dismissal - Glory, Both now: Gospel Sticheron. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT THE LITURGY (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Troparion Sunday and of the Cross; Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Cross. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Instead of the Trisagion we sing "Before Thy Cross". (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Everything else - only of the Cross. (under ### 4.1.14 Third Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	The stichera, not only the two first, but also all others, we sing in the tone of the Idiomela, for of the three types of Prosomoia given in the Triodion at the other stichera, two in our Heirmologia we do not find; and the one which is given at the stichera of St. Andrew of Crete, although it is given also in the Heirmologia, nevertheless, today we do not use. The stichera of St. Andrew of Crete, in the Greek text are given according to the order and in the sequence of the 24 letters of the Greek alphabet. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	If there be no Presanctified, then the Idiomelon and Martyricon stichera on "Lord, I have cried" are not taken, but are transferred with their two refrains to the Aposticha. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	It is not good to neglect prostrations at the stichera of St. Andrew. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	At the entire divine service of this day we use sacred vestments of Lenten colors, as usual. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	From the beginning to the end of the Sessional Hymns of the Octoechos, given at the end of the Triodion, at the 1st Kathisma, we sing everything according to the general Lenten rubric, then Psalm 50 and immediately, having omitted the litany "Save, O God", the Great Canon[^551]. At the time of the singing of the canon the holy doors are opened and candles are lit on the holy table. Before the holy doors in cathedral churches a rug is spread, on which first the Bishop, if he be present, and then the other sacred ministers two by two, according to the rank of their dignity, at each ode make one great prostration, after the singing of each troparion of the ode of the canon. In our seminary, the seminarians make prostrations before the holy doors, and in the sanctuary, before the steps of the altar, all eight superiors make prostrations according to the rank of their dignity, one at one ode, by turn, and at the 9th Ode - all eight together, in epitrachelia. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	Canons two: one - the Great [Canon] of St. Andrew, of nine odes, the second - triodion, contains the 4th, 8th and 9th Odes, which also in these odes takes the first place. The odes of the nine-ode canon do not have an equal number of troparia and each of them has at the end two troparia to St. Mary of Egypt and one to St. Andrew of Crete. The triodion we sing on 10 troparia with the first heirmos, for the 2nd heirmos is not sung. Refrains to the troparia of the Great Canon are these: "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee", then "Venerable Father Andrew, pray to God for us", then "Venerable Mother Mary, pray to God for us". To the troparia of the triodion the refrain will be - "Holy Apostles, pray to God for us"[^552], because according to content they belong to the service of Thursday. At each troparion of the Canon of St. Andrew the rubrics prescribe three prostrations, but we make only one, however, great, and in cathedral temples the clergy makes prostrations only by turn and adapted to the nine odes of the canon. The choirs sing the canon in the middle of the church, each at a separate analogion, making at the troparia of the canon a small prostration. As Katavasia at all odes we take the heirmos of the last canon[^553]. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	Everything else, from the Praises psalms to the end of Matins, is taken according to the general Lenten rubric, only, if we do not add the 1st Hour, then after the 16 final prostrations at the prayer of St. Ephrem we recite the Trisagion with "Our Father" and "Lord, have mercy" (12), and then the Priest: "Glory to Thee, O Christ God" and dismissal. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H4: #### AT THE HOURS (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	Kathisma usual. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	On "Lord, I have cried" - stichera on 10: Idiomelon of the day twice, one Martyric and of the feast on 7, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	At "O Gladsome Light" - Prokimenon of the Triodion and reading, second Prokimenon of the Triodion and reading and the rest of the Presanctified. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	On "God is the Lord": Troparion of the feast twice; Glory, Both now: once more. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	After the 1st Kathisma - Small Litany and the initial Kontakion "To the Directress" is sung by the Choir. The Priest, dressed in all sacred vestments of light color, opens the holy doors and censes everything as usual; then, having come to the altar, sings the first quarter of the ikos-Kontakia and, after its conclusion, the same choir sings once more the initial Kontakion "To the Directress". All rubrics at this singing prescribe standing; (and therefore this is called Akathist, that is non-sitting). The Choir sings the Kontakion in the middle of the church majestically and solemnly. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	After the 2nd Kathisma and after the 3rd and 6th Odes of the Canon, immediately after the Small Litany, the Priest sings the other three quarters of the ikos-Kontakia, and the Choir - the Kontakion "To the Directress" at the beginning and at the end of each quarter. At the beginning of each quarter the Priest censes everything just as at the beginning of the first quarter[^558]. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	Canons 2 [making] 12: Temple of the Saint with heirmos on 6 and of the feast on 6. Katavasia "I shall open my mouth" up to the 6th Ode. From the 6th Ode we do not take the Canon of the Temple, but we sing 3 canons on 14, that is of the feast on 6 and both canons of the triodion on 8 with the addition of two troparia, that is one Martyric with the refrain "Wondrous is God", and the second - for the dead, with the refrain "Their souls", and the Katavasia of the Triodion. Refrain to the troparia of the Canon of the Theotokos - the general "Most Holy Theotokos, save us", however, among us they like to refrain the acrostic of the canon "Container of Joy, to Thee alone it is fitting to rejoice". In Greek: "Vessel of joy, to Thee alone it is fitting to rejoice". Both the troparia and this refrain we sing to the melody given in the new Heirmologion on the Akathist Saturday. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	Stichera of the Praises - 4: all - of the feast, Glory, Both now: of the feast. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6.	After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the feast. (under ### 4.1.17 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Friday of the Fifth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 1.	On the Saturday before Flowery Sunday, called Lazarus [Saturday], let the Liturgy be sung in church. (under ### 4.1.19 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday of the Sixth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 2.	On Flowery Sunday let everything be celebrated as usual, according to the rubric of a feast of the Lord of the first rank with the addition of the blessing of branches at Matins. (under ### 4.1.19 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday of the Sixth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 3.	On the three following days of Great Week it is fitting to sing the Liturgy of the Presanctified. (under ### 4.1.19 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday of the Sixth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 4.	On Thursday of Passion Week it is fitting to sing the Liturgy together with Vespers. (under ### 4.1.19 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday of the Sixth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 5.	On the same day, after sunset, it is fitting to sing the Office of the "Passions" with the 12 Gospels. (under ### 4.1.19 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday of the Sixth Week of Great Lent)
  H5: ##### 6.	On Friday, which we mark as a solemn feast, let the Hours be celebrated in the morning in church, called Royal, and about noon - Vespers with the bringing out of the Shroud[^559], which, when it is brought out, is placed before the iconostasis in the so-called tomb, and there let it remain for constant veneration by the people until Resurrection Matins. (under ### 4.1.19 Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday of the Sixth Week of Great Lent)
