  H5: ##### 196. It will be good if all together recite the introductory prayers before the iconostasis, but let only the principal priest kiss the icons. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 197. All concelebrants, while putting on the prescribed priestly vestments, recite the appropriate prayers. Then each, holding in his hands a small *Liturgicon*, stands at the side of the holy table or also behind it, however not before it, where only the principal celebrant with the deacons occupies the place, and so all arrange themselves, each according to the rank of his dignity or his ordination. The first among the concelebrants or those standing by stands on the right side of the principal celebrant, the second - on the left, the third - again on the right and so on. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 198. The Proskomedia is celebrated by one of the concelebrants and only he recites the Prayer of the Offering. However, all concelebrants may, according to their own intentions, place their particles (see no. 113). (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 199. These exclamations belong to the principal celebrant: (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 200. For the Little Entrance all go out, having lowered their hands under the lowered phelonion, through the northern doors, in front - the younger ones, and stand before the holy doors in two rows, one near another, the principal celebrant stands with everyone in the middle. The principal celebrant himself recites the Prayer of the Entrance. After the call of the (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 201. The concelebrants sit near the High Throne in the same order in which they stand at the throne, that is, those who stand to the right of the principal celebrant sit to his right, and those on the left side - sit to the left. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 202. During the reading of the Gospel all remain in their places, looking towards the ambo or the place from which the Gospel is read. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 203. At the Cherubic Hymn all lift their hands, if this can be done conveniently, but all recite the Cherubic Hymn and make the prescribed bows. During the censing of the holy table by the Deacon all step away from it slightly so that the Deacon can easily go around it. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 204. From the table of oblation each takes one of the things used in the Divine Sacrifice, namely: the spear, the spoon, holding them on their chests. All go out through the northern doors, in front - the Deacon, who exclaims: "All of you" and stands in the holy doors in the sanctuary, behind him other concelebrating priests, beginning from the youngest, exclaiming each: "All of you," one or even several, if there is a custom, and stand facing one another outside the sanctuary before the holy doors as at the Little Entrance. The principal Priest exclaims: "Our most holy [Pontiff]" and stands exactly before the holy doors, turns to the west when he exclaims "All of you," after which he enters the sanctuary, and behind him go the concelebrants and each stands at the holy table at his place. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 205. Having made the Great Entrance, if there is no deacon, one of the concelebrants sings the first part of the litany "Let us complete," and another - the second part, so that each has time to read the Prayer of the Offering. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 206. When the principal priest kisses the Holy Gifts, the concelebrants one by one approach the holy table, according to the order of their dignity. The principal priest, having stepped back slightly from the holy table, turns to the right side and looks to the north, and each concelebrant kisses first the Holy Gifts, and then the left and right shoulder of the principal priest and stands to his left, slightly behind. The second concelebrant kisses first the Holy Gifts, then - the principal celebrant, and at the end - the first concelebrant and stands on his right side. All others do likewise. The principal Priest says: "Christ is in our midst," and the concelebrant responds: "He is and shall be." Likewise do also the others, who kiss one another. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 207. The concelebrants offering the sacrifice point with their hand to the holy diskos, and then to the holy chalice, just as the principal celebrant, simultaneously, slowly and attentively, sing the words of consecration, recite together "Thine own of Thine own," but only the principal celebrant blesses the Gifts. The same applies also to the Epiclesis. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 208. The first part of the litany "Having commemorated all the saints" is sung by one of the concelebrants, if there is no deacon, and the second part - by another, so that all have time to read the prayer "To Thee we entrust all our life." (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 209. After the breaking [of the Lamb], the principal celebrant calls the Deacon and gives him a particle of the Holy Bread, as prescribed. Then all recite the prayer "I believe, O Lord" and the principal priest communicates of the Body of Christ. All concelebrants, in turn, according to their dignity, approach the holy table from the side of the oblation table, and the principal celebrant stands on the opposite side of it. After the communion of the Body of Christ, each goes to the north of the holy table and washes, if necessary, His fingers in the vessel standing on the table of oblation, and, according to the prescribed sequence, they go behind the 1st concelebrant, who stands at his place at the holy table. The Deacon consumes the particle of Bread after the priests. The principal celebrant, having returned to the center, communicates of the Most Precious Blood and moves away from the holy table; after this the concelebrants, according to the prescribed order, approach the holy table, this time from the southern side, and each after the communion of the Blood departs by the table of oblation and stands at his place at the holy table. At the end the Deacon receives, according to the prescription, the communion of the Most Precious Blood from the principal celebrant. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 210. Each, having just communicated, wipes his lips with the purificator at the edge of the chalice. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 211. The Prayer behind the Ambo is exclaimed, standing between the two choirs, by the principal celebrant. Only after the dismissal and after the holy doors are closed does each take off the sacred vestments. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 212. There is no non-actual celebrant, or assistant, at the ordinary or private service. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 213. Everything that was said about concelebration in the solemn form is valid also for concelebration in the ordinary or private form, with the following exceptions: (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 214. In seminaries and houses of religious education it will be good if the Deacon participates in the celebration of the Divine Liturgy also when serving with the simple rite and if ordinary singing is used. Here, however, it is recommended to observe the rules which are binding for simple concelebration without a Deacon. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 215. If there is no time for the recitation of the preparatory prayers before the iconostasis, which all concelebrants recite together or each separately, it is never permitted to omit the prayer "O Lord, stretch forth Thy hand." Having entered the sanctuary, they recite the psalm "I will enter into Thy house" and, having kissed the holy table, the Gospel and, if there exists a custom, - the altar cross, they put on the priestly vestments (see no. 178). (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 216. The members of the litanies are distributed among the concelebrants and are sung so that the one who does not sing the litanies has time to recite the corresponding prayer without haste. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 217. It is not required that all concelebrants go out for the Little Entrance, but if they go out, then the one who is the 1st among them carries the Gospel, he himself recites the Prayer of the Entrance and imparts the blessing. But it is always required that those standing before the holy table or on its right side, before they go out, go around the holy table and go out behind the holy doors. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 218. There will be no blessing of the High Throne and all must remain near the throne during the reading of the Apostle. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 219. For the Great Entrance not all have to go out. If only the 1st makes the Entrance, he himself places the aer on his left shoulder and himself exclaims: "Our most holy [Pontiff]." If all go out, then each does not have to take some object used for the Divine Sacrifice, only the 2nd concelebrant takes the holy diskos, which he holds on his head, and the 1st - the holy chalice; the 2nd exclaims: "All of you, Orthodox Christians," and the 1st: "Our most holy [Pontiff]." Others exclaim nothing (see no. 200). (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 220. Everything else that the Deacon is supposed to say or do, according to what was said about the celebration of the Liturgy without a Deacon (comp. nos. 164-174), the concelebrant says and does. The one who serves the Proskomedia also cleanses the holy vessels after communion. (under ### 6.1.1 V. Rubrics of the Divine Liturgy of St. John Chrysostom & St. Basil)
  H5: ##### 221. On the Holy and Great Forty Days [Lent] at the Proskomedia of the Divine Liturgy on Sunday or on the last day before the Liturgy of Presanctified Gifts, besides the Lamb that is to be consecrated on that day, other Lambs are also prepared for those Liturgies of Presanctified Gifts that are to be celebrated on the following days. Having cut, sacrificed and pierced the first Lamb, the Priest performs the same rites also over the other Lambs, reciting at each of them: "In remembrance," "As a sheep," "Sacrificed," "One of the soldiers" and places them one near another on the diskos. Then he pours wine and water into the holy chalice, having recited what is prescribed and, according to custom, finishes the Proskomedia. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 222. When the Lamb is to be blessed at the invocation of the Holy Spirit, He says: "And make this Bread" in the singular, and not in the plural, for Christ is - only one. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 223. At the elevation he elevates simultaneously all consecrated Lambs; breaks only the first Lamb, places a part into the holy chalice and pours warm water when prescribed. After this he takes the other holy Lambs and places them in the pyx in the tabernacle. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. Then, according to custom, the Priest recites the prayers, communicates and finishes the sacrifice according to the prescription. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 1. In Concelebration of One Deacon (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. For the reading of the Hours, the Priest in the epitrachelion stands before the holy doors and begins: "Blessed is our God." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. At the end of the Typika the Priest and deacon vest in the sacred vestments as usual, but putting on each [vestment] they say nothing more, but only "Let us pray to the Lord." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. When the Reader finishes the Typika, having recited the Trisagion, "Our Father," "Lord, have mercy" (12) and the prayer "Most Holy Trinity," the holy doors are opened. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. The Priest accompanied by the Deacon makes three small bows before the holy table and recites only: "O God, be merciful to me a sinner." They kiss, as was already noted, the Holy Gospel, the holy table and the altar cross. Then the Deacon, having received permission from the Priest, goes out through the northern doors and, having stood at the usual place, makes three small bows, saying nothing and says: "Bless, Master." The Priest, standing before the holy table and making with the Holy Gospel the sign of the cross over it, exclaims: "Blessed is the kingdom." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. The Deacon returns into the sanctuary through the southern doors, and the Priest, having stood before the holy doors, reads the Vespers (Lamp-lighting) Prayers from the fourth to the eighth, for the first three he will read later, at the Antiphons. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. At the end of the opening psalm the Priest returns through the southern doors into the sanctuary, and the Deacon, having gone out through the northern and stood at the usual place, sings the Great Litany. After the exclamation, having made a small bow, he returns through the southern doors into the sanctuary. The Reader begins the 18th Kathisma of the Psalter. At "Alleluia" - they make small bows. When at each of the following Antiphons "Glory" is said, the Deacon goes out through the northern doors from the sanctuary and at the designated place sings the Small Litany, and the Priest recites quietly the first, second and third Vespers Prayers. After the completion of the Small Litany the Deacon returns through the southern doors into the sanctuary, and the Priest says the exclamation, as prescribed. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. At the beginning of the reading of the psalms [stichologia], if the Presanctified Lamb is kept in the tabernacle (artophorion) of the holy table, the transfer to the table of oblation takes place thus[^785]: the Deacon brings to the holy table the holy diskos, the star-cover and the small veil. The Priest kisses the Holy Gospel and places it on the right side of the holy table. After this he places the diskos on the unfolded antimension [iliton], on its left side, and, having opened the tabernacle, makes a small bow. He takes the chalice with the lid, in which the Lamb is kept, places it on the middle of the antimension [iliton] and, having taken from it the Presanctified Bread, places it with great piety on the holy diskos, as also the particles needed for the communion of the faithful. Having placed the pyx back into the tabernacle, he closes it and, having placed the holy diskos on the middle of the antimension [iliton], censes the star-cover and the small veil and covers the diskos with it. Having taken the censer, he censes around the holy table, preceded by the Deacon with a lighted candle. Having returned before the holy table and given the censer to the Deacon, he makes a low (prostrate) bow, making the sign of the cross on himself, after which he takes with both hands the covered holy diskos and, holding it on his head crosses by the right side of the holy table to the table of oblation (altar of preparation), preceded by the Deacon with a lighted candle and censer. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. At the table of oblation the Priest places the diskos on the unfolded antimension [iliton], after which he pours wine and water into the chalice, as is the custom, saying nothing at this. After censing the veil of the chalice over the censer he covers the chalice with it. Then with the large veil, which he likewise censes, he covers the diskos and chalice, saying nothing at this, but only: "Through the prayers of our holy fathers, Lord Jesus Christ our God, have mercy on us." Having made a prostrate bow, the Priest and deacon return to the holy table. There the Priest places the Book of the Holy Gospels on the folded antimension [iliton]. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. If the Presanctified Lamb is kept in the tabernacle which is located on the table of oblation, then at the beginning of the reading of the psalms [stichologia] the Priest and deacon go to the table of oblation. Both make a small bow before it, the Priest opens the tabernacle and, having made again a small bow, censes with three swings the Presanctified Gifts. Then he takes the Presanctified Bread from the pyx and places it with great piety on the diskos, and pours into the holy chalice, according to custom, wine and water, saying nothing. He takes the censer, censes the star-cover and veils, covers the diskos and chalice, each - with its own veil and both - with the large veil (aer), saying nothing, but only: "Through the prayers of the holy fathers." Having made a prostrate bow, he returns to the holy table. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. If the Priest deems it appropriate, he finishes the rite of placing the Lamb on the diskos during the reading of the first Antiphon, the censing - during the second, and the transfer of the Presanctified Gifts - during the reading of the third Antiphon. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. If a larger number of faithful is expected for communion, so that the Presanctified Bread alone will not suffice, the Priest places the appropriate number of already consecrated particles on the diskos, and on top of them - the Lamb Itself. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. After the completion of the reading of the psalms [stichologia] the Choir begins the psalm "Lord, I have cried," and the Deacon censes according to the prescription (see no. 33), at this he censes the Presanctified Gifts - three times, each time - with three swings. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. During the singing of "Glory, Both now" the holy doors are opened and the Entrance takes place in the usual manner. The Deacon censes, if there be an Entrance with the Gospel, and makes the Entrance with the Gospel. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. When the Deacon says in a low voice: "Let us pray to the Lord," the Priest recites quietly the Prayer of the Entrance with its exclamation and, after the completion of the stichera, the Deacon exclaims: "Wisdom, arise." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. Immediately after the second Prokimenon the Deacon says to the (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. After the completion of the second Paremia the Priest says to the (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. It should be noted that during the singing by the Reader or priest of "Let my prayer be set forth" or the verse, the Priest censes the holy table with three swings at the beginning and at the end of each verse which he sings. At this time the Choir and the faithful pray on their knees. When the Choir sings the Priest, having given the censer to the server, and all who are in the sanctuary, pray likewise on their knees. After the completion all bow three times. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. If on these days of Lent falls the memory of a certain saint or a temple feast, having opened the holy doors, the Deacon exclaims: "Let us be attentive," and the Reader reads the Prokimenon of the Apostle, after which the Apostle and Gospel are read, as prescribed. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. The holy doors are closed and the Deacon, having received permission from the Priest, goes out through the northern doors and, having stood at the designated place, sings the litany "Let us all say." Everything else takes place according to the prescription until the very exclamation of the prayer for the catechumens, after which the Priest, having laid aside the Gospel, unfolds the antimension [iliton] on the holy table. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. From Wednesday of the fourth week of Great Lent, which is the midpoint of the Forty Days, after the exclamation "That with us they also," the Deacon dismisses the catechumens, and having invited to prayer those preparing for holy baptism: "All catechumens, depart. Catechumens, depart. All who are for illumination, approach" and so it is sung at all services of the Presanctified, even if a feast day fell, with the exception of Saturday and Sunday. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. After the exclamation "According to the gift of Thy Christ" (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. Then they go directly to the table of oblation. There the Priest, having taken the censer, censes with three swings the Presanctified Gifts, saying nothing at this, takes the large veil (aer) and places it on the left shoulder of the Deacon, then takes the covered holy diskos with his right hand and holds it on his head, and the chalice with wine - with his left hand at his chest. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. If, however, the diskos should not have a foot, the Priest places it on the covered chalice, which he holds with his left hand, and with his right hand holds the veil over the holy diskos and carries the chalice like a diskos to his forehead. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. They go out of the sanctuary through the northern doors, in front walks the Deacon, who frequently censes the Holy Gifts. During the procession no one says anything. When they enter through the holy doors into the sanctuary, the Priest places the holy diskos on the throne on the left, and the holy chalice - on the right side, removes the small veils from the diskos and chalice and places them on the holy table, and with the aer, which he takes from the shoulder of the Deacon and censes over the censer, covers the Holy Gifts, saying nothing, but only censing the Holy Gifts thrice. After the completion of the hymn "Now the Powers of Heaven" all make three great prostrations, after which the holy doors are closed and the curtain is drawn shut. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. The Deacon, having bowed to the Priest, goes out through the northern doors and, having stood at the usual place, exclaims: "Let us complete," "Help us, save us, have mercy." The Priest exclaims: "And vouchsafe us, O Master," and the people: "Our Father." The Deacon girds himself with the orarion crosswise, as at the Liturgy of Chrysostom. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. According to the prescription follows the bowing of heads, and after the prayer "Look down, O Lord Jesus Christ" the Priest and deacon make three small bows, after which the Priest does not undercover the Holy Gifts, but, placing his right hand under the aer, touches the Life-giving Bread piously and with fear, and at the words of the Deacon "Let us be attentive" exclaims: "The Presanctified Holy Things to the holy." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. The Priest and deacon communicate according to custom of the Holy Bread. Then the Priest wipes his fingers with a sponge, if there is a custom to use it, and, having kissed it, places it on its place. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. Everything else takes place just as at the Liturgy of Chrysostom. However, it should be noted that: 1) at the dismissal of this Liturgy the author is not mentioned; 2) in the three first days of Passion Week one must say the dismissal of Palm Sunday at Vespers: "Christ, our true God, Who came to His voluntary Passion." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 2. In Concelebration of Two Deacons (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. After the completion of the Hours and after the dismissal at the end of the Typika the holy doors are closed, the Priest and deacons make before the holy table three small bows each, saying: "O God, be merciful to me a sinner" and kiss the Holy Gospel, the edge of the holy table and the altar cross, as usual. After this the 1st deacon, having paid respect to the Priest, goes out through the northern doors and, having stood at his place before the holy doors, makes three small bows, saying nothing, after which he exclaims: "Bless, Master." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. The first deacon assists the Priest during the placing of the Presanctified Lamb on the diskos and its transfer to the table of oblation, and the 2nd deacon goes out through the northern doors and sings the first Small Litany. The 1st deacon sings - the second and finally the 2nd deacon - the third. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. The censing on the psalm "Lord, I have cried" and the entrance take place just as at Vespers in concelebration with two deacons (see no. 39). Having returned into the sanctuary, the 1st Deacon exclaims from the holy doors: "Let us be attentive." The Priest, turned to the people: "Peace be to all." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. After the second reading of the Old Testament both deacons go before the Priest when he censes the holy table and sings: "Let my prayer be set forth" with the verses of the 140th Psalm. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. If the memory of some saint or a temple feast falls, the 2nd Deacon exclaims: "Let us be attentive." After the Prokimenon of the Apostle the (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. After the reading of the Gospel the deacons, having gone out through their doors, stand at the designated places. 1st deacon begins: "Let us all say." Then (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. During the singing of the hymn "Now the Powers of Heaven" the holy doors are opened and the deacons, having taken censers, cense with three swings (thrice) only the front of the holy table, then - the table of oblation and the Priest. After this they stand to the right and left of the Priest, holding the censers on one of the fingers of the left hand, and recite with the Priest three times the hymn "Now the Powers of Heaven." (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. After the holy diskos and the holy chalice are placed on the holy table, the deacons go out through their doors and stand at the usual places. 1st Deacon exclaims: "Let us complete." At "Our Father" the deacons gird themselves with the oraria crosswise. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 3. Without a Deacon (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 226. If this Liturgy is celebrated without a Deacon, the Priest exclaims everything that belongs to the Deacon, as has already been said in the rubric of Vespers and the Divine Liturgy of John Chrysostom. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 227. When the Priest censes the holy table and transfers the Holy Gifts to the table of oblation, the server, holding a lighted candle, walks before him. Small litanies and exclamations the Priest says, if possible, before the holy table. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 228. During the singing of "Let my prayer be set forth" the server walks with a lighted candle before the Priest. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 224. At the Great Entrance candle-bearers, if there be such, turned to the northern doors and arranged in order, accompany the Presanctified Lamb. After they arrive before the holy doors they stand together on both sides, and then return to their places. (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 4. In Concelebration of Priests (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
  H5: ##### 225. The rules of concelebration of the Liturgy of Presanctified Gifts - are the same as of Vespers and the Liturgy of Chrysostom. Only one must keep in mind that: (under ### 6.1.2 VI. Rubrics of the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts)
